### [1] [Systematic High Overtone Expansion of Kerr Quasinormal Modes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38019)
*Yasuyuki Hatsuda*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种系统性方法，用于计算Kerr黑洞在常规角分支上的高阶泛音准正规模展开，并通过椭圆周期与固定亏格一谱曲线实现任意阶WKB修正的解析确定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确计算Kerr黑洞准正规模（QNMs）在高泛音极限下的渐近行为，对理解黑洞扰动稳定性、引力波模板建模及全息对偶中的谱问题至关重要；现有方法缺乏系统性高阶展开框架。

Method: 1) 利用多项式递推求解大频率极限下角向本征值展开；2) 将其代入径向Teukolsky方程的等价形式；3) 证明所有WKB修正定义于固定亏格一谱曲线上；4) 将二阶及以上量子周期表为仅两个椭圆周期的线性组合，从而逐阶确定展开系数。

Result: 实现了Kerr QNMs在正常角分支上的任意阶高泛音展开；数值验证表明截断级数精确复现直接求解角向与径向方程所得准正频率（标量/电磁/引力场）；标量场结果与极值Kerr黑洞的精确WKB分析一致。

Conclusion: 高泛音极限下Kerr准正规模具有深层可积结构——其WKB展开由亏格一Riemann面的椭圆周期完全控制，为黑洞微扰理论提供了新的几何解析视角，并统一处理不同自旋场。

Abstract: We develop a systematic method to compute the high overtone expansion of Kerr quasinormal modes on normal angular branches to arbitrary order. We first obtain the expansion of the angular eigenvalue at large frequencies by a simple polynomial recursion. Using this result in an alternative form of the radial Teukolsky equation, we find that all the WKB corrections are defined on a fixed genus-one spectral curve, and that the quantum periods at the second and higher orders are expressed in terms of only two elliptic periods. This allows us to determine the expansion coefficients order by order. We confirm that the truncated expansions reproduce quasinormal frequencies obtained by solving the angular and radial equations directly, for scalar, electromagnetic and gravitational fields. We also compare the scalar results with the exact WKB analysis of extremal Kerr black holes.

</details>


### [2] [Covariant phase space analysis of static spherical vacua in power-law $f(R)$ gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39484)
*Mariam Campbell,Eduardo Bittencourt,Peter K. S. Dunsby*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文使用(1+1+2)协变形式将幂律型f(R)引力的静态球对称真空场方程重构为自治动力系统，通过Poincaré紧化与加权坐标分析其临界点结构与渐近行为，识别出Clifton–Barrow解、Schwarzschild端点及标量平坦喉解等关键解，并给出其在径向静态流下的稳定性分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解幂律f(R)引力中静态球对称真空解的全局结构与动力学分类，弥补传统坐标依赖方法在奇点与无穷远行为分析上的不足。

Method: (1+1+2)协变分解、自治动力系统构建、Poincaré紧化、加权与次级坐标系、临界点稳定性分析（线性化与中心流形）、度规重构。

Result: 发现Clifton–Barrow解在参数正区间内为径向向外吸引子；Schwarzschild端点在多项式延拓系统中为鞍点；存在一类标量平坦、具正则喉部与单渐近平直端的奇异解；大归一化曲率下正曲率区出现法向吸引曲面，与负曲率Clifton–Barrow平衡点分离。

Conclusion: 该动力系统框架成功组织了静态球对称真空解的约化相空间结构与边界渐近行为，但未完成全部解的完备分类；所有稳定性结论仅针对静态径向流。

Abstract: We formulate the static, spherically symmetric vacuum equations of power-law metric $f(R)$ gravity as a covariant autonomous system using the $(1+1+2)$ formalism. The finite critical sets include the scale-invariant Clifton--Barrow solution, a Minkowski endpoint shared by the Schwarzschild family, and degenerate boundaries of the normalized chart. The Clifton--Barrow equilibrium is an outward-radial attractor over a positive interval of the power-law parameter, whereas the Schwarzschild endpoint is a saddle of the polynomially extended system. Poincaré compactification, supplemented by secondary and weighted charts, resolves several asymptotic regimes. In particular, normally attracting sheets at large normalized curvature occur in the positive-curvature sector and are separated from the negative-curvature Clifton--Barrow equilibrium. Metric reconstruction also identifies a selected scalar-flat family with a regular throat, one asymptotically flat end, and a curvature-singular end. These results organize the regular reduced chart and the boundary regimes resolved here; they do not constitute a complete classification of static spherical vacua. All stability statements concern the static radial flow.

</details>


### [3] [Quasinormal-mode encounters: Pseudospectra near Kerr--Newman exceptional points](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40323)
*Kyriakos Destounis*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了Kerr-Newman黑洞中例外点（EPs）对准正规模谱不稳定性的影响，通过伪谱和条件数分析标量场与引力-电磁耦合扰动，发现EP附近模式敏感性显著增强，并呈现平方根频率分裂与反平方根条件数增长规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解黑洞准正规模在例外点附近的谱不稳定性增强机制，及其对引力波环down信号建模的潜在影响。

Method: 采用伪谱分析与条件数计算，结合基于应力-能量张量导出的能量范数（标量场）和具有正辐射能量的四场耦合系统（引力-电磁扰动），研究Kerr-Newman时空中的(ℓ,m)=(1,1)球面谐波分支及近EP参数路径。

Result: 在近EP背景中，成对近简并模式的条件数显著高于另两个更阻尼模式；GE扰动沿匹配参数路径满足ω−ω_EP ∝ √(ε) 的频率分裂律，且cond(ω) ∝ ε^(−1/2)；伪谱轮廓揭示了从单模敏感性到双模集体响应的过渡。

Conclusion: 例外点显著放大黑洞准正规模的谱不稳定性，该效应在离散化问题与所选能量范数下可定量刻画，为研究EP对黑洞环down动力学的贡献提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Exceptional points (EPs), at which two quasinormal frequencies and their eigenvectors coalesce, can strongly enhance the spectral instability of black hole quasinormal modes. We investigate this behavior in Kerr--Newman spacetimes through pseudospectra and condition numbers for neutral massless scalar fields and coupled gravitoelectromagnetic (GE) perturbations. For the scalar field, we follow the $(\ell,m)=(1,1)$ spheroidal branch and derive the energy norm from the stress-energy tensor. For GE perturbations, we use a coupled four-field system equipped with a positive radiative energy. At the selected near-EP backgrounds, the condition numbers of both modes in the close pair exceed those of the two more-damped modes shown for comparison at the same background. Along a matched GE parameter path, the frequency splitting follows a square-root law and the condition numbers grow as the inverse square root of the distance to the EP. The pseudospectral contours resolve the transition from individual-mode sensitivity to the collective response of the pair. Within the discretized problems and energy norms considered here, these results quantify the amplification of spectral instability near EPs and provide a basis for studying their contribution to black-hole ringdown.

</details>


### [4] [Validity Domains for Semiclassical Time in Quantum Cosmology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37696)
*Gianpaolo Rosati,Sergio L. Cacciatori*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文探讨了半经典引力WKB流作为下属量子自由度的时间参考的有效性域，指出Born-Oppenheimer绝热性与引力WKB有效性定义了不等价的有效域，即使在最简minisuperspace模型中亦然；时间可靠性取决于多个有效性域（WKB、绝热、尖锐性、退相干）的交集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澄清半经典引力中‘WKB时间’是否可普适地作为可靠时间参数，避免将WKB近似有效性与绝热参考系构建混为一谈。

Method: 在引力组态空间上直接基于Hamilton-Jacobi矢量场表述Born-Oppenheimer绝热条件，区分WKB局部有效性、绝热控制与参考系量子涨落抑制；结合Airy函数统一处理转折点，并比较不同控制参数的渐近行为。

Result: 在FRW minisuperspace耦合标量场模型中，WKB控制参数在转折点发散，而组态空间绝热参数趋于零；Airy修正后前者仍为O(1)，后者为O(M^{-1/3})，证实二者有效性域不重合。

Conclusion: 仅存在振荡型半经典分支不足以保证WKB时间的可靠性；真正的时间参考需同时满足WKB有效性、绝热性、参考系尖锐性及（多分支情形下的）退相干性——即D_t = D_WKB ∩ D_ad ∩ D_sharp (∩ D_dec)。

Abstract: We investigate the domain in which a semiclassical gravitational WKB flow can serve as a quantitatively controlled temporal reference for subordinate quantum degrees of freedom. Rather than introducing WKB time at the outset, we formulate the Born--Oppenheimer adiabatic condition directly on gravitational configuration space using the Hamilton--Jacobi vector field. This separates adiabatic control from the local WKB validity of the gravitational branch and from the sharpness of the candidate reference against quantum fluctuations. For a single selected semiclassical branch, these requirements define the validity domain $D_t = D_{\mathrm{WKB}} \cap D_{\mathrm{ad}} \cap D_{\mathrm{sharp}}$, with an additional decoherence condition when coherent branches this http URL a closed Friedmann--Robertson--Walker minisuperspace coupled to a homogeneous massive scalar mode, the distinction is explicit: approaching the gravitational turning point from the oscillatory region, the ordinary WKB control parameter diverges while the configuration-space adiabatic parameter tends to zero. A uniform Airy treatment of the turning point removes the formal WKB divergence while preserving the inequivalence: the corresponding local control parameter remains of order unity, whereas the configuration-space adiabatic parameter is parametrically suppressed as $O(M^{-1/3})$.Thus Born--Oppenheimer adiabaticity and gravitational WKB validity define inequivalent domains even within the simplest minisuperspace model. We conclude that the existence of an oscillatory semiclassical branch is not sufficient for a reliable WKB temporal description: semiclassical temporal reliability is instead determined by the overlap of the validity domains supporting the reference flow.

</details>


### [5] [Classical traversability of a carbon BTZ wormhole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37704)
*Valerie Tynianska,Alfredo Iorio*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了在二维弯曲碳膜上实现的类空间虫洞几何的经典可穿越性，通过反射对称加倍技术构造光滑可穿越喉部，并分析无质量狄拉克准粒子沿类光测地线的运动行为，发现其经典可穿越性由角动量的二元阈值决定，临界轨迹呈现对数发散的角演化与多次绕喉行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建并研究凝聚态系统中可实现的类比引力时空结构，特别是无奇点、无事件视界的可穿越虫洞几何，以探索其经典动力学与未来介观量子输运之间的联系。

Method: 采用反射对称加倍技术构造BTZ黑洞外部空间区域的类比虫洞背景度规；将无质量狄拉克准粒子视为自旋光子，其运动由超静态时空中的类光测地线完全决定；严格求解测地线方程，分析角动量守恒与喉部最小半径构成的离心势垒效应。

Result: 发现存在一个尖锐的角动量临界值L_c，当|L| < L_c时轨迹被反射，|L| > L_c时可穿越喉部；临界轨迹（|L| → L_c）导致角坐标φ发散为log型，引发准粒子在喉部附近无限次空间绕转；该经典射线追踪构型为后续介观尺度量子输运研究提供了几何基础。

Conclusion: 该类比虫洞在经典层面上具有清晰的可穿越性判据，其几何—动力学对应关系揭示了曲率主导的自旋光子传播可作为探测人工时空拓扑结构的有效探针。

Abstract: We investigate the classical traversability of an analogue spatial wormhole geometry realized on a two-dimensional curved carbon membrane. The background is obtained by implementing a reflection-symmetric doubling technique on the exterior spatial sector of a non-rotating Bañados-Teitelboim-Zanelli black hole, effectively replacing the event horizon with a smooth traversable throat. Within this framework, massless Dirac quasiparticles propagate as spinorial light rays whose kinematics are governed exclusively by the metric curvature, forcing them to track the null geodesics of the ultrastatic spacetime. By evaluating the exact geodesic equations, we demonstrate that classical traversability is dictated by a sharp binary threshold in the conserved angular momentum, where the minimum throat radius acts as a centrifugal potential barrier that separates traversing paths from reflecting ones. We show that near-critical trajectories experience a logarithmic divergence in their angular evolution, causing the quasiparticles to execute multiple spatial windings around the throat before escaping. These classical ray-tracing configurations establish the necessary geometric backbones for future mesoscopic investigations.

</details>

Abstract: We determine thermodynamic and structural properties of the Coulomb plasma (jellium) in the thin atmosphere of a white dwarf using the path integral Monte Carlo method within a general relativity description of the star.

### [7] [Draining Without Emptying a Stringy Black Hole: Repetitive Electric Penrose Process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37713)
*Huai-Fan Li,Xiong-Ying Guo,Ke Wang,Hong Lei*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了GMGHS黑洞中在最优能量提取条件下的重复性电Penrose过程，发现其有效势曲线在迭代中保持不变；迭代终止由逃逸条件（与电荷无关）和负能粒子存在条件（需足够大电荷）共同决定；数值结果表明能量投资回报率可超100%，但能量利用效率上限为50%，大部分提取能量转化为黑洞不可约质量的增加；参数依赖性分析揭示了衰减半径、粒子电荷质比及入射粒子比能对效率的影响规律；最终黑洞仍保留非零质量和电荷，并存留可提取能量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究GMGHS黑洞中重复电Penrose过程的能量提取极限及其与黑洞电荷、粒子参数的动力学耦合机制，弥补RN黑洞之外的稀有精确解中该过程系统性研究的缺失。

Method: 基于GMGHS黑洞精确解，构建带电粒子测地线运动方程与能量-角动量守恒约束；推导迭代过程中有效势函数的不变性；严格分析粒子0逃逸条件与粒子1负能存在的充要几何-电动力学条件；结合数值积分与参数扫描，量化ROI（return on investment）与能量利用效率随初始黑洞电荷、粒子比能、电荷质比及衰减半径的变化规律。

Result: 1) GMGHS黑洞中重复电Penrose过程的有效势在迭代中保持不变；2) 迭代终止需同时满足逃逸条件（独立于电荷）与负能粒子存在条件（要求Q/M > Q_crit）；3) ROI可达>100%，但能量利用效率≤50%；4) 提取能量主要转化为不可约质量增量；5) ROI与效率受粒子电荷质比、衰减半径正相关，与入射粒子比能负相关；6) 初始Q增大使ROI单调增、效率呈单峰变化；7) 迭代后黑洞质量M_f > 0、电荷Q_f > 0，且仍具剩余可提取能量。

Conclusion: GMGHS黑洞支持高效但受限的重复电Penrose能量提取，其物理上限由不可约质量增长与电荷下限共同决定，凸显了标量-电磁耦合背景中黑洞热力学第二定律的微观实现机制；该过程无法使黑洞完全放电或蒸发，验证了广义无毛定理在能量提取语境下的鲁棒性。

Abstract: This paper studies the repetitive electric Penrose process in the Gibbons-Maeda-Garfinkle-Horowitz-Strominger (GMGHS) black hole under the optimal condition for maximum energy extraction. Unlike the Reissner-Nordström (RN) black hole, the effective potential curve of the incident particle in the GMGHS black hole remains unchanged during the iterations. The iteration termination conditions are determined by two aspects: the escape condition requires that particle 0 can overcome the effective potential peak and escape to infinity, and this condition is independent of the black hole charge; the existence condition for negative-energy particle 1 requires the black hole charge to be sufficiently large, indicating that the repetitive process cannot completely consume the charge of the GMGHS black hole to zero. Numerical simulations show that the return on investment of energy extraction can exceed $100\%$, but the energy utilization efficiency does not exceed $50\%$, and most of the extractable energy is converted into an increase in the irreducible mass of the black hole. In addition, a smaller decay radius and a larger absolute value of the charge-to-mass ratio of particle 1 can improve the return on investment and energy utilization efficiency of energy extraction; the larger the specific energy of the incident particle, the smaller the return on investment of energy extraction, while the energy utilization efficiency remains unchanged; for an initially non-extremal black hole, as the initial charge increases, the return on investment of energy extraction increases monotonically, while the energy utilization efficiency first increases and then decreases. After the iterations end, the black hole still retains extractable energy, and neither the charge nor the mass vanishes.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建量子引力的有效路径积分表述，特别是寻求能严格定义泛函积分（如Wiener测度）的引力模型，以规避高阶导数理论中常见的不定度与病态传播子问题。

Method: 采用时空对称性约化（空间平坦FLRW背景）、时间重参数化不变变量构造、非线性场重定义将约化作用量转化为标准Gaussian形式，并结合时间离散化分析变换Jacobi行列式的维度依赖性。

Result: 发现仅在7+1维时空下，所构造的离散非线性变换的Jacobi行列式不依赖于动力学变量，从而保证Wiener测度下的路径积分定义良好；由此获得约化模型中观测量期望值的Wiener积分表示。

Conclusion: 7+1维是使该二次引力模型约化后路径积分能严格由Wiener测度刻画的临界维度；该结果为高阶引力理论的随机微分方程描述与欧氏量子化提供了新范式。

Abstract: We consider a quadratic gravity model supplemented by the terms $\nabla_{\mu}R\nabla^{\mu}R$ and $(\nabla^{2}R)^2$. For specific relations among the coupling constants, the action takes a perfect-square form. In the spatially flat FLRW model, we perform a reduction to a dynamical variable invariant under time reparameterizations. We construct a nonlinear change of variable that transforms the reduced action into the Gaussian action associated with the Wiener measure. An analysis of the transformation Jacobian within a time discretization singles out the case of $7+1$-dimensional spacetime, in which the Jacobian of the discrete transformation under consideration is independent of the dynamical variable. On this basis, we propose a formal representation of expectation values of observables in the reduced model as integrals with respect to the Wiener measure.

</details>


### [9] [Primordial Gravitational Waves in Loop Quantum Cosmology: Regularization Ambiguities and Initial State Dependence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37790)

Abstract: The present study investigates the feasibility of gravitational baryogenesis in a Gauss-Bonnet (GB) modified Randall-Sundrum (RS) type II braneworld cosmology. The modified expansion rate of the Universe in this scenario evolves through three distinct expansion regimes, the GB regime at early times where the expansion rate $H \sim \rho^{1/3}$ falls more slowly than the standard expansion law $H\sim \rho^{1/2}$ of GR, then the RS type era with an enhanced expansion $H \sim \rho$, before finally reducing to the standard expansion law in the low energy limit. Gravitational baryogenesis depends on $\dot{R}$ and requires $\dot{R} <0$. This condition is satisfied for the GB and GR regimes. However, $\dot{R}>0$ for the RS regime and successful baryogenesis requires either that the decoupling temperature occurs during the GB regime or that the Universe makes a direct transition from the GB regime to the GR regime. The GB baryon asymmetry $\eta(T)$ is proportional to $\mu (1+w)(1-w)(1+1/\beta)$, where $\mu$ is the energy scale $\mu \equiv 1/l$ associated with the bulk curvature scale $l$, $\beta \equiv 4 \alpha \mu^{2}$, where $\alpha$ is the GB coupling, and $w$ is the equation of state parameter $p=w \rho$. Studies of inflation in GB-braneworld cosmology place constraints on the allowed ranges of the parameters $\mu$ and $\beta$. Guided by these, the asymmetry has been calculated for ranges of $(\mu, \beta, w)$ and it is found that the observed values can be obtained for a value of $\beta$ within the range $(10^{-2} - 10^{-3})$ for $\mu =10^{12}$ GeV, and within the range $(10^{-1}- 10^{-2})$ for $\mu =10^{13}$ GeV. Abstract: We present a systematic Hamiltonian analysis of the five-parameter class of theories known as Newer General Relativity. We then determine the constraint structure and analyze the resulting physical degrees of freedom in each type.

Abstract: We study the charged Breitenlohner--Freedman (BF) bound for all horizon harmonics of the type IIB $t^+$ Kaluza--Klein tower on extremal charged AdS$_5$ black holes with equal rotations. Below the curve, where $\Phi_H>1$, the BF-passing set is confined to a thin strip and controlled by charge and spin discreteness. At large radius and fixed rotation, a level-four mode with spin proportional to the radius violates the bound even where all U(2) singlets satisfy it. Abstract: Recently, it has been observed that in certain schemes of the bosonic string effective action, the non-derivative tachyon appears only in the tachyon potential, while all other couplings involve covariant derivatives of the tachyon and massless fields. Within these schemes, a minimal basis can be found whose coupling constants are constrained by T-duality upon circle reduction. These results determine the endpoint dimension, the defect $g$-function, and the dimensions and endpoint OPE coefficients of the lowest non-singlet Wilson-line primaries. Moreover, combining the diagrammatic correlator with endpoint crossing, somewhat surprisingly, we find agreement between the bulk and Wilson-line spectra in every non-singlet flavor channel through order $1/N$.   <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 建立了多融合范畴+作用函子的对称性公理化框架；严格分类宇宙对为Type I（可逆界面，同indecomposable summand内）、Type II（非可逆界面，同summand内）、Type III（无界面，跨不同summand）；给出Type III的两种构造：2-群型扩展与UV共源RG流；提出与广义QFT族的精确对应：equivariant ↔ Type I, categorical ↔ Type II, discrete-parameter families ↔ Type III。

Abstract: Two-dimensional quantum field theories with topological point operators decompose into universes. Just as fusion categories have played an important role in understanding non-invertible symmetries, multi-fusion categories play an analogous role for the symmetries of such decomposing theories. We describe a finite symmetry as a multi-fusion category together with its action functor, which keeps track of trivially acting symmetries and of the topological interfaces between universes. Relative to a chosen symmetry, we classify pairs of universes by the topological interfaces it contains, which reflect the structure of the multi-fusion category: universes in different indecomposable summands are joined by no interface (Type~III), while within a summand they are related by invertible (Type~I) or only non-invertible (Type~II) interfaces. For orbifolds with a trivially acting abelian normal subgroup, we derive the full inherited symmetry, which characterizes the operators between universes found case by case by Robbins, Sharpe and Vandermeulen and yields pairs of Types~I and~II but never of Type~III. We construct Type~III pairs instead from extensions of $2$-group type and from renormalization group flows out of a common ultraviolet theory. Finally, we propose a correspondence with the generalized families of quantum field theories of Brennan and Intriligator, in which equivariant families correspond to Type~I pairs and categorical ones to Type~II pairs whose gauging preserves the symmetry, family anomalies appear as universes carrying different symmetry-protected topological phases, and Type~III pairs can realize families over a discrete parameter. An appendix by Xingyang Yu works out the higher-charge Schwinger model as a Lagrangian example.

</details>

*Xianlong Liu*

The cancellation occurs because the residue of the loop integrand on the elliptic sector is a flat-space amplitude, rational in the loop momentum. The one-loop box, by contrast, is not polylogarithmic, even with a single external leg at each site. Its threshold discontinuity involves elliptic integrals, which we also give in closed form. Abstract: We study planar four-point scattering amplitudes on the Coulomb branch of $\mathcal N=4$ super Yang--Mills theory, focusing on multi-parameter families of massive kinematics. Abstract: The regularized master-field approximation (RMA) is an efficient computational method for large-$N$ reduced matrix models, including those with multiple matrices or complex integration weights. Abstract: We introduce virasoro_blocks, software that computes Virasoro conformal blocks at any central charge in a form suitable for the numerical bootstrap. At each finite order in the elliptic nome, Zamolodchikov's recursion gives the block as a positive prefactor times a rational function of the exchanged weight. However, its many Kac poles make it difficult to use in semidefinite programming approaches to the bootstrap. In this software we use optimal interpolation to reduce the number of poles while keeping high accuracy. The software works in arbitrary precision and handles singular central charges, including the minimal models and $c=1$. It also computes global blocks, $N=1$ superconformal blocks, torus characters, and torus one-point blocks. We illustrate it with two applications. Assuming only scalar Virasoro primaries, we compute bounds on the scalar gap that approach the window of Liouville solutions between $(c-1)/16$ and $(c-1)/12$. We also bound the average exchanged dimension in four-point functions of identical scalars at several central charges. These bounds have a rich structure, and many known 2d CFTs lie on or near their boundary.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立一个能同时、自洽地描述量子黑洞中耗散、量子涨落与退相干的实时有效理论框架，弥补传统虚时或纯幺正方法在开放量子系统动力学建模上的不足，并连接黑洞热力学、信息丢失与实验可观测量（如空间叠加退相干）。

Method: 采用Schwinger-Keldysh闭路路径积分形式构造世界线EFT；引入约化密度矩阵的influence functional，提取因果响应核R(t,t′)与对称噪声核N(t,t′)；施加Kubo-Martin-Schwinger (KMS) 条件与正定性约束；通过Hubbard-Stratonovich变换将噪声核转化为经典高斯随机源耦合至世界线与度规自由度；对Schwarzschild双星作1PN展开并进行吸收边界匹配以提取噪声谱与退相干泛函。

Result: 1) 得到满足KMS与正定性的响应/噪声核谱；2) 导出1PN阶完整相对加速度随机力，含直接与度规中介贡献，保持质心动量守恒且为O(ℏG²/c⁵) Planck压制；3) 同一噪声核给出支撑空间叠加态的四极矩退相干泛函，经吸收匹配后精确复现Danielson–Satishchandran–Wald的horizon-induced ∼T_H ℏ G / c³标度；4) 证明非平衡下响应与噪声为独立EFT匹配参数。

Conclusion: Schwinger-Keldysh世界线EFT为量子引力开放系统提供了一致的实时动力学框架；噪声核是连接黑洞热物理、随机引力辐射与量子叠加实验可观测效应的核心对象；该方法自然容纳非平衡与微观结构修正，为检验量子黑洞信息动力学提供了可计算路径。

Abstract: We formulate a Schwinger--Keldysh worldline effective field theory for quantum black holes that treats dissipation, fluctuations, and decoherence within one real-time influence functional. The reduced dynamics is encoded in causal response and symmetric noise kernels. Their spectra obey positivity constraints, while thermal Kubo--Martin--Schwinger symmetry relates them through the fluctuation--dissipation relation. A Hubbard--Stratonovich representation turns the noise kernel into a Gaussian source driving the resolved metric and worldline. For a leading post-Newtonian Schwarzschild binary, direct and metric-mediated stochastic forces combine into the complete relative-acceleration noise at this order, preserve center-of-mass momentum balance, and remain Planck suppressed. The same noise kernel gives the quadrupolar decoherence functional for a supported spatial superposition and reproduces the Danielson--Satishchandran--Wald horizon-induced scaling after absorptive matching. Away from thermal equilibrium, response and noise remain independent matching data. We discuss extensions to microscopic quantum structure in the worldline kernels, matching to spatially extended coarse-grained descriptions, and gravitational splitting.

</details>


### [99] [Quantum field theory with scale-dependent weighted measures: nonlinear momentum representations, vacuum fluctuations, and causal structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39285)
*S. González-Salud,R. Cartas-Fuentevilla*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了通过动量空间加权测度构造自由量子场的方法，引入一个独立的能量标度以在低动量下恢复标准理论；该构造由无量纲谱函数参数化，包含Euclidean Snyder代数对应的特殊空间权重；进一步讨论其与修正色散关系、非线性动量坐标及协变谱权重的联系，并系统推导了复Klein-Gordon场的等时对易关系、守恒加权观测量、真空涨落与任意空间维数下的两点函数；明确了紫外有限性条件，并通过普通Pauli-Jordan分布的空间卷积刻画其非局域因果结构；最后给出相互作用推广示例及核函数与振幅的数值轮廓。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决标准量子场论在紫外区域发散问题，同时保持低能极限下的物理一致性，并探索量子引力候选模型（如Snyder非对易几何）所暗示的动量空间修正结构。

Method: 基于动量空间加权测度构造自由量子场，引入独立能量尺度Λ；采用无量纲谱函数ρ(p)参数化权重；利用类时四速度U^μ定义协变谱权重；对复Klein-Gordon场进行正则量子化，计算等时对易代数、守恒流、Wightman函数与Feynman传播子；分析紫外行为与因果结构（通过Pauli-Jordan分布的空间卷积）。

Result: 获得任意空间维d下加权量子化的等时对易关系[φ(t,x),π(t,y)] = i δ_ρ(x−y)；导出守恒加权电荷Q_ρ；真空涨落⟨0|φ²|0⟩与两点函数G_ρ(x−y)显式表达；确定紫外有限性条件为ρ(p)在|p|→∞时衰减快于|p|^{−d}；证明因果结构由非局域核K_ρ(x) = ∫ d^dp/(2π)^d ρ(p) e^{ip·x}卷积普通Pauli-Jordan函数给出；给出ϕ⁴型相互作用的微扰延拓及K_ρ(x)与谱密度数值曲线。

Conclusion: 动量空间加权测度提供了一类紫外有限、协变可控且低能还原标准QFT的自由场构造框架；Snyder空间权重是其自然实现之一；该方法将非对易几何、修正色散与非局域因果统一于谱函数ρ(p)的数学结构中，为量子引力有效场论提供了新路径。

Abstract: We reconsider the construction of free quantum fields through momentum-space weighted measures, introducing an independent energy scale that restores the conventional theory at low momenta. The resulting family is characterized by a dimensionless spectral function and includes, as a distinguished case, the spatial weight associated with the Euclidean Snyder algebra. We discuss its relation to modified dispersion relations and nonlinear momentum coordinates, and introduce an observer-covariant spectral weight defined using a timelike four-velocity. For the complex Klein-Gordon field we obtain the equal-time algebra, conserved weighted observables, vacuum fluctuations, and two-point functions in arbitrary spatial dimension. The ultraviolet conditions for their finiteness are established, and the nonlocal causal structure is analyzed in terms of a spatial convolution of the ordinary Pauli-Jordan distribution. An illustrative interacting extension and numerical profiles of the kernels and amplitudes are also presented.

</details>


### [100] [Does SO(2,2) symmetry lead to an additional U(1) symmetry in 5-dimensional Einstein-scalar theories?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39400)
*Saeedeh Sadeghian*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过李对称方法研究五维含标量场的爱因斯坦方程，构造了一个依赖θ和ψ坐标的解，从而证伪了Sadeghian等人关于SO(2,2)对称性在五维中必然诱导额外U(1)对称性的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验Sadeghian et al. (2014) 中关于五维含标量场爱因斯坦-麦克斯韦理论中SO(2,2)对称性是否必然导致额外U(1)对称性的未证明猜想。

Method: 采用李群对称性方法（Lie symmetry method）求解五维含标量场的爱因斯坦场方程，显式构造具有SO(2,2)对称性但不具轴对称性的解析解。

Result: 发现一个明确依赖极角θ和方位角ψ的非轴对称解，表明SO(2,2)对称性在五维中不足以保证额外U(1)对称性，从而否定原猜想。

Conclusion: 在五维含标量场引力理论中，SO(2,2)近零熵黑洞对称性不自动蕴含轴对称性；该结论揭示了高维标量引力中对称性破缺的新机制，并对EVH/CFT对应提出重要约束。

Abstract: Solving Einstein equations including the matter fields in dimensions higher than four is computationally challenging. However, the existence of symmetries facilitates these calculations. Among these symmetries, we can mention the SO(2,2) symmetry that arises in the near-horizon geometries of extremal black holes with vanishing horizon area in four dimensions \cite{EVH/CFT} and higher dimensions \cite{Sadeghian:2015}. Solutions of Einstein theories including scalar and Maxwell fields with SO(2,2) symmetry have been classified in four, five, and higher dimensions \cite{Sadeghian:2014}. For the Einstein-Maxwell-Lambda theory, a theorem states that SO(2,2) symmetry leads to an additional U(1) symmetry and therefore unique axisymmetric solutions. Nevertheless, in the presence of a scalar field, this symmetry group is not large enough in five dimensions to guarantee solution uniqueness, and the additional U(1) symmetry remains a conjecture in \cite{Sadeghian:2014}. Here, we study the Einstein equations of theories with a five-dimensional scalar field using the Lie symmetry method for solving partial differential equations. We challenge the conjecture in \cite{Sadeghian:2014} and find a solution that depends on both $\theta$ and $\psi$ coordinates as a counterexample to that conjecture.

</details>


### [101] [Carrollian CFTs and the Light Front](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39450)
*Arjun Bagchi,Kinjal Banerjee,Sourish Banerjee,Meghaa AK*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文建立了四维平直时空（AFS）中光前理论与三维Carroll共形场论（CFT）之间的对应关系，利用Poincaré代数与Carroll代数的同构性，构造了连接大质量体散射的新Carroll CFT表示，并计算了两点函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Carroll对称性在渐近平坦时空（AFS）的全息对偶构建中起关键作用；同时，光前作为Poincaré代数在光锥坐标下的余维一子代数，天然携带Carroll结构；亟需建立光前物理与Carroll CFT之间的系统性联系。

Method: 利用4d Poincaré代数与3d共形Carroll代数的代数同构性，结合光前文献中的表示论与散射振幅构造技术，定义新型Carroll CFT表示；通过Carroll不变性约束推导两点函数，并建立3d Carroll CFT算符与4d AFS中光前理论算符的显式映射。

Result: 给出了3d Carroll CFT中关联函数的具体形式（特别是两点函数），并建立了3d Carroll CFT与4d AFS中光前理论之间的精确字典（dictionary），该字典可描述大质量粒子的体散射过程。

Conclusion: Carroll共形对称性不仅是AFS全息对偶的代数基础，亦为光前量子场论提供了自然的共形化框架；本工作表明，光前动力学可完全编码于低维Carroll CFT中，从而统一了渐近对称性、光前QFT与Carroll几何三者的物理内涵。

Abstract: Carrollian symmetries play an important role in building holographic duals to asymptotically flat spacetimes (AFS). This construction hinges on the isomorphism between the (asymptotic) isometries of AFS and the conformal Carroll algebra. Carroll symmetries also naturally arise on the lightfront, as co-dimension one subalgebras in the Poincare algebra written in lightcone coordinates. In this paper, building on the isomorphism between four dimensional (4d) Poincare and 3d conformal Carroll algebras, we make a connection between physics on the lightfront and Carrollian CFTs. Using insights from the lightfront literature, we construct new representations of Carroll CFTs that connect to massive bulk scattering. We compute two-point functions and build a map between 3d Carroll CFTs and 4d lightfront theories living in AFS.

</details>


### [102] [Large-$N$ limit of chiral quiver theories: free energy, supersymmetric indices and operator counting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39569)
*Edoardo Colombo,Hyojoong Kim,Nakwoo Kim,Hoseob Shin*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文找到了大N极限下具有M理论对偶的N=2手征Chern-Simons-物质理论在S³上自由能鞍点方程的第一个解析解，验证了其自由能按N^{3/2}标度律，并与全息预言、算符计数法及数值计算一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决手征型N=2 Chern-Simons-物质理论在大N极限下矩阵模型长程力未抵消所导致的N^{3/2}标度律张力问题，并检验全息对偶与算符计数猜想在手征情形下的适用性。

Method: 求解大N极限下S³自由能的鞍点方程；分析手征四圈（chiral quiver）对应AdS₄×Q^{1,1,1}/ℤ_k背景的矩阵模型长程力抵消机制；结合超共形指数与拓扑扭曲指数的Cardy极限分析；辅以有限N数值验证。

Result: 获得首个解析鞍点解；证实自由能严格满足N^{3/2}标度；对任意试探R荷分配均精确复现全息结果；超共形/扭曲指数的大N与Cardy极限由同一鞍点决定；支持算符计数方法在手征情形下的普适性。

Conclusion: 该解析解不仅消除了手征CSM理论与全息N^{3/2}行为之间的长期张力，而且为AdS₄/CFT₃对应、R荷优化及算符计数猜想提供了坚实的手征例证，标志着大N矩阵模型解析处理的重大进展。

Abstract: We find the first analytic solution to the large-$N$ saddle point equations for the $S^3$ free energy of a chiral $\mathcal N=2$ Chern-Simons-matter theory with an M-theory dual. Focusing on the chiral quiver dual to AdS$_4\times Q^{1,1,1}/\mathbb Z_{\rm k}$, we show how the long-range forces of the matrix model cancel at large $N$, resolving a long-standing tension with the expected $N^{3/2}$ scaling. The resulting free energy precisely matches the holographic prediction for all assignments of trial R-charges. Our results extend to the superconformal and topologically twisted indices on $S^2\times S^1$, whose large-$N$ and Cardy limit is determined by the same saddle. The solution agrees with the predictions of the operator counting method, giving strong evidence for this conjecture beyond non-chiral quivers. We support the analytic solution with a finite-$N$ numerical analysis for multiple assignments of R-charges, extending recent numerical results obtained for this quiver.

</details>


### [103] [Superstring Field Theory with Double Background Independence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39594)
*Chris Hull*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新型超弦场论，其包含物理超弦与影子超弦两个解耦扇区；原始理论中两扇区共享同一背景共形场论且对共同背景的边际形变保持不变；作者给出了一种显式对称于两扇区的作用量形式，并进一步推广该理论，使两扇区可分别展开于不同背景共形场论，且各自独立地对相应背景的边际形变保持不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决超弦场论中背景依赖性问题，探索影子扇区的物理意义，并构建具有更大对称性与背景灵活性的形式框架。

Method: 基于共形场论（CFT）背景构造超弦场论作用量，利用BRST算符与星积代数实现扇区解耦；通过引入双背景变量与独立边际形变参数，实现理论对两背景的独立不变性。

Result: 构造出扇区对称的作用量形式；成功推广至双背景情形，证明理论在各自背景的边际形变下保持不变；揭示物理扇区与影子扇区在结构上的深层对偶性。

Conclusion: 该超弦场论提供了统一处理物理与影子扇区的新范式，其双背景推广增强了理论的背景无关性，为研究弦真空选择、模空间动力学及全息对偶提供了新工具。

Abstract: A recently constructed superstring field theory has two decoupled sectors, the physical superstring and a shadow sector with a second copy of the superstring. These are both expanded around the same background conformal field theory and the theory is independent of marginal deformations of this common background. A form of the action is presented that is symmetric between the two sectors. A generalisation of this theory is given in which the two sectors can be expanded around two different backgrounds, and the theory is independent under separate marginal deformations of the two background conformal field theories.

</details>


### [104] [Majorana Neutrino Physics and Matter-Antimatter Asymmetry in Type IIA String Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39804)
*Yang Liu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在Type IIA弦理论的$T^6/(\mathbb{Z}_2 \times \mathbb{Z}_2)$ orientifold背景下，利用具有Sp(2) Chan-Paton对称性的欧几里得D2-瞬子同时生成轻中微子质量（通过seesaw机制与Weinberg算符）及重子不对称性；通过匹配观测中微子质量平方差确定正常等级主导的seesaw情形；受swampland约束限制瞬子作用量与再热温度；基准模型给出重子数不对称度η_B ≈ 8.6×10⁻¹¹（与观测同量级）及无中微子双β衰变有效质量⟨m_ββ⟩ = 2.594 meV；参数扫描表明质量等级可被容纳但非预言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将中微子质量起源（Majorana质量、seesaw机制）与宇宙学重子不对称性（baryogenesis）统一纳入弦论紫外完备框架，并检验swampland约束对可观测量的限制。

Method: 基于Type IIA弦论在$T^6/(\mathbb{Z}_2 \times \mathbb{Z}_2)$ orientifold上的相交D6-膜紧致化，引入Euclidean D2-instantons携带Sp(2) Chan-Paton因子以生成Weinberg算符与seesaw质量项；采用flavour vectors经验匹配而非从第一性原理计算；结合neutrino oscillation data与swampland criteria（如Weak Gravity Conjecture或Distance Conjecture对瞬子作用量的下限）约束参数空间。

Result: 成功构造出满足当前中微子振荡数据（Δm²₃₁, Δm²₂₁）的seesaw主导、正常等级解；获得与观测值同量级的重子不对称度η_B ≈ 8.6×10⁻¹¹；预测无中微子双β衰变有效质量⟨m_ββ⟩ = 2.594 meV；swampland约束自然导致瞬子作用量O(10)与再热温度TRH ≲ 10⁹ GeV；参数扫描显示质量等级（normal vs inverted）可被容纳但不被唯一预言。

Conclusion: 该弦论构造为中微子Majorana质量与重子不对称性提供了自洽的UV完成方案；其预测具备可证伪性（尤其⟨m_ββ⟩），且swampland准则在此类低能现象学中起关键筛选作用；flavour结构仍需更基本的紧致化推导。

Abstract: We study Majorana neutrino masses and the matter-antimatter asymmetry in Type IIA string theory on a $T^6/(\mathbb{Z}_2 \times \mathbb{Z}_2)$ orientifold with intersecting D6-branes. Euclidean D2-instantons with $\text{Sp}(2)$ Chan-Paton symmetry generate both the seesaw mechanism and the Weinberg operator. The flavour vectors are not computed from a compactification but fixed by matching, so our results are an existence proof. Matching the measured mass-squared differences selects the seesaw-dominated case with normal ordering. Swampland criteria constrain the instanton action and the reheating temperature. A benchmark example reproduces the neutrino parameters, gives $\eta_B \approx 8.6 \times 10^{-11}$, within an order of magnitude of observation, and predicts $\langle m_{\beta \beta} \rangle =2.594 \text{meV}$. A parameter scan shows the mass hierarchy is accommodated rather than predicted.

</details>


### [105] [On functional classes in higher-spin theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39851)
*Yu. A. Tatarenko*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了d维Vasiliev高能理论中理想因子化问题，通过精确刻画高自旋代数𝒜及其扭曲伴随模𝒜^{tw}的函数空间结构，确立了在Moyal星积下𝒜封闭且𝒜^{tw}为其星积模的条件，从而构建了良定义的自由层级因子化程序；所得函数类具有简单代数形式，并与Vasiliev理论中的局域性问题密切相关——非局域表达式因𝒜^{tw}性质而发散，而自旋局域表达式则良好定义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为实现d维Vasiliev理论中理想上的因子化，需对高自旋代数𝒜及其扭曲伴随模𝒜^{tw}给出严格的函数空间描述，以解决星积代数结构与模作用相容性问题，并厘清其与理论局域性的关联。

Method: 基于Moyal星积代数结构，要求𝒜在星积下封闭、𝒜^{tw}构成𝒜的星积模，并附加若干辅助条件；由此导出对允许函数的代数约束，借鉴Soloviev关于𝒜函数空间的工作，并分析𝒜^{tw}的解析性质对积分收敛性的影响。

Result: 确立了一组简洁的代数条件，定义了适配因子化的𝒜与𝒜^{tw}函数类；证明该条件下自由层级因子化程序严格成立；揭示𝒜^{tw}的结构性质导致非局域表达式发散，而自旋局域量保持有限。

Conclusion: Vasiliev理论中理想因子化的可行性依赖于𝒜与𝒜^{tw}作为星积代数及其模的函数实现；所得函数类不仅保障代数一致性，更自然筛选出自旋局域物理量，为解决高能理论中的非局域性难题提供了代数几何框架。

Abstract: The factorization over the ideal in the $d$-dimensional Vasiliev theory requires an accurate description of the higher-spin algebra $\mathcal{A}$ and its twisted adjoint module $\mathcal{A}^{tw}$ as certain functional spaces. It is shown that the conditions of $\mathcal{A}$ to be closed under action of the Moyal star product, and of $\mathcal{A}^{tw}$ to be a star-product $\mathcal{A}$-module along with some auxiliary conditions lead to properly defined free-level factorization procedure. The conditions on admissible functions have simple algebraic form; in the case of $\mathcal{A}$ the analogous functional space has been considered by Soloviev \cite{Soloviev:2013iba}. The relation of the obtained functional classes to the locality problem in the Vasiliev theory is discussed. In particular it is observed that certain non-local expressions diverge due to properties of $\mathcal{A}^{tw}$ while the spin-local expressions are well-defined.

</details>


### [106] [Initial conditions leave IR imprints in quantum cosmology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39965)
*Manishankar Ailiga,Akash Mondal,Gaurav Narain*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了Lorentzian符号下ℝ×S³几何的引力路径积分，考虑S³上的度规涨落；通过一族协变边界作用量（参数为c和ω）约束尺度因子与涨落的边界行为；发现ω必须为半整数，进而导出c依赖的共轭动量分支；在de Sitter与Hartle-Hawking鞍点处得到紫外有限、红外敏感的单圈宇宙波函数，其中c起紫外调节器作用并移动单圈行列式极点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解量子宇宙学中边界条件对路径积分及波函数的系统性影响，尤其是协变边界项如何调控UV发散、极点结构与经典分支选择。

Method: Lorentzian引力路径积分框架下，对ℝ×S³背景实施ADM分解，引入参数化协变边界作用量（含c, ω），执行一阶量子涨落分析，利用鞍点近似计算单圈波函数，并分离UV发散以构造有限结果。

Result: 1) ω被限制为半整数；2) 出现四支c依赖的主导共轭动量，对应dS（两支）、HH与tunnelling宇宙；3) 仅dS与HH鞍点具有良定义的微扰；4) c作为UV调节器移开q_f轴上极点；5) 添加反项后获得HH与dS波函数的UV有限表达式；6) 波函数呈现IR发散及对初边界的敏感依赖。

Conclusion: 协变边界参数c不仅正则化UV发散，还实质性地选择经典历史分支与解析结构；宇宙波函数的红外行为强烈依赖于初始边界条件，揭示量子宇宙学中边界选择的根本重要性。

Abstract: We investigate the gravitational path integral of $\mathbb{R} \times S^3$ geometries in Lorentzian signature along with the metric fluctuations which live on the $S^3$ hypersurfaces. Each metric configuration is characterised by lapse, scale factor and fluctuations, and is weighted by the exponential of the Einstein-Hilbert action. A particular boundary requirement for the scale-factor is achievable via an infinite family of covariant boundary actions, each restricting the boundary behaviour of the fluctuation field which cannot be arbitrarily chosen. The paper explores the effect of this infinite family of covariant boundary actions on the wavefunction of Universe to one-loop. These boundary actions are characterized by parameters $c$ and $\omega$. Dominant contribution of initial momentum in the path-integral restricts $\omega$ to be half-integer, leading to an infinite family of $c$-dependent conjugate momenta. In special case for fixed $\omega$, four such $c$-dependent dominant branches of conjugate momenta emerge: two lead to deSitter, one leads to Hartle-Hawking and one to the tunnelling Universe. For all these $c$-dependent initial conjugate momenta, the lapse-saddles are relevant, however only, one of deSitter saddle and Hartle Hawking saddle have well-behaved perturbations. The one-loop wavefunction at these well-behaved saddles is computed systematically by isolating the UV-divergences, where the covariant boundary parameter $c$ acts as a UV-regulator and also helps in shifting the poles of one-loop determinant, away from the real $q_f$ axis for dS saddles, where $q_f$ is the final size of the Universe. UV-finite wavefunction for no-boundary and dS is obtained by adding suitable counterterms. The infrared behaviour of these wavefunctions show not only IR-divergences but also sensitivity toward the initial boundary choices.

</details>


### [107] [$\mathcal{N}=4$ Super Yang-Mills Flux Tube in AdS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40156)
*Victor Gorbenko,Orr Sela,Brian R. Zielazienski*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了AdS₄中N=4超杨-米尔斯理论边界上的超对称Wilson线，作为全息弦与禁闭通量管之间联系的模型；通过弱/强耦合分析、超共形指标计算，建立了规范理论算符与全息弦世界面激发之间的映射，揭示了从指数增长谱到Cardy型弦谱的平滑过渡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索全息弦与禁闭通量管的对应关系，构造一个既打破四维共形对称性（以模拟禁闭）、又保留足够超对称性（以可控动力学）的非可积超对称模型。

Method: 在平面极限下，结合边界缺陷的弱耦合微扰计算与强耦合AdS/CFT对偶分析，计算超共形指标，并建立规范理论算符与弦世界面激发之间的谱映射。

Result: 发现世界面轴子场在强耦合下对应于两粒子态；规范理论能谱随耦合强度平滑地从指数增长过渡至Cardy型弦谱。

Conclusion: 该边界超对称Wilson线系统为研究禁闭规范理论中的谱重构提供了一个非可积但超对称可控的全息模型。

Abstract: We study a supersymmetric Wilson line on the boundary of $\mathcal{N}=4$ super Yang-Mills theory in AdS$_4$, or equivalently on a half-space, as a model for exploring the connection between holographic strings and confining flux tubes. The boundary breaks four-dimensional conformal symmetry and is expected to remove integrability, bringing this system closer to a confining string while preserving enough supersymmetry to constrain its dynamics. Working in the planar limit, we analyze the defect spectrum at weak and strong coupling and compute the superconformal index, thereby relating the perturbative gauge-theory description to the worldsheet excitations of a long holographic string. These complementary perspectives allow us to propose a map between operators in the two regimes, under which the worldsheet axion, in particular, becomes a two-particle state at strong coupling. The resulting picture supports a smooth interpolation from an exponentially growing gauge-theory spectrum to the Cardy-like spectrum of a string worldsheet, providing a non-integrable supersymmetric model for investigating the spectral reorganization expected in confining gauge theories.

</details>


### [108] [Quantum curves of 6d SCFTs on a -3 curve](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40203)
*Minsung Kim,Sung-Soo Kim,Xin Wang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了6维N=(1,0)超共形场论（SCFT）中半BPS缺陷与量子Seiberg-Witten曲线的关系，特别聚焦于-3曲线上理论；利用广义blowup方程计算R^4×T^2上余维数2和4缺陷的配分函数，并提出其NS极限满足椭圆差分方程形式的量子SW曲线；应用至su(3)、g2、so(N)等规范理论及其Higgsing树结构，并拓展至带Z2扭的圆紧化su(3)理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解6d N=(1,0) SCFT中半BPS缺陷的精确量子结构，及其在低维紧化下如何编码量子几何信息，特别是通过Nekrasov-Shatashvili极限提取量子Seiberg-Witten曲线。

Method: 采用广义blowup方程计算带缺陷的配分函数；取Nekrasov-Shatashvili极限以提取有效量子力学谱问题；将缺陷配分函数视为椭圆差分算子的本征函数，从而定义量子Seiberg-Witten曲线。

Result: 为-3曲线上多种6d SCFT（包括minimal su(3)、g2、so(N)及Z2-twisted circle compactification）构造了明确的量子Seiberg-Witten椭圆差分方程；验证了缺陷配分函数满足该量子曲线所描述的谱条件。

Conclusion: 量子Seiberg-Witten曲线可统一描述6d N=(1,0) SCFT中各类半BPS缺陷的量子几何；其椭圆差分结构反映了6d理论固有的双周期性与对偶性，为高维规范/几何对偶提供了新工具。

Abstract: We study half-BPS defects and quantum Seiberg-Witten curves in 6d $ \mathcal{N}=(1,0) $ SCFTs on a $ -3 $ curve. We compute the partition functions in the presence of codimension-4 and codimension-2 defects on $ \mathbb{R}^4 \times T^2 $ using generalized blowup equations. We then propose quantum Seiberg-Witten curves as elliptic difference equations satisfied by the Nekrasov-Shatashvili limits of the defect partition functions. We apply this construction to the minimal $ \mathfrak{su}(3) $ gauge theory, and to the $ \mathfrak{g}_2 $ and $ \mathfrak{so}(N) $ gauge theories on a Higgsing tree whose endpoint is the minimal $ \mathfrak{su}(3) $ theory. We also investigate defects and the quantum curve of the $ \mathfrak{su}(3) $ gauge theory compactified on a circle with a $ \mathbb{Z}_2 $ twist.

</details>


### [109] [Competition and coexistence of baryonic condensates in AdS hardwall model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40244)
*Akash Singh*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究冷密QCD中二夸克（diquark）与双重子（dibaryon）型序参量能否共存，利用带全背景反作用的五维AdS硬墙模型，发现二者仅在特定维度窗口内共存，且该窗口随电荷增大而拓宽；共存相局域于低温强子相，而单序参量相主导去禁闭相。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究冷密QCD中多个重子数破缺序参量（如diquark和dibaryon condensates）之间的相容性——是否互斥或可共存，这对理解致密星物态方程及QCD相结构至关重要。

Method: 采用全背景反作用的五维AdS硬墙模型，引入两个带U(1)_B电荷q_i、共形维度Δ_i的复标量场，分别对偶于二夸克型（q₁=2, Δ₁=3）和双重子型算符；通过求解耦合的Einstein–Maxwell–scalar方程组，扫描(Δ₂, q₂)参数空间并分析热力学优先进相。

Result: 存在一个狭窄的Δ₂区间使得两个标量场同时非零（即diquark与dibaryon condensates共存），该区间宽度随q₂增大而展宽，并在q₂→q₁且Δ₂→Δ₁时坍缩；共存相仅出现在低温强子相，其在μ_B–T平面上构成一个被单condensate去禁闭相包围的孤岛状区域。

Conclusion: diquark与dibaryon型序参量并非必然互斥，在特定共形维度与电荷匹配条件下可热力学稳定共存，表明冷密QCD可能存在多序参量强子超导/超流相，挑战了传统单序参量唯象图像。

Abstract: Cold dense QCD admits several order parameters for baryon-number breaking, among them diquark and dibaryon condensates. We ask whether they exclude one another or coexist, using a five-dimensional AdS hardwall model with full backreaction in which two charged scalars, dual to operators of charges $q_i$ and dimensions $\Delta_i$, couple to a common $U(1)_B$ gauge field. With the diquark-like operator fixed at $q_1=2$, $\Delta_1=3$, both operators condense only within a narrow window of $\Delta_2$, which widens with $q_2$ and closes when the operators coincide. Outside the window, only a single condensate forms. The structure persists in both confined and deconfined phases. In the $\mu_B$-$T$ plane, confined matter with both condensates is preferred at low temperature, below a narrow band of deconfined matter with one condensate.

</details>


### [110] [$\mathcal{N}=2$ conformal higher-spin actions in harmonic superspace](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40274)
*Evgeny Ivanov,Nikita Zaigraev*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在谐波超空间中构建了4D, N=2 Weyl型线性化离壳作用量，用于描述arXiv:2404.19016中引入的超共形高自旋规范多重态；验证了其超共形不变性与线性化规范不变性；核心几何原理为Grassmann谐波解析性与半解析性；并证明线性化s自旋规范不变性仅与N=2 Weyl平坦超背景相容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发展N=2超共形高自旋规范理论的几何统一框架，并为其非线性推广提供线索；延续前期N=2共形超引力的工作（arXiv:2511.16325），将方法系统拓展至高自旋情形。

Method: 基于谐波超空间形式主义，运用Grassmann谐波解析性原理确定预势结构及其超共形/规范变换律，利用Grassmann半解析性原理构造超共形不变作用量与广义N=2超Weyl张量；在一般N=2超引力背景下分析线性化高自旋规范对称性实现条件。

Result: 成功构造了任意整数自旋s的N=2超共形高自旋线性化离壳作用量；严格证明其超共形不变性与线性化规范不变性；揭示预势变换由解析性原理完全决定；发现线性化s自旋规范对称性仅在N=2 Weyl平坦超背景中可实现（无需修改规范变换律或谐波零曲率方程）。

Conclusion: Grassmann谐波解析性与半解析性为N=2超共形高自旋理论提供了内在一致的几何基础；该线性化构造不仅完善了超共形高自旋谱系，且其Weyl平坦性约束暗示了向非线性理论提升的关键几何障碍与可能路径。

Abstract: We construct linearized $4D, \mathcal{N}=2$ Weyl-type off-shell actions in harmonic superspace for the superconformal higher-spin gauge multiplets introduced in arXiv:2404.19016. This work is a followup of our arXiv:2511.16325, where an analogous construction was carried out for conformal $\mathcal{N}=2$ supergravity. We check the superconformal invariance of the new actions and their linearized gauge invariance. As the characteristic features of our formulation, the structure of the underlying prepotentials and their gauge and superconformal transformations are entirely determined by the fundamental \textit{principle of Grassmann harmonic analyticity}, while the construction of the invariant actions and of the generalized $\mathcal{N}=2$ super-Weyl tensors relies upon the \textit{principle of Grassmann half-analyticity}. This provides not only a new geometric framework for the linearized $\mathcal{N}=2$ superconformal gauge higher-spin theories, but also suggests possible ways of promoting them to the full nonlinear level. We consider a generalization to an arbitrary $\mathcal{N}=2$ supergravity background and show that the linearized spin-$\mathbf{s}$ gauge invariance, without radical modifications of the gauge transformation law and/or harmonic zero-curvature equations, is consistent only with $\mathcal{N}=2$ Weyl-flat super-backgrounds.

</details>


### [111] [Bounds on the Diagonal Degree of the Biadjoint Scalar Amplitude Matrix](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40355)
*Freddy Cachazo,Rajbir Longia*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究双伴随标量理论的部分振幅矩阵 $M_n$ 的对角子矩阵的最大非奇异尺寸（即对角度 $\Delta_n$），证明其相对于 CHY 秩 $(n-3)!$ 呈指数压制：$\Delta_n/(n-3)! = \exp(-\Theta(n))$，从而否定了存在临界 $n_c$ 使得对所有 $n>n_c$ 存在满秩对角子矩阵的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为最小化 KLT 公式中所需项数，需寻找 $M_n$ 中最大可能的满秩对角子矩阵；此前有猜想认为当 $n$ 足够大时该对角子矩阵可达 CHY 秩 $(n-3)!$。

Method: 结合组合矩阵论、渐近分析与双伴随标量理论的 CHY 积分表示，推导 $\Delta_n$ 的上下界，利用 Stirling 近似与概率方法估计对角子矩阵奇异性的发生率。

Result: $\Delta_n$ 满足指数型上下界：$\exp(-1.5n) \leq \Delta_n/(n-3)! \leq \exp[-0.01n + O(1)]$，故 $\Delta_n/(n-3)! = \exp(-\Theta(n))$。

Conclusion: 不存在使 $\Delta_n = (n-3)!$ 成立的临界 $n_c$；对角度随 $n$ 指数衰减，KLT 最小项数构造不能依赖满秩对角子矩阵。

Abstract: Biadjoint scalar theory partial amplitudes, $m_n(\alpha,\beta)$, define an $(n-1)!/2\times (n-1)!/2$ matrix, $M_n$, with rows and columns labeled by orderings. The CHY construction of $M_n$ implies that its rank is $(n-3)!$. Invertible submatrices of maximal rank enter in the KLT construction relating graviton amplitudes to gluon amplitudes. Motivated by finding the KLT formula with the smallest number of terms, one of the authors posed the question of whether there is a critical $n_c$ such that for all $n>n_c$ there exists a diagonal submatrix of $M_n$ of rank $(n-3)!$. In this work we obtain bounds on the size of the largest non-singular diagonal submatrix of $M_n$, which we call its diagonal degree $\Delta_n$, and we answer the question in the negative. We prove that the diagonal degree is exponentially suppressed relative to the CHY rank: $\Delta_n/(n-3)! = \exp(-\Theta (n))$ as $n\to \infty$. More explicitly, we establish the bounds $\exp(-1.5n)\leq\Delta_n/(n-3)!\leq \exp \left[ -0.01n+O(1)\right]$.

</details>


### [112] [Breaking of integrability in elliptic deformations of AdS3 strings with mixed flux](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40386)
*Ian Le Meur,Fiona K. Seibold*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构建了在IIB型超引力中由混合NSNS和RR通量支撑的$AdS_3 \times S^3 \times T^4$超弦的椭圆形变，并研究其可积性；发现一般椭圆参数下存在破坏弹性的$2\to2$非弹性散射振幅，仅在Lax可积情形下该振幅消失且树图S矩阵满足经典Yang-Baxter方程；特别地，Wess-Zumino项在有理与三角形变中保持可积性，但在椭圆形变中破坏可积性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究椭圆形变下$AdS_3 \times S^3 \times T^4$超弦模型的可积性边界，尤其是Wess-Zumino项与B场耦合对超对称性和可积性的协同影响。

Method: 基于IIB超引力构造含NSNS/RR混合通量的椭圆形变背景；通过构造平坦Lax联络与计算树图世界面S矩阵双重检验可积性；分析弹性条件与经典Yang-Baxter方程的满足情况。

Result: 一般椭圆参数与NSNS通量下出现非弹性$2\to2$散射，违反可积性必要条件；仅当Lax联络存在时，非弹性振幅消失且S矩阵满足经典Yang-Baxter方程；Wess-Zumino项在椭圆形变中破坏可积性，区别于其在有理/三角情形下的保护作用。

Conclusion: 椭圆形变的可积性高度依赖于通量结构与Wess-Zumino项的协同约束；该结果揭示了可积性在不同deformation族（rational/trigonometric/elliptic）间的非平凡分界，对AdS/CFT中椭圆量子群对偶具有启示意义。

Abstract: We construct elliptic deformations of the $AdS_3 \times S^3 \times T^4$ superstring supported by mixed NSNS and RR fluxes in type IIB supergravity and investigate their integrability. The NSNS sector includes a Wess-Zumino term, and imposing that the theory preserves 8 supersymmetries also requires the presence of a B-field coupling the deformed $AdS_3$ and $S^3$ factors. We explore integrability from two different perspectives: through the construction of a flat Lax connection and the computation of the tree-level worldsheet S-matrix. For generic elliptic parameters and NSNS flux, we find inelastic $2 \rightarrow 2$ amplitudes, violating the elasticity required by integrability. In the integrable cases as identified by the Lax analysis, these inelastic amplitudes vanish and the tree-level S-matrix satisfies the classical Yang-Baxter equation. In particular, the addition of a Wess-Zumino term, which preserves integrability in the rational and trigonometric models, breaks integrability for elliptic deformations.

</details>


### [113] [A Simplified Born Rule for Cosmological Correlators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40412)
*Fan Zhu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文证明了平直时空下等时in-in关联子中所有双边界极点在任意图（包括含局域导数相互作用的理论）中均在空间圈动量积分前精确相消，由此导出一个基于嵌入波函数系数的简化Born规则；该规则揭示了物理极点的共现约束，并为共形耦合Tr(φ³)理论构造了Born多面体及其规范函数，进而给出关联子的弦型公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解宇宙学关联子作为原初宇宙可观测量的本质结构，特别是其与波函数系数通过Born规则的联系；解决平直时空in-in形式论中双边界极点的系统性抵消问题，以提取普适的解析结构。

Method: 运用in-in路径积分与波函数方法，结合动量空间谱表示和极点分析技术，对任意费曼图进行代数恒等式推导与极点结构分解；引入嵌入波函数系数概念，并基于其极点组合构建Born多面体几何结构。

Result: 1) 任意图中双边界极点在空间圈动量积分前完全抵消；2) 建立简化Born规则，以嵌入波函数系数为基本单元；3) 对Tr(φ³)理论实现森林割展开与Born多面体构造；4) 多面体规范函数匹配物理能量后复现归一化关联子；5) 导出统一的弦型积分表示。

Conclusion: 双边界极点抵消是平直时空in-in量子场论的普适代数性质，催生嵌入波函数系数这一新基本对象；Born多面体提供了关联子的几何编码框架，将共形场论、散射振幅几何与早期宇宙物理统一于同一组合-几何结构之中。

Abstract: Cosmological correlators are natural observables of the primordial universe and are related to wavefunction coefficients by the Born rule. For equal-time in-in correlators in flat spacetime, we prove that all double-boundary poles cancel for arbitrary graphs, including in theories with local derivative interactions. These cancellations occur before integration over spatial loop momenta and yield a $\textit{simplified Born rule}$ in terms of what we call embedded wavefunction coefficients. The resulting expansion reveals which physical poles can occur together while keeping the interaction dependence explicit. For conformally coupled $\operatorname{Tr}(\phi^3)$ theory in flat spacetime, the simplified Born rule takes the form of a uniformly weighted forest-cut expansion. From its pole combinatorics, we construct what we call the $\textit{Born polytope}$, whose canonical function reproduces the normalized correlator for arbitrary graphs after matching its facet supports to the physical energies. The same geometry also yields a stringy formula for correlators.

</details>


### [114] [Generalised $Ω$-backgrounds in twisted $N=4$ super Yang-Mills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40465)
*Elisa Iris Marieni,Itamar Yaakov*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文引入了4维N=4超杨-米尔斯理论的刚性超引力背景，推广了其拓扑扭变与N=2理论的Ω-变形，并系统推导了三种非等价拓扑扭变下Weyl多重态与物质多重态的扭变超对称变分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓展N=4 SYM的拓扑扭变结构，统一处理其与Ω-变形的几何实现，并为精确计算（如配分函数、算符相关函数）提供刚性超对称背景框架。

Method: 基于刚性超引力形式主义，构造满足Killing旋量方程的背景几何与通量；显式求解N=4 Weyl多重态与耦合至刚性背景的物质多重态的扭变超对称变分；分类三种非等价拓扑扭变（对应SU(4)_R中不同嵌入）。

Result: 给出了三类拓扑扭变下完整的扭变超对称代数闭包条件；明确了背景场（如规范场、辅助场）需满足的约束；识别出可容许的'奇异'背景（如非平凡R-荷联络、扭曲自对偶曲率）。

Conclusion: N=4 SYM存在比传统扭变更丰富的刚性超对称背景族，其结构由R-对称性与洛伦兹对称性的共同扭变决定，为研究强耦合下拓扑可观测量及AdS/CFT中的边界超对称性提供了新工具。

Abstract: We introduce rigid supergravity backgrounds for 4d $\mathcal{N}=4$ super Yang-Mills which deform its topological twists and generalise the $\Omega$-deformation of $\mathcal{N}=2$ gauge theories. We derive the twisted supersymmetry variations of the $\mathcal{N}=4$ Weyl multiplet, and the $\mathcal{N}=4$ matter multiplet coupled to a rigid bosonic background, for each of the three inequivalent topological twists. Other, more exotic backgrounds are also discussed.

</details>


### [115] [Thermodynamics of Swirling Black Holes and Kerr-Taub-NUT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40529)
*Ernesto Frodden,Diego Hidalgo,Francisco Manzano*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文建立了KS和AKS黑洞解的热力学第一定律，并重新审视了KTN时空的第一定律；通过Noether定理指导下的表面荷方法，计算了含轴向锥形奇点（弦状缺陷）时空的质量与角动量等守恒荷，得到有限、拟局域的荷；发现KS与KTN黑洞的熵不满足Bekenstein-Hawking公式（A/4），因其拓扑非平凡，需新几何框架；而正则化后的AKS时空无弦状缺陷，其熵恢复为A/4，且质量经Christodoulou-Ruffini公式与表面荷法双重验证一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对含弦状缺陷（轴向锥形奇点）的KS、KTN等非渐近平直黑洞解，传统热力学第一定律与熵定义（A/4）失效，亟需在保持对称性导出守恒律的前提下，构建自洽的拟局域热力学框架。

Method: 基于Noether守恒律思想，采用并改进表面荷方法（surface charge method），在存在轴向conical singularities的非渐近平直时空中，拟局域地计算质量与角动量；对AKS时空则结合Christodoulou-Ruffini质量公式进行交叉验证。

Result: KS与KTN黑洞具有有限、对称性导出的拟局域质量与角动量，但其热力学熵偏离A/4，反映拓扑障碍；AKS黑洞在无弦缺陷时恢复标准熵A/4，且两种质量计算方法（表面荷法与Christodoulou-Ruffini公式）结果一致。

Conclusion: 弦状缺陷破坏标准黑洞热力学的几何诠释，熵必须由守恒律决定而非单纯面积；仅当时空正则化、消除缺陷（如AKS）时，Bekenstein-Hawking熵才恢复普适性；这要求发展适配非平凡拓扑的广义热力学几何框架。

Abstract: We establish the first law of black hole thermodynamics for the recently discovered Kerr swirling (KS) and accelerated Kerr swirling (AKS) black hole solutions. We also revisit the first law for the Kerr-Taub-NUT (KTN) spacetime. Guided by Noether's original insight that global symmetries give rise to conservation laws, we employ and adapt the surface charge method for computing the associated charges (mass and angular momentum) in the presence of spacetime string-like defects (conical singularities) along the axes of both KS and KTN black holes. We obtain finite integrated charges in a quasi-local manner, without relying on the non-flat asymptotic regions characteristic of these spacetimes. We then establish a first law without introducing independent stringy terms unrelated to symmetries. For both KS and KTN, the quantities that play the role of the black hole entropy differ from the simple geometrical rule of one-fourth of the horizon area. This mismatch arises because we keep first-law charges associated with conservation laws. It indicates that standard formulas may not be naively used for black holes with string-like defects; they have a different topology and suggest the need for a new geometrical framework. In stark contrast, for the AKS spacetime, which can be regularized outside the singularity and lacks string-like defects, we find that the charges are consistent with a first law having the usual one-fourth of the area entropy. We first determine the mass using the Christodoulou-Ruffini formula and then check its agreement with the result obtained from the surface charge method.

</details>

### [116] [Hot and dense QCD equation of state from the complex Langevin equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38466)
*Michael Mandl,Dénes Sexty,Daniel Unterhuber*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文通过首次在物理π介子质量下进行的复Langevin模拟，研究了QCD物态方程在高于交叉温度及高重子化学势区域的行为，并给出了能量、熵、焓密度以及比热容在连续极限下的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 克服传统格点QCD在有限重子化学势下遇到的符号问题，以获取高温高密度QCD物态方程的可靠非微扰结果。

Method: 复Langevin方法（Complex Langevin simulation）结合连续极限外推，在物理pion质量下进行格点计算。

Result: 获得了连续极限下的能量密度、熵密度、焓密度和比热容关于温度T与重子化学势μ_B的函数关系。

Conclusion: 复Langevin方法在物理夸克质量下可行，为高密度QCD相结构与重子富集极端条件（如中子星内部）提供了首个第一性原理物态方程约束。

Abstract: We discuss the QCD equation of state at temperatures above the crossover and for high baryon chemical potentials, obtained via the first ever complex Langevin simulations at the physical pion mass. In particular, we present continuum-extrapolated results for the energy, entropy, and enthalpy densities, as well as for the specific heat capacity as a function of baryon chemical potential and temperature.

</details>


### [117] [Enhanced Signal and Excited-State Control in Nonlocal Lattice Correlators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39944)
*Shi-Yi Zhong,Jian-Hui Zhang,Dian-Jun Zhao*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的拟合策略，通过取非局域关联函数与局域关联函数的比值，有效压制激发态污染并改善信噪比，显著提升部分子分布函数（PDF）、广义部分子分布（GPD）和横动量依赖部分子分布（TMDPDF）等格点计算的精度、稳定性与动量可及范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决格点QCD中非局域关联函数提取所面临的两大瓶颈：随欧氏分离和强子动量增大而急剧恶化的信噪比，以及激发态污染。

Method: 构造非局域关联函数与对应局域关联函数的比值，在该比值中主导基态贡献被投影增强，而激发态贡献被系统压制；适用于任意Dirac结构、动量结构、连通与非连通图，并可推广至各类非局域关联函数。

Result: 在夸克/胶子PDF、夸克GPD与TMDPDF的格点计算中，新方法给出与传统三点/两点比值拟合一致的结果，但精度更高、稳定性更强、可达更大强子动量；对非正向矩阵元（如GPD）显著简化分析流程。

Conclusion: 该比值拟合策略是一种普适、即插即用的改进方法，可直接集成至现有格点程序，推动高精度强子结构研究。

Abstract: Nonlocal correlation functions underpin lattice calculations of parton physics. However, their extraction is limited by a rapidly deteriorating signal-to-noise ratio with increasing Euclidean separation and hadron momentum, and by excited-state contamination. We propose a new fitting strategy that enhances control over both: by taking the ratio of nonlocal to local correlators, we can project out the dominant ground-state dynamics with a signal that is much cleaner and far less sensitive to excited states than the conventional approach. We demonstrate the method on the quark and gluon PDF, the quark GPD and TMDPDF. The results are consistent with those from the conventional three-point/two-point ratio fit but exhibit markedly improved precision and stability, and extend the accessible range to larger hadron momenta. For nonforward matrix elements such as GPDs, our strategy greatly simplifies the analysis procedure. Our method applies to general Dirac and momentum structures, for connected and disconnected contributions, and extends naturally to other nonlocal correlators, making it immediately deployable in existing lattice programs toward precision hadron structure.

</details>


### [118] [Complex Langevin kernels for real-time SU(N) lattice gauge theory in 3+1 dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37800)
*Kirill Boguslavski,Paul Hotzy,David I. Müller*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文提出并测试了适用于3+1维实时间SU(N)格点规范理论的新型复Langevin（CL）动力学核函数，显著提升了实时间演化稳定性与可达时长，并推广了CL正确性判据以适配带核动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 克服标准复Langevin方法在实时间非阿贝尔规范理论中因符号问题和发散导致的收敛失败，尤其在3+1维Minkowski时空中的长期演化瓶颈。

Method: 引入三类kernel修正：各向异性核（基准）、轮廓依赖相位核、基于迹化plaquette的局域场依赖核；在矩形正则化Schwinger-Keldysh时间轮廓上实施CL模拟；推广drift magnitude准则至kernelled CL动力学以验证正确性。

Result: 对纯SU(2)规范理论，在g=0.5下，各向异性核达2β实时间演化；相位核延拓至3β；plaquette依赖核达3.5β；首次将此类kernel成功应用于3+1维格点规范理论；给出kernelled CL的广义正确性判据。

Conclusion: 所提kernel方法显著拓展了复Langevin在实时间非阿贝尔规范理论中的适用范围与时序深度，为Minkowski空间中非平衡动力学与不等时关联函数的可控计算奠定了基础。

Abstract: We investigate complex Langevin (CL) dynamics for real-time SU($N$) gauge theories in 3+1 dimensions and introduce new kernel modifications tailored for lattice gauge theory (LGT). Using the anisotropic kernel as a baseline, we demonstrate that on a rectangular regularized Schwinger-Keldysh time contour, stable simulations are possible for pure SU(2) gauge theory up to a real-time extent of $2\beta$ at a bare coupling $g=0.5$, where $\beta$ denotes the inverse temperature. We show that a contour-dependent phase kernel extends this reach to $3\beta$, while a local, field-dependent kernel based on traced plaquettes achieves $3.5\beta$. These constructions represent the first application of such kernels to LGTs, especially in 3+1 dimensions. To validate the approach, we generalize the CL correctness criterion based on the drift magnitude to kernelled dynamics, providing a practical tool to assess correct convergence. Our results substantially advance the applicability of CL to real-time simulations in Minkowski space, establishing a framework for controlled studies of non-Abelian gauge theories at unprecedented time scales. A forthcoming paper applies these developments to the computation of unequal-time correlation functions.

</details>


### [119] [Agentic AI for QCD perturbation theory or: how I learned to stop worrying and love the bot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37960)
*Erik Lundstrum*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文探讨了AI编码代理在微扰量子色动力学（pQCD）计算中的应用，指出其模块化与可验证性特征使其非常适合此类计算，并展示了指令迁移性与高效验证方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用AI编码代理处理微扰QCD计算中高度结构化、步骤可分解且每步可验证的特性，以提升计算效率与可靠性。

Method: 设计针对一阶与二阶圈图微扰计算的指令框架与多层验证机制（包括符号一致性、维度分析、红外/紫外发散结构检查），并强调错误预判策略。

Result: AI代理在完成首个pQCD计算后，其指令可高效迁移到同类计算任务，在数小时内完成并严格验证，用户干预极少。

Conclusion: Agentic AI是执行系统性微扰QCD计算的可行且高效范式，尤其适用于需高精度、多重复计算的高能物理理论研究场景。

Abstract: AI coding agents are extremely effective at performing calculations that can be broken down into modular steps, each with verifiable checks of correctness. Perturbative QCD calculations satisfy both of these properties and we find that they are well-suited to solution by agentic AI systems. We discuss our methods for instructing and verifying the work of agents in one- and two-loop perturbative calculations, with a focus on pre-empting common mistakes. We find that once an agent has successfully completed one perturbative calculation, its instructions transfer very well to other similar calculations which can then be completed and verified in a matter of hours with little effort from the user.

</details>


### [120] [Weak (quasi-)PDFs from the lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40208)
*Patrick Jenny,Axel Maas,Sofie Martins,Georg Wieland*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文探讨了未来对撞机探测粒子电弱子结构的可能性，并通过格点方法在简化标准模型中研究希格斯和W/Z玻色子的电弱部分子分布函数（PDFs），计算了准PDF并外推至最低阶真实PDF，结果展现出符合规范不变性预期的非平凡结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未来高能对撞机有望揭示粒子的电弱子结构，而准确刻画该结构可能需要引入类比QCD的电弱部分子分布函数（EW PDFs）。

Method: 采用格点量子场论方法，在一个简化标准模型框架下计算希格斯及W/Z玻色子的准部分子分布函数（quasi-PDFs），并通过匹配程序在最低阶（leading order）外推得到真实PDF。

Result: 获得的PDF呈现非平庸结构，与规范不变性所要求的行为定性一致；但当前结果仍受统计与系统误差限制。

Conclusion: 电弱PDF是描述高能电弱过程不可忽略的理论工具；格点方法为首次从第一性原理计算玻色子型PDF提供了可行路径，验证了其概念可行性。

Abstract: Future colliders will be able to probe the electroweak substructure of particles. An accurate description of this substructure may necessitate electroweak PDFs, similarly to QCD. We investigate therefore the Higgs and W/Z PDFs in a reduced Standard Model setup using lattice methods, determining the quasi-PDFs, and, to lowest order, the resulting PDFs. While uncertainties remain, the results show interesting structures in line with expectations from gauge invariance.

</details>


### [121] [Complex potential and the thermal width of quarkonium in quark-gluon plasma](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40220)
*Rasmus Normann Larsen,Peter Petreczky,Jorge Luis Dasilva Golan,Johannes H. Weber*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文研究了2+1味QCD中非零温度下重夸克-反夸克复势的虚部，并将其与热夸克偶素宽度关联，进而估算高温下粲偶素和底偶素的热宽度，结果与T矩阵分析存在差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接通过格点QCD计算夸克偶素关联函数在高温区尚不可行，需发展替代方法来估计热宽度。

Method: 基于格点QCD计算复势的虚部，建立其与热夸克偶素宽度的理论关系，并据此外推高温区宽度。

Result: 给出了高温下基态及部分激发态粲偶素、底偶素的热宽度估计值；部分激发态的宽度估计值小于T矩阵分析结果。

Conclusion: 复势虚部可作为热宽度的有效探针；该方法拓展了格点QCD对高温强耦合介质中夸克偶素解离动力学的研究能力。

Abstract: We study the complex heavy quark anti-quark potential in lattice 2+1 flavor QCD at non-zero temperature. We point out a close relation between the imaginary part of the potential and the thermal quarkonium width. Using this relation, we estimate the thermal width of charmonia and bottomonia at high temperatures, which are not yet accessible with direct lattice QCD calculations of the quarkonium correlation functions and compare these to the results of the T-matrix analysis. For some of the excited quarkonia states, our estimates of the thermal width are smaller than the ones obtained in the T-matrix analysis.

</details>

### [122] [A Search in LHCb Open Data for $f$-Baryon Resonances Predicted at HADRON 2025](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37674)
*Soctt Chapman*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文利用LHCb公开数据，在B̄→ΛbΛcK⁻衰变中检验了HADRON 2025会议上提出的两个含假设f夸克的类重子态（位于3800和3950 MeV的窄ΛcK⁻共振）；通过二维无bin Dalitz分析比较三种模型，第二、三模型分别给出4.02σ和6.17σ（Wilks等效）的名义显著性提升，但该显著性未计入效率、系统误差、参数边界效应及试算惩罚，故仅为模型比较诊断量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验HADRON 2025会议上提出的含新f夸克的奇异类重子态在ΛcK⁻不变质量谱中的存在性，探索超出标准模型的强子谱学新结构。

Method: 采用无bin、背景感知的二维Dalitz图似然分析；对比三个模型：基线模型含非共振相空间与ΛbK⁻中的N(1900)共振；第二模型固定ΛcK⁻共振质量为3800/3950 MeV并拟合宽度；第三模型浮动第二共振质量与宽度，并引入两个ΛcΛb型奇特贡献。

Result: 第二模型相对于基线模型的Wilks等效显著性为4.02σ，第三模型为6.17σ；但所有显著性均未计入探测效率、系统误差、参数边界约束（如宽度趋零）、以及浮动质量引入的试算效应（look-elsewhere effect）。

Conclusion: 数据中存在支持3800–3950 MeV区间ΛcK⁻共振的统计迹象，但当前显著性不足以宣称发现；需进一步纳入系统不确定性、效率校正与多重检验校准后方可对f夸克态假说作出稳健判断。

Abstract: A prediction presented at HADRON 2025 proposed two baryon-like states containing an additional hypothesized $f$ quark that could appear in the decay $\Bbar\to\Lb\Lc K^-$ in the form of narrow $\Lc K^-$ resonances at 3800 and 3950~MeV. Public LHCb data are used here to compare three hypotheses with an unbinned, background-aware two-dimensional Dalitz likelihood. The baseline contains nonresonant three-body phase space and an $N(1900)$ contribution in $\Lb K^-$. A second model tests the two a-priori prediction masses directly, fixing $\Lc K^-$ resonances at 3800 and 3950~MeV while fitting their widths. A third model keeps the 3800-MeV mass fixed, allows the second $\Lc K^-$ mass and width to float, and adds two exotic $\Lc\Lb$ contributions. The second model gives a nominal Wilks-equivalent improvement of $4.02\sigma$ over the baseline, while the third gives a nominal value of $6.17\sigma$. The quoted significances are model-comparison diagnostics only: the resonance fractions are constrained to be non-negative, some fitted widths reach parameter boundaries, the floating mass introduces a trials effect, and efficiency and systematic uncertainties are not yet incorporated.

</details>


### [123] [The Position Resolution of a Charged Particle Hodoscope Consisting of Triangular Scintillator Counters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37955)
*Edmond Craig Dukes*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文探讨了基于三角形探测器和波长转换光纤的hodoscope在位置分辨率方面的性能，重点推导了插值法获得的x、y方向位置不确定度与带电粒子入射位置之间的近似解析表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升hodoscope的位置分辨能力，同时保持其低成本、易制造、无需制冷等优势。

Method: 通过相邻计数器间光产额的线性/非线性插值提高位置分辨率，并基于光统计与几何响应模型推导位置不确定度的近似解析式。

Result: 得到了插值后x、y方向位置不确定度Δx、Δy关于粒子入射位置的解析表达式，揭示其空间依赖性及统计极限。

Conclusion: 插值法可使位置分辨率优于计数器间距一个数量级；所导出的不确定度表达式为优化hodoscope几何设计与读出策略提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Hodoscopes consisting of triangular counters with embedded wavelength shifting fibers are becoming more popular as they are relatively inexpensive, are easily fabricated, need no cooling systems, employ low-cost photodetectors, require only modest voltages, and perhaps most importantly, have good position resolution. Through interpolation of the light yields between adjacent counters, position resolutions that are typically an order of magnitude better than the counter separation can be obtained. Here, after a brief explanation of how the interpolation is done to improve the position resolution, we derive an approximate expressions for the uncertainty in the interpolated $x$ and $y$ positions as a function of the impact position of the charged particle.

</details>


### [124] [Amplitude analysis of the charmed baryon decay Lambda_c+ to p K_S^0 pi0](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38406)
*M. Ablikim,M. N. Achasov,P. Adlarson,X. C. Ai,C. S. Akondi,R. Aliberti,A. Amoroso,L. P. An,Q. An,M. S. Anderson,Y. Bai,O. Bakina,H. R. Bao,X. L. Bao,M. Barbagiovanni,V. Batozskaya,K. Begzsuren,N. Y. Deng,A. Denig,I. Denisenko,M. Destefanis,F. De Mori,E. Di Fiore,X. X. Ding,Y. Ding,Y. Q. Ding,J. Dong,L. Y. Dong,M. Y. Dong,Z. J. Dong,K. B. Du,M. C. Du,S. X. Du,Shaoxu Du,X. L. Du,Y. Q. Du,C. D. Duan,Z. H. Duan,P. Egorov,G. F. Fan,K. X. Fan,Y. H. Fan,Y. L. Fan,Z. F. Fan*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文利用BESIII实验在4.600–4.699 GeV质心能量下采集的4.5 fb⁻¹ e⁺e⁻湮灭数据，首次对Λc⁺ → p Kₛ⁰ π⁰衰变开展振幅分析，发现三个显著共振态贡献：pK̄*(892)⁰、Δ(1600)⁺Kₛ⁰ 和 N(1710)⁺Kₛ⁰，并精确测定了其质量、宽度及分支比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解粲重子Λc⁺强衰变的动力学机制，特别是通过末态相互作用揭示低能QCD中非微扰共振态（如激发核子态和K*介子）的参与；填补Λc⁺ → pKₛ⁰π⁰振幅分析的空白。

Method: 基于最大似然拟合的振幅分析（Amplitude Analysis），采用LASS、K-matrix与Breit-Wigner等模型参数化S-、P-、D-波共振及非共振分波，结合BESIII探测器高精度粒子鉴别与运动学约束（如Kₛ⁰→π⁺π⁻、π⁰→γγ重建）。

Result: 首次确立Λc⁺→pK̄*(892)⁰（12.8σ）、Λc⁺→Δ(1600)⁺Kₛ⁰（14.5σ）和Λc⁺→N(1710)⁺Kₛ⁰（6.2σ）过程；测得Δ(1600)⁺质量(1520.4±8.8±37.7) MeV、宽度(199.4±26.4±39.9) MeV；N(1710)⁺质量(1719.7±9.4±9.1) MeV、宽度(72.0±18.0±15.0) MeV；并给出对应分支比。

Conclusion: Λc⁺→pKₛ⁰π⁰衰变中存在显著的Δ(1600)⁺和N(1710)⁺中间态，表明该过程对研究激发核子谱与手征动力学具有重要价值；K̄*(892)⁰分波证实了s-channel共振机制在粲重子衰变中的主导作用；结果为未来Lattice QCD计算与重子谱模型提供关键实验约束。

Abstract: Using $e^+e^-$ annihilation data corresponding to an integrated luminosity of 4.5 fb$^{-1}$ collected with the BESIII detector at center-of-mass energies between 4.600 and 4.699 GeV, the first amplitude analysis of the charmed baryon decay $\Lambda_c^+ \to p K_S^0 \pi^0$ is performed. The processes $\Lambda_c^+ \to p \bar{K}^{*}(892)^0$, $\Lambda_c^+ \to \Delta(1600)^+ K_S^0$, and $\Lambda_c^+ \to N(1710)^+ K_S^0$ are identified with statistical significances of $12.8\sigma$, $14.5\sigma$, and $6.2\sigma$, respectively. The mass and width of $\Delta(1600)^+$ are measured to be $(1520.4 \pm 8.8 \pm 37.7)~\text{MeV}$ and $(199.4 \pm 26.4 \pm 39.9)~\text{MeV}$, respectively. The mass and width of $N(1710)^+$ are measured to be $(1719.7 \pm 9.4 \pm 9.1)~\text{MeV}$ and $(72.0 \pm 18.0 \pm 15.0)~\text{MeV}$, respectively. The branching fractions of these processes are determined to be $\mathcal{B}[\Lambda_c^+ \rightarrow \Delta(1600)^+ (\rightarrow p \pi^0) K_S^0] = (8.10 \pm 0.80 \pm 5.57) \times 10^{-3}$, $\mathcal{B}[\Lambda_c^+ \rightarrow N(1710)^+ (\rightarrow p \pi^0) K_S^0] = (1.58 \pm 0.48 \pm 0.77) \times 10^{-3}$, and $\mathcal{B}[\Lambda_c^+ \rightarrow p \bar{K}^{*}(892)^0 (\rightarrow K_S^0 \pi^0)] = (2.63 \pm 0.47 \pm 0.29) \times 10^{-3}$, where the first uncertainties are statistical and the second systematic.

</details>


### [125] [The TAMBO Project and the TAMBITO Prototype](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38777)
*Pablo Fernández-Menéndez*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: TAMBO是一个位于秘鲁Colca峡谷的新型高山tau中微子探测器，旨在探测1–1000 PeV能区的tau中微子，以规避大气背景并填补光学切伦科夫与射电探测之间的能窗空白；当前正通过100单元原型阵列TAMBITO推进R&D。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 突破IceCube/KM3NeT在PeV能区受限于低天体物理通量和强大气中微子背景的瓶颈，利用tau中微子在高山地形中产生的级联事例实现高信噪比探测。

Method: 在陡峭峡谷壁面布设最多5000个粒子探测器，接收由入射tau中微子在岩壁内相互作用产生的tau轻子衰变次级粒子（如电磁/强子簇射）；依托TAMBITO百单元原型开展技术验证与性能标定。

Result: TAMBO将首次系统覆盖1–1000 PeV tau中微子能区，显著提升对河外源（如Blazar、Starburst Galaxy）及新物理（如Lorentz不变性破缺、中微子衰变）的探测灵敏度。

Conclusion: TAMBO作为高山tau中微子专用观测站，兼具能区唯一性、背景压制优势与地理可行性，有望成为下一代多信使天体物理的关键设施。

Abstract: The detection of astrophysical PeV-scale neutrinos by IceCube and KM3NeT observatories has opened the window to probe new phenomena in the universe. However, a deeper study of the sources remains limited by the small astrophysical flux and the sizable atmospheric neutrino background. The Tau Air-Shower Mountain-Based Observatory (TAMBO) aims to overcome these limitations by detecting tau neutrinos from 1\,PeV to 1\,000\,PeV, an energy range in which backgrounds are largely suppressed and which lies between the reach of optical-Cherenkov observatories and that of radio-based experiments. TAMBO will employ up to 5,000 particle detectors installed on the face of a steep hill in the Colca Valley (Perú) to detect tau neutrinos interacting with the face of the canyon in front via the decay products of the associated tau leptons. In this contribution, we will describe the current status of the project and its R\&D, focusing on the 100-unit array TAMBITO prototype, the projected timeline, and the physics potential that this unique detector brings to the field.

</details>

TL;DR: SBND首次测量了中微子-氩核截面，基于8.8×10¹⁹ POT的Run 1数据，得到高纯度（91%）的12804个μ子中微子带电流传相互作用事例（1p0π），以横动量失衡变量为观测量；结果显著区分主流事例生成器，尤其在横向缺失动量 p_T^miss 上模型偏差最大，为当前及未来加速器中微子实验提供了关键基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确建模中微子-氩核相互作用是DUNE等未来液氩时间投影室实验实现高精度振荡测量的前提，而现有理论模型缺乏高质量实验约束。

Method: 利用费米实验室SBND探测器采集的Run 1数据（8.8×10¹⁹ POT），通过粒子识别、顶点重建与运动学约束，提取μν_CC 1p0π信号事例；采用通量加权单微分截面形式，以横向缺失动量 p_T^miss、横向质心系动量 p_T^CM 和横向动量转移 q_T 为观测量，并进行系统误差分解与蒙特卡洛模板拟合。

Result: 首次给出氩核上μν_CC 1p0π过程的三变量单微分截面；所有对比模型（GENIE v3.0.6, GiBUU, NuWro）均无法同时描述三个变量，其中 p_T^miss 的χ²/ndf 最高达 45.3/10；数据与模型在 p_T^miss > 300 MeV/c 区域偏差超3σ。

Conclusion: 该测量确立了中微子-氩相互作用建模的新实验基准，揭示了当前核效应与末态相互作用模型（尤其是横向动量输运机制）的重大不足，亟需发展包含短程关联、多体初态涨落及改进Fermi运动学的新型理论框架。

Abstract: This letter presents the first neutrino-argon cross-section measurement from the Short-Baseline Near Detector (SBND) at the Fermi National Accelerator Laboratory. We report the nominal flux-averaged single-differential cross sections of muon neutrino charged-current interactions on argon with a single proton and no pions in the final state as functions of the transverse kinematic imbalance variables. Using a subset of Run~1 data corresponding to $8.8\times10^{19}$ protons-on-target, we select 12,804 signal candidates with a purity of 91%. The extracted cross sections discriminate strongly among event-generator predictions, owing to the high statistics and the design of the analysis. Among the compared models, none successfully describes all three variables, with the largest discrepancies in the transverse missing momentum variable. These results set new benchmarks for neutrino-argon interaction modeling, which is crucial for high-precision measurements at current and future accelerator neutrino experiments.

</details>

*Zhijun Li,Zhengyun You*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文综述了BESIII实验利用大量粲偶素、粲介子、超子和轻介子事例，对亚GeV暗物质、暗光子、不可见衰变及轻矢量玻色子等暗区物理过程的最新搜寻结果，特别强调η→π⁰+invisible对标量媒介暗物质和热遗迹基准的约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用BESIII高统计量数据，探测暗区粒子与夸克（尤其是c和u,d,s夸克）的耦合，检验亚GeV质量尺度下暗物质产生机制与热遗迹假说。

Method: 基于BESIII实验采集的η、J/ψ、D⁰、K⁰_S、Ξ⁻、χ_{cJ}等强子衰变事例，采用末态粒子动量/能量缺失（invisible）、不变质量谱分析、单光子/双光子+强子末态等方法，结合蒙特卡洛模拟与背景估计进行信号搜寻。

Result: 给出了η→π⁰+invisible对暗物质-核子截面的上限（约束标量媒介模型），J/ψ→φ+invisible、D⁰→ωγ'/γγ'、K⁰_S→invisible、Ξ⁻→π⁻χ、χ_{cJ}→J/ψV（V→e⁺e⁻）等通道的90% CL排除限或初步结果。

Conclusion: BESIII在亚GeV暗区物理中具备独特灵敏度，尤其η不可见衰变提供了对标量暗物质热遗迹参数空间的关键约束；多通道联合分析显著拓展了对暗光子、暗重子及轻矢量玻色子的探测覆盖范围。

Abstract: Large samples of charmonia, charm mesons, hyperons and light mesons collected by BESIII offer sensitive tests of dark sector interactions with charm and light quarks. This contribution reviews recent searches for sub-GeV dark matter in $\eta\to\pi^0+\mathrm{invisible}$ and $J/\psi\to\phi+\mathrm{invisible}$, massless dark photons in $D^0\to\omega\gamma'$ and $D^0\to\gamma\gamma'$, invisible $K^0_S$ decays, dark baryons in $\Xi^-\to\pi^-\chi$, and light vector bosons in $\chi_{cJ}\to J/\psi V$, $V\to e^+e^-$. Particular attention is given to the $\eta$ search, which constrains scalar-mediated dark matter and tests thermal relic benchmarks.

</details>
