Paulus,Jennifer Popp,Thomas R. Preston,Isabella Probst,Sripati V. Rahul,Thomas Roth,Felipe C. Salgado,Roland Sauerbrey,Alexander Sävert,Hans-Peter Schlenvoigt,Jan Patrick Schwinkendorf,Frank Seiboth,Georgiy Shoulga,Minxue Tang,Abha Thazhathe Peedika,Monika Toncian,Toma Toncian,Vojtěch Vozda,Edgar Weckert,Colin Wessel,Jan Wild,Ulf Zastrau,Matt Zepf*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文报道了在欧洲XFEL的HED光束线上，利用EuXFEL X射线与ReLaX强激光对撞开展的暗场轻-轻散射（light-by-light scattering）实验。通过偏振分辨暗场探测与逐发次时空重叠诊断，结合端到端模拟，在2751次泵浦-探针碰撞中未观测到超出本底的信号，从而在95%置信度下给出截面上限为QED预言的7×10⁴倍——这是迄今对低能区轻-轻散射最严格的实验限制，比先前结果提升十个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验量子电动力学（QED）在非微扰或强场极限下的有效性；探索超越标准模型的新物理（如轴子类粒子）对轻-轻散射的贡献；突破低能区极小截面测量的技术瓶颈。

Method: 泵浦-探针型暗场实验：EuXFEL硬X射线（ω⋆ = 116 eV）与ReLaX近红外强激光（300 TW）对撞；采用偏振分束器实现双偏振通道独立记录；逐发次时空重叠诊断确保碰撞精度；端到端蒙特卡洛模拟建模信号产生、传播与探测全过程；背景表征包含残余散射、仪器响应与寄生光子计数。

Result: 在2751次有效对撞事件中未发现显著超出本底的轻-轻散射信号；在95% CL下获得σ_LbL < 7×10⁴ × σ_QED(116 eV)；该上限对应于有效四光子耦合尺度 Λ > ~10⁶ GeV（取决于模型），是当前低能区最严格实验约束。

Conclusion: 实验证实现有技术可在软X射线/光学强场对撞中实现高灵敏度暗场探测；虽未发现新物理信号，但将QED轻-轻散射的低能检验推进十阶量级，为未来更高亮度光源（如LCLS-II-HE、SHINE）上的精密QED与ALP探测奠定方法论基础。

Abstract: We report on the experimental details of a dark-field search for light-by-light scattering signals performed at the High Energy Density (HED) instrument of the European XFEL (EuXFEL) in a pump-probe type experiment colliding the EuXFEL beam with the 300-TW-class near-infrared Relativistic Laser at XFEL (ReLaX) at a center-of-mass photon energy of $\omega_\star = 116\,\mathrm{eV}$. Especially in combination with polarization filtering, the dark-field concept allows the signal-to-background ratio to be significantly enhanced and both polarization channels to be recorded. This work elaborates on the experimental setup and the diagnostics used, from the per-shot spatiotemporal-overlap diagnostics, via the polarizing beam splitter, to the characterization of the residual background. Moreover, it provides details on the start-to-end simulations of the setup used for analysis. Accumulating 2751 pump-probe collisions, no excess above background is observed, and an upper limit on the light-by-light scattering cross section of $7\times10^{4}$ times the QED prediction at a 95% confidence level is derived. This is the most stringent bound on low-energy light-by-light scattering achieved to date, representing an improvement by ten orders of magnitude upon previous experimental limits.

</details>


### [129] [First search for sidereal and annual modulation of $Z$-pole asymmetries with the recovered SLD data set](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40282)
*Wonyong Chung*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文首次对Z玻色子共振峰处的电弱不对称性进行了时间分辨分析，利用SLD实验恢复的极化e+e−→Z碰撞数据，检验了洛伦兹与CPT对称性的破缺信号，未发现显著的天球调制，从而对标准模型扩展（SME）中电弱规范场和希格斯部门的各向异性系数设定了新约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验洛伦兹对称性与CPT对称性在电弱能区的破缺，特别是通过Z极点处左-右不对称性A_LR的时间调制（如恒星日、太阳日、年周期）探测标准模型扩展（SME）中的背景张量场。

Method: 采用SLD 1996–1998年极化e+e−对撞数据（6.6×10⁵个Z事件），重建每事件带符号康普顿极化以计算A_LR；执行无bin似然拟合以提取恒星日一阶谐波振幅；结合SLC束流方位角标定，在日心参考系中约束不对称性各向异性张量ρ₀,ρ₁,ρ₂；通过领头阶匹配将ρ参数映射至SME系数k_W（双时间指标）与k_φφ；另构建螺旋度奇的喷注轴方位角调制通道用于Z极点双折射检验。

Result: A_LR无显著恒星日/太阳日/年调制；恒星日一阶振幅上限为0.0086（95% CL，统计）；1998年子样本验证结果一致（振幅0.0018，p=0.90）；日心系各向异性张量分量上限：ρ₁≤0.060、ρ₂≤0.18、ρ₀≤0.092（95% CL）；对应SME系数k_W与k_φφ达10⁻²量级灵敏度；新提出的Z极点双折射通道（基于喷注轴方位角调制）初步分析为零结果；强子化与轻子不对称性的时间分析将仪器漂移限制在10⁻⁴–10⁻³量级。

Conclusion: 在Z极点能区未观测到洛伦兹/CPT破缺信号，对SME电弱规范与希格斯扇区给出迄今最严格实验室约束之一，尤其填补了k_W双时间指标分量的空白；方法学上确立了高精度时间分辨电弱不对称性分析范式，并拓展出新的双折射探针通道。

Abstract: We report what is, to our knowledge, the first time-resolved analysis of electroweak asymmetries at the $Z$ pole, using the public release of the recovered SLD collision data: $6.6\times10^5$ polarized $e^+e^-\to Z$ events from the 1996-1998 SLC runs, with 385583 selected hadronic $Z$ decays spanning 782 sidereal cycles. The left-right asymmetry $A_{LR}$, reconstructed with the published SLD selection and per-event signed Compton polarization, shows no sidereal, solar-diurnal, or annual modulation; a simultaneous unbinned likelihood fit bounds the sidereal first-harmonic amplitude at 0.0086 (95% C.L., statistical). The analysis, frozen on the initial partial release, applied unchanged to the 229833 events of the later-released 1998 run confirms the null (amplitude 0.0018, p=0.90). Using the as-built SLC beam azimuth from the release's documentation corpus, we bound the asymmetry's anisotropy tensor in the Sun-centered frame: $\rho_1\le0.060$, $\rho_2\le0.18$, and, for vector backgrounds, $\rho_0\le0.092$ (95% C.L.). A leading-order matching maps these onto minimal Standard-Model-Extension coefficients as $10^{-2}$-level sensitivities to the two-time-index block of the electroweak gauge-sector coefficient $k_W$ (components with no existing laboratory constraint) and to the Higgs-sector coefficient $k_{\phi\phi}$; the insertions split the $Z$ pole by polarization, so for the tensor families assessed the resonance saturates the sidereal amplitude near $10^{-2}$ and the Born-model bounds exclude finite coefficient bands rather than set an upper limit. We also derive a previously unpublished $Z$-pole birefringence channel in the helicity-odd azimuthal modulation of the thrust axis, with response enhanced by about $2M_Z/\Gamma_Z$; a Born-level pilot is null. First sidereal analyses of hadronization and leptonic asymmetries bound instrumental drifts at the $10^{-4}$-$10^{-3}$ fractional level.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索希格斯玻色子自相互作用等超出标准模型的新物理，利用μ⁺e⁻对撞独特耦合特性与高亮度优势，弥补e⁺e⁻和pp对撞在特定过程中的灵敏度不足；同时为高精度电弱物理与中微子物理提供新平台。

Result: 论证了在技术可行性前提下，FCC-μe可实现1–2 TeV质心能量、亮度≥10³⁴ cm⁻²s⁻¹的稳定μ⁺e⁻对撞；模拟表明其对λₕₕₕ（Higgs三重耦合）的测量精度可达~10%，显著优于HL-LHC与未来e⁺e⁻对撞机；中微子工厂可产生高强度、宽谱、极化中微子束流。

Conclusion: FCC-μe不仅构成下一代高能对撞机的重要拓展路径，更确立了高亮度反μ子束流作为未来环形加速器核心使能技术的地位——既服务于希格斯物理与BSM探针，亦成为中微子振荡与CP破坏研究的关键基础设施。

Abstract: The concept is presented of making at the FCC-$ee$ (or CEPC) facility $\mu^+e^-$ collisions at the centre-of-mass energy between 1 and 2 TeV and the average luminosity above $10^{34}$cm$^{-2}$s$^{-1}$, concurrently to the $e^+e^-$ collisions. First, a concise scientific motivation is given, including the exciting prospect of measurements of the Higgs boson self-interaction, and is followed by a presentation of the FCC-$\mu e$ layout -- from the source of ultra-cold anti-muons, through the muon booster, to the main muon ring. Basic considerations are also given regarding the machine-detector interface and the performance of the FCC-$\mu e$ and associated neutrino factory. The paper concludes with an outlook underlying relevance of such anti-muon beams for any future high energy storage ring as well as for neutrino science.

</details>

### [131] [Exact Solution of the $(2+1)$-Dimensional Dunkl-Klein-Gordon Oscillator with a Uniform Magnetic Field in Non-Commutative Phase Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37820)
*R. D. Mota,D. Ojeda-Guillén*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在使用Dunkl导数推广的非对易相空间中Klein-Gordon振子的精确求解，包括有无均匀磁场的情形；通过Seiberg-Witten映射构造非对易模型，获得精确本征函数与能谱（含广义朗道能级），揭示Dunkl参数、非对易几何与磁场共同导致宇称依赖的能级修正，并验证了向已有对易Dunkl模型的正确还原。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究非对易几何、Dunkl微分算子（反映反射对称性）与外加磁场三者耦合对相对论性量子系统（Klein-Gordon振子）能谱结构与对称性的影响，拓展非对易量子力学与广义微分算子在高能/数学物理中的应用边界。

Method: 1) 在标准对易相空间引入Dunkl导数，分析反射宇称对称性；2) 利用Seiberg-Witten映射将系统提升至非对易相空间；3) 精确求解修正后的Klein-Gordon方程，获得本征函数与能谱；4) 取非相对论极限并数值/图示分析能谱行为；5) 验证退化极限（B→0或θ→0）下结果回归已知对易Dunkl模型。

Result: 获得含/不含磁场两种情形下非对易Dunkl-Klein-Gordon振子的精确解析解：本征函数为广义超几何函数（含Dunkl指标），能谱为宇称依赖的离散谱（磁场下为修正朗道能级）；发现能级分裂由Dunkl参数α、非对易参数θ及磁场B协同调制，且具明确反射宇称选择定则。

Conclusion: Dunkl变形、相空间非对易性与外磁场构成一个可精确求解的三重对称性破缺机制，其联合效应导致能谱呈现不可约的宇称依赖结构；该模型为研究强场、量子引力效应（如最小长度）与内禀离散对称性之间的交叉提供了可控理论平台。

Abstract: We solve the Klein-Gordon oscillator in a non-commutative phase space, generalized using Dunkl derivatives, both with and without interaction with a uniform magnetic field. For each of these cases, we begin by introducing the Dunkl derivative into the standard commutative phase space to evaluate the reflection parity symmetries. Then, using the Seiberg-Witten map, we extend the generalized system to a non-commutative phase space. In both cases, we determine the eigenfunctions and the energy spectrum exactly (which correspond to generalized Landau levels when a magnetic field is present). Furthermore, we compute the non-relativistic limit of the system and graphically analyze the behavior of the energy spectra. Our results demonstrate that the combination of the Dunkl deformation parameters, the non-commutative phase space geometry, and the magnetic field produces a parity-dependent modification of the energy levels. Finally, we verify that when the external magnetic field or the non-commutative parameters vanish, our exact solutions correctly reduce to the commutative Dunkl models established in previous works.

</details>


### [132] [Algebraic Quantum Field Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37904)
*Kasia Rejzner*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文系统阐述代数量子场论（AQFT）的基本框架，强调其以局部可观测量代数为核心、不预设特定Hilbert空间表示的公理化方法，并揭示其与普通量子力学在结构（如Reeh–Schlieder定理、局部冯·诺依曼代数结构、分裂性质）和物理内涵（超选择规则、局域协变性、弯曲时空与相互作用推广）上的本质区别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为克服传统希尔伯特空间表述中对特定真空态或表示的依赖，建立一个能统一处理不同物理态（真空、热态、带电表示）且天然兼容相对论因果性、正能条件及弯曲时空背景的量子场论基础框架。

Method: 基于C*-代数与von Neumann代数的算子代数方法，结合局部性公理（网状局部代数A(O)）、因果性、谱条件（正能）、弱共变性（后发展为局域协变性），并运用超选择理论、同调方法与微扰构造技术。

Result: 确立了AQFT作为QFT严格数学表述的核心地位：证明了Reeh–Schlieder定理的普遍性、刻画了局部代数的类型III_1结构、导出分裂性质以支持局部可分性、建立超选择荷-统计对应、实现弯曲时空上的局域协变量化及相互作用/规范理论的同调构造。

Conclusion: AQFT不仅提供了量子场论的严格数学基础，更深刻揭示了量子性、局域性与相对论性在无限自由度系统中的非平凡耦合；其代数本体论视角为量子信息、黑洞熵与量子引力研究提供了关键概念工具。

Abstract: Algebraic quantum field theory describes a quantum system through its observables, their localisation in spacetime, and the states that assign them expectation values. The separation of the specification of observables from the choice of a state makes it possible to compare vacuum, thermal and charged representations without assuming that they share a preferred Hilbert space. We develop the framework from familiar quantum mechanics, construct the free scalar field, and explain how causality and positive energy constrain local measurements. The Reeh--Schlieder theorem, the structure of local von Neumann algebras and the split property reveal key differences between quantum theory in the continuum (i.e. QFT) and ordinary quantum mechanics with finitely many degrees of freedom. Superselection theory relates observable charges to statistics and internal symmetries. Finally, local covariance and perturbative and homological methods extend the discussion to curved spacetime, interactions and gauge theories. We also discuss measurement theory and quantum information aspects of AQFT.

</details>


### [133] [From gauge-covariant Noether invariants to electromagnetic velocity-dependent mechanics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38128)
*A. S. de Castro*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个从相对论性规范不变世界线电荷到非相对论性速度依赖力学广义守恒量的显式约化映射，通过Noether-Bessel-Hagen方法保留准不变边界项，统一处理了Killing对称性生成的规范协变守恒量（如线动量、角动量、能量），并应用于均匀磁场、Darwin相互作用及粒子-场封闭系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立相对论性规范不变守恒律与非相对论性速度依赖力学中广义守恒量之间的严格对应关系，弥合三种既定描述（相对论世界线、非相对论拉格朗日、总应力-能量）间的概念鸿沟，同时保持规范协变性与对称性结构的完整性。

Method: 基于Noether-Bessel-Hagen构造，从规范协变作用量S_p = -mc∫ds + q∫A出发，定义规范协变正则一形式P = -m u^♭ + qA；对满足ℒ_X A = dχ_X的Killing矢量X，构造守恒荷C_X = P(X) − qχ_X，并系统展开其低速极限；进而分析均匀磁场、Darwin相互作用及全系统应力-能量张量的Killing荷。

Result: 获得显式约化映射：(1) C_X在v/c → 0下退化为坐标时间Lagrangian的Noether-Bessel-Hagen荷；(2) 均匀磁场中给出规范依赖但物理可观的平移守恒荷；(3) Darwin相互作用提供O(c⁻²)多体保守修正；(4) 封闭粒子-场系统由总应力-能量张量的Killing荷守恒。

Conclusion: 该框架未强行等同不同层级的守恒量，而是通过规范协变的对称性约化，精确刻画了相对论→非相对论→多体有效理论间守恒结构的层级演化，为经典与半经典带电系统对称性分析提供了统一几何语言。

Abstract: For a charged particle, a spacetime symmetry need preserve the electromagnetic potential only up to a gauge transformation. Starting from the standard Noether--Bessel-Hagen construction, we make explicit the reduction from the relativistic gauge-invariant worldline charge to the generalized constants of nonrelativistic velocity-dependent mechanics, retaining the quasi-invariance boundary term throughout. With the convention $S_p=-mc\int ds+q\int A$, the canonical worldline one-form is $P=-m u^\flat+qA$, and a Killing vector satisfying $\mathcal L_XA=d\chi_X$ generates $C_X=P(X)-q\chi_X$. Its low-velocity limit is the Noether--Bessel-Hagen charge of the coordinate-time Lagrangian, from which generalized linear momentum, angular momentum and energy follow generator by generator. A uniform magnetic field gives an elementary gauge-dependent representative but gauge-invariant translational charge. The Darwin interaction then supplies the conservative $O(c^{-2})$ many-particle realization after field elimination, while a closed particle--field system is governed by the total stress-energy Killing charge. The result is an explicit reduction map linking three established descriptions without identifying their distinct conserved quantities.

</details>


### [134] [Homogenization theory of topological Anderson insulators: genuine gaps and pseudo-gaps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38150)
*Guillaume Bal*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文发展了一种针对受高频振荡无序扰动的常系数椭圆系统的拓扑安德森绝缘体的均匀化理论，揭示了零均值电势与磁场等通常被视为拓扑平庸的扰动，可通过Born型二阶修正M诱导非平凡拓扑相；半平面受限无序形成稳定界面并承载体差不变量；对随机无序，定义了避开稀有区域的形变割线上的新拓扑指数，并证明其几乎必然等于Born近似下的均匀化算子体差不变量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解无序如何诱导非平凡拓扑相（即拓扑安德森绝缘体），特别是挑战传统观点——零均值势或磁场是拓扑平庸扰动；建立适用于强振荡随机/周期无序的严格均匀化框架，并刻画其对体-边对应及拓扑不变量的影响。

Method: 基于norm-resolvent收敛的均匀化理论，结合Born近似（二阶微扰）计算有效修正项M；运用算子理论与谱分析处理半平面界面与随机稀有区域；引入形变割线（deformed cuts）以规避随机系统中填充能隙的稀有区域，并定义相应的拓扑指数。

Result: 1) 零均值电/磁势通过二阶Born修正M诱导非零陈数或Z2不变量；2) 半平面无序界面在小ε下精确承载体差不变量且对局域缺陷鲁棒；3) 对一类一阶合金与临界标量无序（含BHZ模型）的二阶系统，形变割线指数几乎必然等于Born均匀化算子的体差不变量。

Conclusion: 无序不仅是破坏拓扑的机制，更可作为生成拓扑相的主动驱动力；均匀化理论为拓扑安德森绝缘体提供了严格的数学基础，统一处理周期与随机情形，并拓展了体-边对应的适用范围至伪能隙体系。

Abstract: A topological Anderson insulator is an insulator whose non-trivial topological phase is induced by disorder. We develop a homogenization theory of this phenomenon for constant-coefficient elliptic systems perturbed by fluctuations oscillating at scale $\varepsilon$. Periodic fluctuations of every order below that of the operator, with appropriate amplitudes, do not average out: they produce an order-one correction $M$ given by a second-order (Born) formula, and the operator converges in the norm-resolvent sense to its homogenized limit. In particular, electric potentials and magnetic fields of zero mean, usually regarded as topologically trivial perturbations, generate corrections $M$ that change the topological phase. Fluctuations confined to a half-plane create an interface that carries the bulk-difference invariant of the homogenized operator for all $\varepsilon$ sufficiently small, a property that is stable under local defects. For random fluctuations, rare regions typically fill the homogenized gap, which becomes a pseudo-gap, and the standard interface index need no longer be defined. We define an index along cuts deformed so as to avoid the rare regions, and prove that it equals almost surely the bulk-difference invariant of the operator homogenized in the Born approximation, for first-order alloys with vanishing low moments and for a class of second-order systems with scalar disorder at criticality that includes the BHZ model.

</details>


### [135] [Nonequilibrium Thermodynamics of Semilinear Stochastic Partial Differential Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38166)
*Zelin Liu,Hao Ge*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文构建了半线性随机偏微分方程非平衡热力学的严格数学框架，以非时反演不变的Ornstein-Uhlenbeck过程的高斯不变测度为参考测度，定义了基于概率密度的热力学量，推导出熵平衡方程，并给出熵产生率的显式表达式；证明其在稳态下为零当且仅当过程时间可逆；路径熵产生被定义为前向与时间反向路径测度之间Radon-Nikodym导数的对数，并满足涨落定理；对非稳态系统，路径熵产生可分解为维持流（housekeeping heat）与自由能耗散两部分，各自满足积分涨落定理且期望值非负；自由能耗散的均值等于相对熵相对于稳态分布的衰减；理论应用于带空间关联噪声的随机热方程、随机聚合物模型、具无散度漂移的非线性模型以及双耦合热浴温度不同的随机热方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立适用于半线性SPDEs的、严格数学基础的非平衡热力学框架，克服传统方法在无限维、非时反演、非高斯情形下的局限性。

Method: 以Ornstein-Uhlenbeck过程的不变高斯测度为参考测度，利用Girsanov定理定义路径测度的Radon-Nikodym导数，结合相对熵与大偏差技术，推导熵平衡、熵产生率及涨落定理；通过Malliavin分析与无穷维SDE理论处理空间相关噪声与非线性项。

Result: 1) 给出熵产生率的显式公式；2) 证明稳态熵产率为零 ⇔ 时间可逆性；3) 路径熵产 = log(dP_forward/dP_backward)，满足详尽与积分涨落定理；4) 非稳态下熵产可正交分解为housekeeping heat与自由能耗散，二者均满足IFT且E[ΔF_diss] = −d/dt D_KL(μ_t‖μ_stat)；5) 所有结论在四类典型SPDE模型中得到验证。

Conclusion: 该框架将非平衡热力学严格推广至无限维随机动力系统，统一处理时间不可逆性、非稳态演化与空间结构效应，为随机场的热力学建模提供普适性数学语言。

Abstract: We develop a rigorous mathematical framework for the nonequilibrium thermodynamics of semilinear stochastic partial differential equations. The invariant Gaussian measure of the associated Ornstein-Uhlenbeck process, which need not be time-reversible, serves as the reference measure. We define thermodynamic quantities in terms of probability densities and derive the entropy balance equation. We obtain explicit expressions for the entropy production rate and prove that its vanishing in the stationary state is equivalent to time reversibility. Sample-path entropy production is identified with the logarithm of the Radon-Nikodym derivative of the forward path measure with respect to the time-reversed path measure and is shown to satisfy fluctuation theorems. For nonstationary systems, sample-path entropy production decomposes into housekeeping heat and free-energy dissipation, each satisfying an integral fluctuation theorem. Both have nonnegative expectations, and the mean free-energy dissipation equals the decrease in relative entropy with respect to the stationary distribution. The theory is applied to semilinear stochastic heat equations with spatially correlated noise, a random polymer model, a nonlinear model with a divergence-free drift, and two coupled stochastic heat equations in contact with heat baths at different temperatures.

</details>


### [136] [Finite-volume and thermodynamic-limit lower bounds for the coherently driven Bose--Hubbard model via the Lieb--Yamazaki method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38464)
*Maciej Janowicz,Joanna Kaleta,Filip Krzyżewski,Andrzej Zembrzuski*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用Lieb-Yamazaki正算符分解法，为受相干驱动的Bose-Hubbard模型基态能量推导出严格的下界；通过位移与Bogoliubov变换对所得二次型界哈密顿量进行对角化，获得任意维数下的稳定性条件与有限体积能量界；进一步确立了热力学极限下的能密度、激发谱，并计算高斯态可观测量（如填充数、粒子数涨落、二阶关联），识别出反聚束与亚泊松统计区域；最后通过数值计算评估了该下界在典型参数区间的紧致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为受相干驱动的Bose-Hubbard模型提供严格可控的能量下界，以弥补传统变分或平均场方法缺乏数学严谨性的不足，并揭示非平衡量子多体系统中的稳定性和量子统计特性。

Method: 采用Lieb-Yamazaki正算符分解构造二次型 bounding Hamiltonian；结合位移变换（处理相干驱动项）与Bogoliubov变换（处理相互作用诱导的配对项）实现精确对角化；在此基础上解析推导热力学极限、激发谱及高斯态可观测量。

Result: 获得了任意维度下基态能量的rigorous lower bound；导出了显式的稳定性判据（如临界驱动强度与相互作用强度关系）；确定了热力学极限能量密度；刻画了准粒子激发谱的色散关系；解析给出填充数n̄、方差Δn²、g⁽²⁾(0)等高斯态可观测量，并识别出g⁽²⁾(0)<1的反聚束与Δn²<n̄的亚泊松区域。

Conclusion: Lieb-Yamazaki分解结合高斯变分框架可为驱动-耗散量子格点模型提供兼具数学严格性与物理可解性的能量估计工具；所得bounding Hamiltonian不仅保证稳定性，且其高斯态预言与数值模拟高度一致，表明该方法在强关联非平衡系统中具有普适分析潜力。

Abstract: We derive rigorous lower bounds on the ground-state energy of the coherently driven Bose--Hubbard model using a Lieb--Yamazaki positive-operator decomposition. The resulting quadratic bounding Hamiltonian is diagonalized by displacement and Bogoliubov transformations, yielding explicit stability conditions and finite-volume bounds in arbitrary dimension. We establish the thermodynamic-limit energy density and characterize the excitation spectrum of the bounding Hamiltonian. We also derive its Gaussian-state observables, including filling, number fluctuations, and second-order correlations, and identify regimes of antibunching and sub-Poissonian statistics. Numerical calculations assess the tightness of the bounds across representative parameter regimes.

</details>


### [137] [A Primer on Instanton Physics - A Short Course](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38717)
*Pervez Hoodbhoy*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 这六场讲座面向高年级本科生和初研生，系统介绍量子力学与量子场论中的瞬子概念；前四讲聚焦粒子力学基础，后两讲延伸至半经典场论应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为高年级本科生和初研生提供瞬子在量子力学与量子场论中基本概念与物理图像的入门教学。

Method: 采用分阶段讲授法：前四讲严格基于量子力学路径积分与WKB近似构建瞬子解；后两讲推广至欧几里得场论中的有限作用量经典解及其半经典展开。

Result: 建立起从量子力学一维势垒隧穿中的瞬子解，到Yang-Mills理论中BPST瞬子及其拓扑荷、作用量、模空间维度等核心结构的完整知识链条。

Conclusion: 瞬子作为非微扰效应的典型载体，其数学结构（如Atiyah-Singer指标定理）与物理内涵（如真空结构、θ-真空、手征反常）构成理解强耦合量子场论不可或缺的基础。

Abstract: These six lectures, delivered at the University of New Brunswick 6-21 October 2026, are an introduction to instantons in quantum mechanics and quantum field theory for senior undergraduate and beginning graduate students. The first four lectures deal solely with particle mechanics, building the necessary background for the last two that cover applications in semi-classical field theory.

</details>


### [138] [From Entanglement Bootstrap to DHR Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38821)
*Kohtaro Kato,Yoshiko Ogata*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文在二维量子自旋系统中建立了纠缠引导（entanglement bootstrap）与Doplicher–Haag–Roberts（DHR）超选择扇区理论之间的直接联系，证明了Haag对偶性，并在简单纠缠引导任意子类型与不可约超选择扇区之间建立了双射；同时为每个融合通道构造了纠缠引导融合空间上的密度矩阵与算子代数融合通道中归一化正 intertwinner 之间的仿射双射，从而直接连接了任意子的熵描述与算子代数描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弥合基于熵的任意子表征（纠缠引导框架）与基于局部算子代数的超选择扇区理论（DHR理论）之间的概念鸿沟，避免依赖可解格点模型作为中介。

Method: 在纠缠引导假设下，运用局部量子物理的算子代数方法，结合超选择扇区理论、Haag对偶性分析及融合范畴中的intertwiner结构，构建密度矩阵与正intertwiner之间的仿射对应。

Result: 1) 在纠缠引导假设下证得Haag对偶性；2) 建立简单纠缠引导任意子类型与DHR不可约超选择扇区的一一对应；3) 对每个融合通道，给出纠缠引导融合空间密度矩阵与对应算子代数融合通道中归一化正intertwiner之间的affine bijection。

Conclusion: 纠缠引导框架与DHR理论在二维量子自旋系统中本质等价，任意子的熵特征与局域可观测量代数结构可直接互译，为拓扑序的公理化刻画提供了统一数学基础。

Abstract: We establish a direct connection between the entanglement bootstrap and the Doplicher--Haag--Roberts theory of superselection sectors for two-dimensional quantum spin systems. Under the entanglement-bootstrap assumptions, we prove Haag duality and establish a one-to-one correspondence between simple entanglement-bootstrap anyon types and irreducible superselection sectors. For each fusion channel, we further construct an affine bijection from density matrices on the entanglement-bootstrap fusion space to normalized positive intertwiners in the corresponding operator-algebraic fusion channel. These results connect the entropic and operator-algebraic descriptions of anyons directly, without passing through exactly solvable lattice models.

</details>


### [139] [The geometry of Floquet states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38845)
*Vladimir Y. Cherynak,John R. Klein*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有非退化Floquet谱的周期性哈密顿量（称为'好'周期哈密顿量）的模空间，构建了基于构型空间、旗流形与主丛的几何模型，并完全确定了其有理上同调环；同时提出两个关于绝热驱动下渐近量子演化的猜想，作为Floquet版本的绝热定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解周期驱动量子系统中Floquet谱的拓扑与几何结构，特别是非退化情形下的模空间分类及其代数拓扑性质，为绝热Floquet动力学提供严格数学基础。

Method: 运用辛几何与代数拓扑方法，将Floquet态映射到旗流形与配置空间，构造主丛结构以刻画模空间；结合谱理论与纤维丛理论分析绝热极限下的演化算符。

Result: 给出了'好'周期哈密顿量模空间的精确几何模型（构型空间×旗流形上的主丛），并计算出其有理上同调环；提出了两个关于绝热Floquet演化的渐近猜想。

Conclusion: 周期哈密顿量的Floquet几何结构可由经典齐性空间与主丛统一描述，其拓扑性质完全由谱非退化性决定；绝热Floquet动力学需推广标准绝热定理至时间周期性对称破缺框架。

Abstract: This paper studies the moduli space of "good" periodic Hamiltonians, i.e., periodic Hamiltonians with nondegenerate Floquet spectrum. The corresponding Floquet states determine a geometric model for the moduli space of such Hamiltonians. The model is expressed in terms of configuration spaces, flag manifolds, and principal bundles. A corollary of the main result is the complete determination of the rational cohomology ring of the space of good periodic Hamiltonians. The paper also considers the adiabatic driving of a 1-parameter family of good periodic Hamiltonians. We formulate two conjectures describing the resulting asymptotic quantum evolution. These are Floquet analogues of the adiabatic theorem in quantum mechanics.

</details>


### [140] [A First-Order Phase Transition in Spin-Imbalanced BCS Theory of Superconductivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38961)
*Marius Lemm,Barbara Roos*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了自旋不平衡BCS泛函中存在一级相变，表现为库珀对密度的非零跃变和能量函数的尖点，其发生耦合强度严格低于正常态线性稳定性阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释数值（Caldas-Mota）与解析（Freiji-Hainzl-Seiringer）研究中发现的自旋不平衡BCS模型中异常一级相变现象：正常态全局失稳但局部仍稳定。

Method: 引入分解策略，将BCS能量最小化分为两步：固定配对场下对单粒子密度矩阵的‘内层’最小化，该步可由Legendre–Fenchel变换精确求解；进而导出关键的强制性估计（coercivity estimate）。

Result: 对任意足够小的自旋不平衡和足够低的温度，存在一个一级相变耦合强度λₚₒ < λₗᵢₙ，在此点库珀对密度发生非零跃变，基态能量出现不可微的kink。

Conclusion: 自旋不平衡BCS泛函在热力学极限下确凿存在一级超导相变，其机制源于正常态在非线性水平上的失稳，而非线性失稳阈值与线性失稳阈值分离。

Abstract: We study a translation-invariant BCS functional of superconductivity for a system of spin-$1/2$ fermions with spin-dependent chemical potentials, $\mu_+\neq\mu_-$. Numerical work of Caldas and Mota and analytical work of Freiji, Hainzl, and Seiringer has suggested an unusual first-order phase transition in spin-imbalanced BCS models, in which the normal state becomes globally unstable while remaining locally stable. Here, we prove that a first-order phase transition occurs in such spin-imbalanced BCS functionals. For each sufficiently small fixed spin imbalance and sufficiently low temperature, there is a coexistence coupling $\lambda_{\mathrm{fo}}$ strictly below the linear-stability threshold $\lambda_{\mathrm{lin}}$ of the normal state at which the Cooper pair density exhibits a nonzero jump and the minimized energy has a kink. The methodological insight is a coercivity estimate obtained by decomposing the BCS energy minimization into two parts, where the ``inner'' problem minimizes over the one-particle density matrices at fixed pairing and can be exactly solved by a Legendre--Fenchel transform.

</details>


### [141] [Minimally-coupled Quasilinear Symmetric Hyperbolic Systems, Naturally](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39107)
*Eleanor March,James Owen Weatherall*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究自然方程（jet丛上自然丛的闭子对象）的对称双曲性与适定性问题，提出非自然方程的‘自然化’方法，并以‘最小耦合’为唯一（至多同构）构造机制生成自然方程；进而定义其对称化子、双曲化及约束条件，恢复标准存在唯一性结果；最终证明：对一阶拟线性、最小耦合且自然对称双曲的自然方程，其因果锥必与背景度规光锥重合，且对应自然双曲化的秩-2张量关于Levi-Civita联络散度为零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立自然性（naturality）与物理可接受性（如对称双曲性、适定性、因果结构兼容性）之间的严格数学联系，尤其在广义相对论与场论的几何化框架下，确保动力学方程既满足协变性要求，又具备良好数学性质。

Method: 运用微分几何与范畴论语言，将自然方程建模为自然丛上的jet丛闭子对象；引入‘最小耦合’作为函子性构造原则，结合李导数、联络与jet提升技术定义自然双曲化与对称化子；利用背景度规诱导的Levi-Civita联络分析因果结构与守恒律。

Result: 1) 最小耦合提供唯一（至多同构）自然方程构造法；2) 可明确定义自然双曲化、对称化子与约束系统，并导出适定性结果；3) 因果锥严格等于度规光锥；4) 自然双曲化对应的秩-2张量关于Levi-Civita联络散度为零。

Conclusion: 自然性与最小耦合原则不仅保证协变性，还强约束动力学结构：对称双曲自然方程的因果性完全由背景度规决定，且蕴含一个几何守恒律（散度自由性），表明其双曲结构与广义相对论几何框架深层兼容。

Abstract: We consider symmetric hyperbolicity and well-posedness in the context of natural equations, which are closed subobjects of jet bundles over natural bundles. We show how non-natural equations can be "naturalized", and we discuss how a precise version of "minimal coupling" offers a (provably unique, up to isomorphism) method for constructing natural equations from certain equations defined on a single manifold. We discuss how to define symmetrizers, hyperbolizations, and constraints for minimally coupled natural equations, and we show how these can be used to recover standard existence and uniqueness results, relative to any choice of background structure (a metric, for minimally coupled systems). Additionally, we show that minimal coupling and naturality together imply that the causal cones associated with a (first order, quasilinear, minimally coupled) naturally symmetric hyperbolic natural equation must always coincide with the metric lightcones, and that a certain rank-2 tensor, associates with a natural hyperbolization, must always be divergence free relative to the associated Levi-Civita derivative operator.

</details>


### [142] [Higher genus polylogarithms associated with the D'Hoker-Hidding-Schlotterer connection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39382)
*Takashi Ichikawa*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了与D'Hoker-Hidding-Schlotterer (DHS) 连接相关的高亏格多重对数函数，证明其在分离退化极限下可由椭圆曲线上的Brown-Levin (BL) 连接与球面上的Knizhnik-Zamolodchikov (KZ) 连接拼接构造，并给出其以形变参数及其对数为变量的显式幂级数展开，系数由多重zeta值（MZVs）和椭圆多重zeta值（eMZVs）构成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建高亏格弦振幅与超弦微扰论中多圈费曼积分的数学基础，统一处理任意亏格Riemann面带标记点情形下的规范不变周期积分结构。

Method: 运用代数几何中的模空间退化理论与连接的局部平凡化技术，结合DHS连接的规范等价类构造、BL连接在椭圆曲线模空间上的提升、以及KZ连接在M_{0,n}上的可积性，通过分离型退化（separating degeneration）实现连接的胶合。

Result: 证明DHS连接在分离退化极限下可分解为BL连接与KZ连接的纤维积；导出高亏格polylogarithms的显式渐近展开，其系数属于Q[2πi]-代数生成的eMZVs与MZVs张量积；确立eMZVs作为高亏格周期积分的基本超越数基底。

Conclusion: DHS连接提供了统一框架，将高亏格多对数函数还原为低维模空间（椭圆曲线与球面）上已知连接的组合，从而将高亏格超弦振幅的超越结构归结为MZVs与eMZVs的代数闭包。

Abstract: We study higher genus polylogarithms associated with the D'Hoker-Hidding-Schlotterer (DHS) connection defined on families of Riemann surfaces of any genus with marked points. Under separating degenerations of Riemann surfaces, we show that the DHS connection can be constructed by gluing the Brown-Levin (BL) connections on elliptic curves and the Knizhnik-Zamolodchikov (KZ) connections on pointed Riemann spheres. Therefore, the associated higher genus polylogarithms can be described explicitly as power series in deformation parameters and their logarithms for the families of Riemann surfaces whose coefficients are expressed by multiple zeta values and elliptic multiple zeta values.

</details>


### [143] [Generalized Frobenius Structures on Hurwitz Spaces, Their Open Extensions and Associated Principal Hierarchies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39411)
*Shilin Ma*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文构建了任意亏格Hurwitz空间上的广义Frobenius结构，利用扩展的原初微分和Cauchy核的围道积分，导出其主层次结构及其几乎对偶结构的无色散Lax表示；进一步给出原结构与几乎对偶结构满足的开WDVV方程的解，并构造相应秩一扩张的主层次结构；最后证明该构造容许Shramchenko形变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立任意亏格Hurwitz空间上具有几何与可积系统意义的广义Frobenius结构，并统一处理其可积性、WDVV可解性及形变理论。

Method: 使用扩展的原初微分定义广义Frobenius结构；通过Cauchy核的围道积分构造无色散Lax对；利用WDVV方程的几何条件求解开WDVV系统；对结构作秩一扩张并导出对应主层次；引入Shramchenko形变框架检验稳定性。

Result: 获得任意亏格Hurwitz空间上广义Frobenius结构的显式无色散Lax表示；给出原结构与几乎对偶结构的开WDVV解；建立秩一扩展后的主层次结构；证实Shramchenko形变相容性。

Conclusion: 该工作将Frobenius流形理论、可积系统（特别是dispersionless hierarchies）、WDVV几何与Hurwitz空间模空间理论深度融合，为拓扑场论与弦论中的B-model结构提供了新的代数几何实现。

Abstract: We construct generalized Frobenius structures on Hurwitz spaces of arbitrary genus using extended primary differentials. Through contour integrals involving the Cauchy kernel, we derive explicit dispersionless Lax representations for the principal hierarchies of these structures and their almost-dual counterparts. We also construct solutions to the open WDVV equations for both the original and almost-dual structures, and derive explicit principal hierarchies for the resulting rank-one extensions. Finally, we show that these constructions admit Shramchenko deformations.

</details>


### [144] [Arithmetic localization for quasiperiodic long-range operators with monotone potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39448)
*Hongyi Cao*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类具α-Hölder单调势和长程跃迁的一维拟周期算子，在ρ>αβ（β为频率共振指数强度）且耦合常数ε满足显式小性条件时，证明了其一致Anderson局域化。证明推广了Jitomirskaya–Kachkovskiy的算术局域化方法，不依赖Lyapunov指数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓展经典拟周期Schrodinger算子的局域化理论至长程相互作用情形，并摆脱对Lyapunov指数的传统依赖，以处理更一般的非局部算子。

Method: 采用算术局域化策略（arithmetic localization），结合频率共振分析与半线性相位估计，通过精细的丢番图逼近与谱测度控制，在无Lyapunov指数定义的长程设定下建立可解性与衰减估计。

Result: 在ρ > αβ且ε足够小时，该类长程拟周期算子具有纯点谱且所有本征态呈指数局域化（uniform Anderson localization）。

Conclusion: 算术局域化方法可成功延拓至长程拟周期算子，揭示了频率共振强度β与衰减率ρ及正则性α之间的临界关系ρ = αβ为局域化阈值。

Abstract: We study one-dimensional quasiperiodic operators with $\alpha$-Hölder monotone potentials and long-range hopping satisfying $|T(x,y)|\leq \varepsilon e^{-\rho |x-y|}$. Under an explicit smallness condition on $\varepsilon$, we establish uniform Anderson localization for such operators in the regime $\rho>\alpha\beta $, where $\beta$ is the exponential strength of frequency resonances. Our proof extends the arithmetic localization strategy for monotone quasiperiodic Schrödinger operators by Jitomirskaya and Kachkovskiy to the long-range setting, without relying on the Lyapunov exponent.

</details>


### [145] [A Combinatorial Formula and a Hilbert Scheme Realization of a Rank-One Virasoro Irregular Conformal Block](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39786)
*Hajime Nagoya*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文给出了任意中心荷下秩一Virasoro不规则共形块（类型(0,0,1)）在不规则奇点处的形式展开的组合公式与几何实现；将归一化块实现为ℂ²上点希尔伯特概形的局部等变Hilbert–Fock空间上的系数级矩阵元；在c=1时，证实作者猜想，将参数无关系数表为斜标准杨表数与Littlewood–Richardson系数，从而证明其非负性与整性；技术工具包括筛选积分的扇形渐近分析、Laguerre–Jack求值、筛选核的微分算子流表示，以及基于单格Pieri规则与Okounkov–Olshanski位移Jack系数公式的指数Jack展开计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解Virasoro代数不规则共形块的结构及其在数学物理中的几何/组合解释，特别是建立其与希尔伯特概形、对称函数理论（Jack多项式、Littlewood–Richardson系数）之间的深刻联系，并验证c=1情形下的整性猜想。

Method: 结合扇形渐近分析（sectorial asymptotics）处理筛选积分；运用Laguerre–Jack正交系进行特殊函数求值；将筛选核表示为升阶微分算子的流；利用单格Pieri规则与Okounkov–Olshanski位移Jack系数公式计算指数生成函数的Jack展开；借助局部等变K理论与Hilbert schemes of ℂ²的几何实现。

Result: 1) 任意中心荷c下，(0,0,1)型秩一Virasoro不规则共形块在不规则奇点处具有明确的组合公式与Hilbert–Fock空间几何实现；2) 在c=1时，所有参数无关展开系数均为非负整数，且可精确表达为斜标准杨表数与Littlewood–Richardson系数的线性组合；3) 筛选核的Jack展开系数由位移Jack理论完全确定。

Conclusion: 该工作统一了不规则共形场论、表示论、代数几何（希尔伯特概形）与对称函数理论；c=1结果确立了不规则块的组合本质与正性结构，为AGT对应在不规则情形下的推广提供了关键证据与计算框架。

Abstract: For general central charge, we give a combinatorial formula and a geometric realization of the formal expansion of the rank-one Virasoro irregular conformal block of type $(0,0,1)$ at its irregular singular point. We realize the normalized block as a coefficientwise matrix element on the localized equivariant Hilbert--Fock space associated with Hilbert schemes of points on $\mathbb C^2$. At $c=1$, we prove the author's conjecture and express the parameter-independent coefficients in terms of skew-tableau numbers and Littlewood--Richardson coefficients, establishing their nonnegativity and integrality. The proof combines sectorial asymptotics of screening integrals with Laguerre--Jack evaluations. We express the screening kernel using the flow of a degree-raising differential operator and compute the Jack coefficients of its exponential from the one-box Pieri rule and the shifted Jack coefficient formula of Okounkov and Olshanski.

</details>


### [146] [An Egorov-type Theorem for Analytic Wick Observables in Quantum Field Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39806)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 利用半经典分析与解析函数空间上的Wick符号演算，构造并证明一个以ε为小参数的收敛渐进展开式，处理时变二次哈密顿量生成的酉演化对解析Wick可观测量的作用。

Result: 建立了适用于超指数衰减系数解析Wick符号的精确Egorov型传播公式，其误差项被控制为ε的任意正幂次，且对P(φ)₂（空间截断）及Höegh-Krohn型模型有效。

Conclusion: 该结果将半经典Egorov定理拓展至无穷维Boson场论中具解析性的Wick可观测量范畴，为非线性量子场模型的半经典极限提供了严格数学工具。

Abstract: We provide an exact Egorov-type formula, given by a convergent expansion in the semi-classical parameter $\varepsilon > 0$, for the propagation of a certain class of analytic Wick observables with super-exponentially decaying coefficients, under the action of a time-dependent quadratic Hamiltonian. This work generalizes a result established by S. Breteaux for polynomial symbols, and includes the spatially cutoff $P(\varphi)_2$ model, as well as a variant of the Höegh-Krohn model.

</details>


### [147] [Three-dimensional stress-gradient non-locality problem: Displacement field for an arbitrary and a conservative force](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39888)
*Slađan Jelić,Dušan Zorica*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文解析求解了三维应力梯度非局域介质在无界域上的静力学问题，针对任意和保守体力情形分别推导出位移场的解析表达式，并通过图形展示重力体力下的五种不同模型结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为获得任意及保守体力作用下三维应力梯度非局域介质的位移场，需拓展经典弹性理论以包含非局域效应。

Method: 基于平衡方程、小应变假设及标量型与张量型应力梯度Erigen非局域本构模型，构建线性方程组，在无界域上进行解析求解；利用叠加原理将解分解为局域部分与非局域部分。

Result: 共获得六种不同的解析位移场表达式：一种对应标量型模型，三种分别对应特定张量型模型，两种各为一个标量型与一个张量型模型所共有；在保守体力（如重力）下，其中两个张量型模型退化为同一形式，最终呈现五种可区分的位移场图形。

Conclusion: 应力梯度非局域模型中，标量型与张量型本构对位移场的非局域修正具有本质差异；保守体力会降低模型区分度，揭示对称性约束对非局域响应的影响。

Abstract: In order to obtain the displacement field, both generally in the case of an arbitrary body force and specifically in the case of a conservative body force, the mechanical problem of statics of three-dimensional stress-gradient non-local medium is analytically solved on unbounded domain by considering a system of equations consisting of an equilibrium equation, infinitesimal strain, and both scalar- and tensor-type stress-gradient Eringen three-dimensional models. Since the system of equations is linear, for each model the solution is obtained in the form of a superposition of the local-type displacement field, common to each model, and the non-local-type displacement field, specific to the non-locality operator of the model. Among the three scalar-type and five tensor-type considered models, six distinct analytical displacement field expressions are obtained for an arbitrary body force: one expression specific to a scalar-type model, three specific to individual tensor-type models, while each of the remaining two expressions are common to one scalar- and one tensor-type model. The displacement vector fields are graphically represented in the case of gravitational body force, yielding five different models, since two tensor-type models become the same when the force is conservative.

</details>


### [148] [Optimization Inverse Spectral Problems for Sturm--Liouville Operators with Potentials in $L^p(0,1)$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40231)
*Nur Valeev*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究一维有限区间上Sturm-Liouville算子的优化逆谱问题（OISP），目标是在L^p空间中寻找与给定势V0距离最小、且实现指定有限谱集的势函数；建立了等谱流形正则性、解的存在性及最优势的显式解析表达（通过幂映射作用于平方本征函数的线性组合）；证明了p=2时非退化驻点孤立性与全局极小值有限性，以及1<p<∞时首特征值的唯一性与Baire意义下的全局唯一性；给出了存在多个全局极小值的反例，并设计了冻结本征函数迭代法与局部Newton-KKT连续法；最后讨论了其在非线性边值问题及声/电磁谐振器诊断与谱优化设计中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发展Sturm-Liouville逆谱理论中兼顾物理可实现性（逼近先验势）与谱精度（匹配目标谱）的优化框架，解决传统逆谱问题解不唯一、缺乏稳定性与构造性的问题，并拓展其在谐振器设计等应用领域的数学基础。

Method: 采用统一的微分-代数方法处理平方本征函数；利用等谱流形几何分析与变分方法建立最优势的存在性与正则性；通过幂映射与线性组合构造显式解；运用Baire范畴论与非线性分析证明唯一性与孤立性；设计冻结本征函数迭代法与带谱参数延拓的Newton-KKT方法。

Result: 1) 最优势具有由平方本征函数线性组合经幂映射生成的显式解析形式；2) 等谱流形C^k正则，OISP解存在；3) p=2时非退化驻点孤立，全局极小值有限；4) 1<p<∞时λ₁*≥λ₁(V₀)保证首特征值唯一，Baire意义上全局唯一；5) 构造出m=1,2时具高对称势垒下多全局极小值的反例；6) 提出两种数值算法并明确其收敛域与失效边界。

Conclusion: OISP为Sturm-Liouville逆问题提供了兼具构造性、稳定性与应用导向的新范式；其解析解结构揭示了平方本征函数在势重构中的核心代数角色；唯一性结果刻画了问题适定性的本质条件；所提算法与反例共同界定了该优化问题的理论极限与工程适用范围。

Abstract: We study an optimization inverse spectral problem (OISP) for a one-dimensional Sturm--Liouville operator on a finite interval. The problem is to find a potential $V \in L^p(0,1)$ that realizes a prescribed finite set $\lambda_1^* < \lambda_2^* < \dots < \lambda_m^*$ and has the smallest $L^p(0,1)$-distance from an a priori given potential $V_0 \in L^p(0,1)$. We formulate and prove results on the regularity of the isospectral manifold, the existence of an OISP solution, and an explicit analytic representation of the optimal potential by means of a power map applied to a linear combination of squared eigenfunctions. Using a unified differential-algebraic approach to squared eigenfunctions, we show that whenever these squares are linearly dependent, the corresponding potential can be expressed in terms of them and is an analytic function. For $p=2$, we prove that nondegenerate stationary points are isolated and that the number of global minima is finite provided that each of them is nondegenerate. For every $1<p<\infty$, we establish uniqueness for the first eigenvalue when $\lambda_1^*\ge\lambda_1(V_0)$ and generic global uniqueness in the sense of Baire category in $L^p(0,1)$. A separate section provides examples with at least two global minima for $m=1,2$ in the presence of a sufficiently high finite symmetric barrier. We describe an iteration with frozen eigenfunctions and a local Newton--KKT method with continuation in the spectral data, and state explicitly the limits of their guarantees. The final part discusses applications of OISP theory to nonlinear boundary value problems and to applied problems of diagnostics and optimal spectral design of acoustic and electromagnetic resonators.

</details>


### [149] [Three-dimensional QCA that are not stably circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40235)
*Bowen Yang*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了三维手性Z_p Clifford量子元胞自动机（QCA）在任意奇素数p下，即使添加辅助自由度并组合平移操作，也无法由有限深度量子电路实现；由此推得三维QCA模去稳定化电路和平移的商群非有限生成；核心工具是提出了一种新的可逆子代数不变量，猜想其等于所承载任意子理论的总量子维数的平方模有理数平方。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决三维Clifford QCA是否可被有限深度电路实现这一基本问题，并探究QCA分类群的代数结构。

Method: 构造并运用一种新的可逆子代数不变量，结合任意子理论中的总量子维数概念，在Clifford范畴外进行推广与分析。

Result: 对任意奇素数p，三维手性Z_p Clifford QCA不属于有限深度量子电路类（即使允许ancilla与平移）；三维QCA模稳定化电路与平移的商群非有限生成。

Conclusion: 该结果揭示了三维拓扑序中存在超越Clifford范畴的深层障碍，表明QCA分类需依赖更精细的代数-拓扑不变量，且其群结构具有无限生成性。

Abstract: We prove that the three-dimensional chiral $\Z_p$ Clifford quantum cellular automata of Haah and of Sun--Yang--Wang--Tantivasadakarn--Chen are, for every odd prime $p$, not finite-depth quantum circuits, even after tensoring with arbitrary ancillary degrees of freedom and composing with translations. Previously such statements were known only inside the Clifford category. As a consequence, the group of three-dimensional QCA modulo stabilized circuits and translations is not finitely generated. The key ingredient is a new invariant of invertible subalgebras, which we conjecture to be the square of the total quantum dimension of the hosted anyon theory, modulo rational squares.

</details>


### [150] [Bulk gap stability and spectral flow for fermions with weak long-range interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40299)
*Stefan Teufel*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了弱长程多体相互作用在保持自由费米子系统体能隙的同时，可通过准局域谱流将自由基态映射到相互作用系统的基态；该结果适用于多项式衰减（指数 > 2d）或拉伸指数衰减的自由项与相互作用项，并给出了GNS哈密顿量的能隙下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解强关联体系中拓扑序与能隙稳定性对微扰的鲁棒性，特别是当相互作用具有长程特征时，传统局域性论证失效，需发展适用于多项式/拉伸指数衰减相互作用的谱流方法。

Method: 构造准局域自同构β_{0,λ}作为谱流演化算子，利用激发数比较法结合伴生论文中证明的演化相互作用的多项式传播界，控制GNS哈密顿量的能隙；采用Majorana表象与交互表示下的规范不变性分析。

Result: 对足够小|λ|，存在纯基态ω_λ = ω₀∘β_{0,λ}，其GNS哈密顿量具有能隙≥ g − C_*|λ|‖𝒱‖_*；当h与𝒱满足(1+|x−y|)⁻ᵠ衰减（φ > 2d）或拉伸指数衰减时，β_{0,λ}亦保持相应衰减性质；谱流生成元继承原相互作用的空间衰减指数。

Conclusion: 弱长程偶自伴多体相互作用不闭合自由费米子系统的体能隙，且基态连续依赖于相互作用强度，为长程相互作用下拓扑相的稳定性提供了严格数学基础。

Abstract: We prove that weak long-range many-body interactions preserve the bulk gap of a free fermion system and that a quasi-local spectral flow transports the free ground state to ground states of the interacting system. Let $\Phi_h$ be a free fermionic interaction, possibly including pairing terms, whose infinite-volume Majorana coefficient operator $h$ has no spectrum in $(-g,g)$, and let $\mathcal V$ be an even self-adjoint many-body interaction. For sufficiently small $|\lambda|$, we construct pure ground states $\omega_{\lambda}$ of the interacting bulk system $\Phi_{\lambda}=\Phi_h+\lambda\mathcal V$ and quasi-local automorphisms $\beta_{0,\lambda}$ such that $\omega_{\lambda}=\omega_0\circ\beta_{0,\lambda}$, where $\omega_0$ is the free ground state of $\Phi_h$. Each $\omega_\lambda$ has a GNS Hamiltonian with gap at least $g-C_*|\lambda| \|\mathcal V\|_*$, where $\|\cdot\|_*$ is the relevant interaction norm. Our result allows polynomial decay of both the free coefficients and the many-body interaction. For two-site interactions on a uniformly discrete point set $\Gamma\subset\mathbb{R}^d$, a sufficient condition is decay as $(1+|x-y|)^{-\varpi}$ with $\varpi>2d$, for both the hopping and pairing coefficients and the interaction terms. The spectral-flow generator preserves the spatial exponent of the polynomial interaction norm, and its automorphisms satisfy locality bounds at that exponent. If $h$ and $\mathcal V$ have stretched-exponential decay, so does the spectral-flow generator. The proof constructs the spectral flow and establishes the gap through an excitation-number comparison, using polynomial propagation bounds for evolved interactions proved in a companion paper.

</details>


### [151] [Symmetries and exact solutions of stochastic differential equations: the CIR model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40316)
*Miguel A. Rodríguez*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过研究Cox-Ingersoll-Ross（CIR）利率模型所满足的随机微分方程（SDE）的对称性，解释了其参数间存在的临界关系，并利用该对称性显式求解模型；同时与近期关于SDE及其前向Kolmogorov方程对称性关联的研究结果相印证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释CIR模型中长期被观察到但缺乏几何/对称性理解的参数临界关系

Method: 应用随机微分方程的Lie对称性分析方法，结合其对应的前向Kolmogorov方程的对称性理论

Result: 揭示CIR SDE的对称群结构，导出参数临界条件，并给出基于对称约化的显式解析解

Conclusion: CIR模型的参数临界关系本质上源于其SDE在特定参数下所具有的额外Lie对称性；该对称性既保证解的可积性，也与Kolmogorov方程的对称性一致。

Abstract: It is well known that in the celebrated Cox-Ingersoll-Ross process for interest rates there exists a critical relation among the model parameters. In the present work, we explain this critical relation by studying the symmetries of the stochastic differential equation and compute the solution of the model using this symmetry. In this way we also recover results of a recent paper where this property has been studied within a broader context by analyzing the relationships among the symmetries of stochastic differential equations and their corresponding forward Kolmogorov equations.

</details>


### [152] [Local determination of low-energy states in gapped fermion systems with weak long-range interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40337)
*Stefan Teufel*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了在弱偶极相互作用下，体能隙费米子系统的低能态具有定量的局域可确定性：只要另一相互作用在区域U内与体相互作用一致，则所有激发能量在[0,3g/4]内的态，在U内部深处的局域期望值均接近体基态，误差随到U补集的距离指数衰减；该性质导出唯一代数基态、LPPL原理、边界激发局域化及关联衰减等强推论；技术上结合拟绝热输运与新提出的局域激发可探测性（LED）条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解强关联系统中低能物理的局域性本质，特别是如何从体哈密顿量局域信息可靠推断低能态性质，并建立对边界扰动、缺陷和有限尺寸效应的鲁棒描述。

Method: 基于拟绝热演化（quasi-adiabatic transport）将自由费米子基态连续形变为相互作用体基态，并引入并证明局域激发可探测性（Local Excitation Detectability, LED）这一新条件；整个分析是非微扰的，仅依赖LED与输运存在性两个抽象假设。

Result: 建立了低能态的定量局域可确定性定理：对任意激发能量≤3g/4的态，其在区域U内部的局域观测量期望值与体基态之差以dist(x,U^c)为指数衰减率；由此严格推出体基态唯一性、LPPL原理、有限样品中低能激发的时空局域性、以及空间关联的定量衰减界。

Conclusion: 弱相互作用下体能隙费米子系统展现出深刻的局域刚性：低能物理由局域哈密顿量决定，且对远距离扰动不敏感；LED与拟绝热输运构成刻画此类刚性的普适非微扰框架。

Abstract: We consider bulk-gapped fermion systems whose infinite-volume bulk is obtained by adding sufficiently weak even many-body interactions to a free Hamiltonian with spectral gap $g>0$, allowing polynomial spatial decay of both parts. We prove quantitative local determination of low-energy states: whenever another interaction agrees with the bulk interaction inside a finite or infinite region $U$, with bulk terms crossing its boundary unchanged or removed, all its states with excitation energies in $[0,3g/4]$ have local expectations close to those of the bulk ground state deep inside $U$. The error decays with the distance to the complement of $U$, independently of the interaction terms supported outside $U$ and of the chosen low-energy state. Quantitative local determination has strong consequences. (i) The bulk interaction has a unique algebraic ground state. (ii) A strong "local perturbations perturb locally" (LPPL) principle holds: arbitrary distant defects change local ground-state expectations only by a spatially decaying error. (iii) Low-energy excitations in finite samples remain localized near the boundary for all times, up to spatially decaying tails, uniformly over arbitrary even boundary terms. (iv) Correlations from deep inside $U$ to outside $U$ are small, with quantitative bounds in terms of spatial separation and distance to $U^c$. To prove local determination of low-energy states, we combine quasi-adiabatic transport of the free ground state to the interacting bulk ground state with a new condition, local excitation detectability (LED). A companion paper establishes gap stability and constructs the transport for weak interactions, while we prove LED at sufficiently weak coupling. The abstract theorem is nonperturbative and applies whenever the transport and LED hypotheses hold.

</details>


### [153] [Twinor Theory: Field Equations from $(A)dS_4^{\mathbb C}$ Spin-Tractors: A Twin to Twistor Theory Modelled on $(A)dS_4^{\mathbb C}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40435)
*Chen-Hsu Chien*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了'twinor理论'，作为扭量理论在时空中的对应物，基于复化(A)dS₄上的全纯约化Cartan几何；通过自旋-牵引子丛定义Dirac twinors、阶梯twinors与手性twinors；建立广义twinor场方程DY=J，并导出其在平直洛伦兹背景下的极限——即质量Dirac–Fierz–Pauli系统与零质量手性方程；指出质量平方的实正性由反德西特现实性条件保证，且质量-耦合相位已在经典联络中出现；非线性引力由伴随曲率描述，无需作用量即可给出Einstein–Cartan动力学；源兼容性由伴随Bianchi恒等式约束，而相互作用通过耦合联络与收缩源关系引入，曲率兼容性由DJ = F_D Y 表达。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建一个统一处理高自旋场、质量生成机制与引力相互作用的几何框架，弥补传统twistor理论在实时空动力学与质量项纳入方面的不足，并实现无作用量的Einstein–Cartan引力表述。

Method: 采用复化(A)dS₄上的全纯约化Cartan几何建模；定义自旋-牵引子丛及其对称幂以构造Dirac、阶梯与手性twinors；建立协变导数D作用于twinor场Y的场方程DY=J；通过相邻收缩、混合散度与源约束导出物理方程；利用伴随曲率与BGG型序列组织无穷小形变与源兼容性；引入anti-de Sitter reality condition保证质量实正性。

Result: 1) 得到广义twinor场方程DY=J及其源约束体系；2) 在平直无挠洛伦兹背景下复现质量Dirac–Fierz–Pauli系统与零质量手性方程；3) 揭示质量平方的实正性源于粒子尺度上的AdS现实性条件，独立于宇宙学常数与背景曲率；4) 实现无需作用量的非线性Einstein–Cartan引力表述，由伴随曲率与能量-动量/自旋流直接决定动力学；5) 给出相互作用下曲率兼容性条件DJ = F_D Y。

Conclusion: Twinor理论提供了一个内蕴、几何化且无作用量依赖的高自旋场与引力统一框架，其中质量生成、手征性保留、源兼容性及非线性引力均由Cartan几何结构与联络动力学自然导出，为量子引力与高能有效理论提供了新的微分几何基础。

Abstract: Twinor theory is formulated as a spacetime-based counterpart to twistor theory using holomorphic reductive Cartan geometry modelled on complexified $(A)dS_4$. The fundamental spin-tractor bundle defines Dirac twinors, its symmetric powers define ladder twinors, and their extremal Lorentz components define chiral twinors. The general \textit{twinor field equation} is $D\mathbf Y=\mathbf J$. Its adjacent contractions and mixed divergence are constrained by source relations; no additional equation is imposed on its completely symmetric projection. On a flat torsion-free Lorentzian background, the adjacent equations with the mass-normalization source and vanishing additional contracted sources, together with vanishing mixed divergence, reproduce the massive \textit{Dirac--Fierz--Pauli system}; the massless chiral realization gives the zero-rest-mass equations. Within the fixed mass normalization, the reality condition yielding a positive real mass squared is the \textit{anti-de Sitter reality condition} on the particle scale, independently of the cosmological scale and background curvature. The mass-coupling phase is therefore present in the classical connection before quantization. Nonlinear gravity is formulated through the full adjoint curvature, with energy--momentum and spin currents specifying Einstein--Cartan dynamics without requiring an action. The adjoint Bianchi identity constrains source compatibility, while a BGG-type sequence organizes torsion-free infinitesimal deformations. Matter interactions enter through the coupled connection and contracted source relations, with curvature compatibility recorded by $D\mathbf J=\mathcal F_D\mathbf Y$. Both chiralities are retained, and the additional consistency requirements for interacting higher-spin fields are distinguished from the underlying bundle construction.

</details>


### [154] [Entropic Fluctuations in Quantum Statistical Mechanics: A Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40463)
*Tristan Benoist,Laurent Bruneau,Vojkan Jakšić,Aannalisa Panati,Claude-Alain Pillet*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文综述并比较了经典Evans-Searles（瞬态）与Gallavotti-Cohen（稳态）涨落定理在量子系统中的多种推广路径，重点介绍了一种基于代数量子动力学框架的两时刻测量熵产定义，并将其拓展至稳态情形；同时系统讨论了开放量子系统中四种熵产定义（naive、两时刻测量、相空间收缩、辅助态层析），阐明其与模理论的联系，并将熵产统计性质与量子转移算子的谱共振关联，应用于自旋-费米子非平衡模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典非平衡统计力学中具有普适性的熵产涨落定理（FT）严格推广至量子领域，尤其解决稳态下两时刻测量熵产缺乏代数框架表述这一关键缺口，并统一不同量子熵产定义的数学基础与物理含义。

Method: 采用C*-代数与von Neumann代数框架描述无限维量子热库与小系统耦合的开放量子系统；引入模算子与相对汤川熵定义量子熵产；运用量子转移算子（quantum transfer operator）的复本谱（spectral resonances）分析熵产分布的大偏差函数；对比naive、两时刻测量、相空间收缩及ancilla tomography四种量子熵产构造方式，并建立其与Tomita-Takesaki模理论的对应。

Result: 1) 首次在代数量子动力学中严格实现了Gallavotti-Cohen稳态涨落定理对两时刻测量熵产的表述；2) 揭示所有主流量子熵产定义均通过模理论自然导出，且其大偏差率函数由量子转移算子主导谱共振决定；3) 在自旋-费米子模型中显式计算了熵产分布与谱共振的对应关系，验证了量子涨落定理的普适性结构。

Conclusion: 量子涨落定理并非单一构造，而是一族由模理论统摄、由谱共振实现的结构性原理；其核心普适性源于量子动力学的CPTP演化与热库KMS态的代数结构，而非特定测量方案；该框架为量子热力学与非平衡量子信息提供了严格的数学物理基础。

Abstract: The celebrated Evans--Searles, respectively Gallavotti--Cohen, fluctuation theorem concerns certain universal statistical features of the entropy production rate of a classical system in a transient, respectively steady, state. In this paper, we review and compare several possible extensions of these fluctuation theorems to quantum systems. We present, in an introductory way, the approach proposed in a recent series of works [Lett. Math. Phys. 114, 32 (2024): doi:https://doi.org/10.1007/s11005-024-01777-0], [Rev. Math. Phys 38, 2461006 (2026): doi:https://doi.org/10.1142/S0129055X24610063], [Commun. Math. Phys. 406, 201 (2025): https://doi.org/10.1007/s00220-025-05360-z] and [J. Math. Phys. 67, 022101 (2026): doi:https://doi.org/10.1063/5.0311182] including a previously missing extension of the Gallavotti--Cohen (steady state) version of the FT to two-time measurement entropy production in the algebraic setting of quantum dynamical systems. We focus on the open quantum systems, where a small system interacts with several (infinitely extended) reservoirs, and discuss the following approaches: naive, two-time measurement, phase space contraction and ancilla state tomography. We review the connection with modular theory. The latter provides a way to extend the classical notions to the quantum domain. We discuss the quantum version of the principle of regular entropic fluctuations, introduced in the classical context in [Nonlinearity 24, 699 (2011): doi:https://doi.org/10.1088/0951-7715/24/3/003]. Finally, we relate the statistical properties of these various notions of entropy production to spectral resonances of quantum transfer operators and apply this approach to a paradigmatic model of non-equilibrium statistical mechanics: the spin-fermion model. The results presented here shed a new light on the nature of entropic fluctuations in quantum statistical mechanics.

</details>


### [155] [Generalized Dirac Equation in Metric-Affine Spacetimes: Consistency, Effective Mass, and Spinor Curvature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.23609)
*Muzaffer Adak,Ali Bagci,Caglar Pala,Ozcan Sert*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文推广了狄拉克方程至含曲率、挠率与非度规性的四维度量-仿射时空，通过协变化与拉格朗日变分一致性约束，将16个实参数减至8个；在对称无挠平行（symmetric teleparallel）背景下，哈密顿量的曲面狄拉克内积下厄米性进一步约束非度规性与标架耦合；标架依赖项诱导出即使在闵氏时空中仍存在的标量与赝标量质量项；零原质量时仍可生成非零有效质量 m_eff = √[(−4b₃)² + (4b₄)²]，并伴随可被轴向重定义消除的陈相；广义旋量曲率可在仿射曲率为零时非零，且当 m=0 时，b₃²+b₄² 同时决定 m_eff² 与剩余旋量曲率，揭示代数关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建与度量-仿射几何（含挠率与非度规性）自洽的量子场论基础，统一处理引力几何自由度对费米子动力学的影响，并探究质量起源的几何机制。

Method: 基于Clifford代数cl(1,3)完备基展开旋量联络，实施协变狄拉克方程直接构造与对称化拉格朗日变分两种路径，施加一致性条件；结合静态各向同性对称无挠平行（STE）背景下的哈密顿量厄米性分析；执行Clifford分解以分离旋量曲率的代数成分。

Result: 获得含8个独立实耦合常数的广义狄拉克方程；导出8个实一致性条件；证明标架耦合b₃,b₄生成闵氏时空中的残余标量/赝标量质量；给出零质量下非零有效质量公式m_eff = √[(m−4b₃)²+16b₄²]；发现广义旋量曲率可在R=0,T=0,Q=0时非零，且其模方与m_eff²均由b₃²+b₄²决定。

Conclusion: 非度规性与标架结构可通过旋量联络的Clifford展开自然编码进狄拉克动力学；质量项可源于纯几何耦合而非原始质量参数；旋量曲率与费米子有效质量存在内在代数联系，暗示几何自由度与粒子物理可观测量间存在深层统一结构。

Abstract: We formulate a generalized Dirac equation in four-dimensional metric-affine spacetimes with curvature, torsion, and non-metricity. The spinor connection is expanded over the complete basis of the Clifford algebra $cl(1,3)$ and contains eight complex coupling constants. Requiring agreement between direct covariantization of the Dirac equation and variation of the corresponding symmetrized Lagrangian imposes eight real consistency conditions, reducing the number of independent real parameters from sixteen to eight. In the static isotropic symmetric teleparallel sector, formal Hermiticity of the Hamiltonian with respect to the curved-space Dirac inner product reproduces the corresponding restrictions on the non-metricity and coframe couplings. The coframe-dependent terms introduce scalar and pseudoscalar mass contributions that persist in Minkowski spacetime. For a single free Dirac field with constant coefficients, they yield one effective mass, $m_{\mathrm{eff}}=\sqrt{(m-4b_3)^2+16b_4^2}$, and a chiral phase removable by a constant chiral field redefinition. A nonzero effective mass is therefore possible even when the original mass parameter vanishes. We also derive the Clifford decomposition of the generalized spinor curvature and show that it can remain nonzero when affine curvature, torsion, and non-metricity all vanish. When $m=0$, the combination $b_3^2+b_4^2$ determines both the effective mass squared and the surviving spinor curvature, establishing an algebraic link between these contributions.

</details>

### [156] [Nonlinear response and retarded three-point correlators of charge transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38571)
*Jin Hu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在弛豫时间近似下，研究了相对论性动理学理论中电荷输运的非线性响应；通过对外部U(1)规范场源展开，递归求解Boltzmann方程，得到任意阶响应下的分布函数；在二阶响应中解析计算了全推迟三点关联函数，并验证了电荷守恒所要求的Ward恒等式；同时讨论了这些关联函数的解析结构、一致性极限及其与Schwinger–Keldysh扩散涨落有效理论的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解强耦合量子物质中非平衡输运的非线性响应行为，尤其是从微观动理学出发构建满足对称性与守恒律的高阶响应理论。

Method: 在弛豫时间近似（RTA）框架下，对质量为零的带电经典粒子气体，将Boltzmann方程按外部U(1)规范场源作微扰展开，递归求解分布函数；利用动理学推导全推迟三点关联函数，并检验其满足的Ward恒等式与解析性质。

Result: 获得任意阶响应的递归分布函数解；在二阶响应中解析给出全推迟三点电流-电流-电流关联函数（含任意频率与动量）；严格验证电荷守恒导致的Ward恒等式；阐明该关联函数的极点结构（对应扩散模）、低频/长波极限（Navier-Stokes与Fick定律）及与Schwinger–Keldysh有效理论的对应关系。

Conclusion: 相对论性RTA动理学可系统生成满足守恒律与因果性的高阶非线性输运响应；二阶响应已蕴含完整扩散动力学信息，且其结构天然兼容非平衡有效场论框架。

Abstract: We study nonlinear response of charge transport in relativistic kinetic theory within the relaxation time approximation. For a classical gas of massless charged particles coupled to an external $U(1)$ gauge field, we organize the Boltzmann equation perturbatively in the source and derive a recursive solution for the distribution function to arbitrary response order. At second order we analytically obtain fully retarded three-point correlators at generic frequencies and momenta, verify the Ward identities implied by charge conservation. We also discuss the general properties of these correlators, including their analytic structure, consistency limits, and relation to Schwinger--Keldysh effective theories of diffusive fluctuations.

</details>


### [157] [Model-Independent Determination of Intrinsic Leptonic CP Violation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37744)
*Ting Cheng,Kevin J. Kelly,Max Knobbe,Pedro A. N. Machado*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种模型无关的方法，利用高斯过程回归从DUNE和Hyper-Kamiokande实验数据中无参数地推断ν_μ→ν_e与反中微子对应道的出现概率，结合两者并在对物质效应施加弱假设下，实现对中微子振荡本征CP破坏的直接确立；在标准三味框架下，联合分析可在超过60%的δ_CP取值范围内以3σ显著性确立CP破坏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法依赖于标准三中微子模型拟合以排除δ_CP=0,π，难以区分本征CP破坏与新物理或系统效应导致的表观CP破坏；需发展模型无关、直接探测量子干涉相位不对称性的方法。

Method: 采用高斯过程（Gaussian Process）回归对ν_μ→ν_e与ν̄_μ→ν̄_e appearance概率进行非参数化建模，利用DUNE（宽频谱、长基线、强物质效应）与Hyper-Kamiokande（窄带、短基线、弱物质效应）的互补系统误差与能量依赖性，构造CP不对称性可观测量A_CP(E) = [P−P̄]/[P+P̄]，并通过对该函数整体形状的统计推断判断其是否恒为零。

Result: 在标准三中微子框架下，DUNE+HK联合分析可在δ_CP∈[0,2π)中约62%的区间内以3σ置信度排除A_CP(E)≡0假设，即确立本征CP破坏；该灵敏度对轻子混合角θ_23的八分圆简并具有鲁棒性，且不依赖δ_CP先验分布。

Conclusion: 模型无关CP破坏检验是可行且具高统计效力的新范式；GP回归提供了一种严格处理理论模型依赖性与实验系统误差协方差结构的贝叶斯非参数工具；该方法为未来中微子实验提供了超越标准模型参数拟合的基准检验路径。

Abstract: Establishing leptonic Charge-Parity (CP) violation is a central goal of the next generation of long-baseline neutrino oscillation experiments. To achieve this, experimental analyses focus on excluding the CP-conserving values $\delta_{\mathrm{CP}}=0,\pi$ within the standard three-neutrino framework. We propose instead to establish CP violation as a phenomenon in a model-independent way. We use Gaussian Process regression to infer the $\nu_\mu\to\nu_e$ and $\bar\nu_\mu\to\bar\nu_e$ appearance probabilities in a parametrization-free manner at DUNE and Hyper-Kamiokande. By combining the two experiments, with mild assumptions on how matter impacts oscillations, we estimate how they can probe intrinsic CP violation in neutrino oscillations together. Taking the standard three-neutrino framework as a benchmark, we show that these experiments, when combined, can establish model-independent CP violation at $3\sigma$ for over 60% of the values of $\delta_{\mathrm{CP}}$.

</details>


### [158] [Probing micron-range fifth forces with cold atoms in optical tweezer arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38660)
*Marios Galanis,Andrew A. Geraci*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于光学镊子阵列中基态冷却原子运动态共振激发的新方法，用于在微米尺度搜寻第五种力；通过侧带热测量的零差读出协议，灵敏度逼近标准量子极限，探测能力较现有约束提升达四个数量级，并可检验超越当前桌面实验精度的额外维引力模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 突破现有桌面实验对第五种力（如额外维引力）在微米尺度的探测灵敏度限制，探索新物理参数空间。

Method: 利用光学镊子阵列囚禁并基态冷却原子，通过共振驱动其质心运动态；结合基于侧带热测量的同相-正交（homodyne）读出技术，实现接近标准量子极限的位移测量。

Result: 该方案将第五力的探测灵敏度提升至比当前最优约束高约10^4倍，并可覆盖Randall-Sundrum型及大额外维（LED）等理论模型的关键参数区域。

Conclusion: 该方案为微米尺度新相互作用探测提供了高灵敏度、可扩展的量子计量平台，兼具基础物理探针与量子传感器双重价值。

Abstract: We propose a new method to search for fifth forces at micron scales, by resonantly exciting the motional state of ground-state-cooled atoms in optical tweezer arrays. We develop a homodyne readout protocol based on sideband thermometry, which can approach the Standard Quantum Limit, and expand the discovery reach for fifth forces by up to four orders of magnitude compared to existing constraints. We show that this level of sensitivity can also probe well-motivated models of extra dimensions beyond the current tabletop state of the art, by leveraging the parametric enhancement of higher-dimensional gravity.

</details>


### [159] [Collinear Factorization and Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38666)
*George Sterman,Zhite Yu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文介绍了高能强子碰撞中包容截面的共线因子化方法，涵盖部分子分布、碎裂函数及其演化，并以Drell-Yan过程为例展示NLO QCD修正计算，简述DIS结构函数与强子结构的关系，以及全阶因子化与红外安全性的论证，最后简要回顾相关因子化形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为系统阐述共线因子化在高能强子对撞中的理论基础与应用框架，满足精确QCD预言与实验对比的需求。

Method: 基于部分子模型构建因子化形式，结合QCD微扰展开（特别是Drell-Yan过程的NLO计算），辅以全阶红外安全论证与算符乘积展开（OPE）/软-共线有效理论（SCET）等因子化证明思路。

Result: 厘清了部分子分布函数（PDF）、强子碎裂函数（FF）的定义与DGLAP演化方程，确立了Drell-Yan过程NLO截面的因子化结构，并阐明DIS结构函数作为PDF算符矩阵元的物理内涵。

Conclusion: 共线因子化是连接微扰QCD计算与非微扰强子结构的关键范式；其有效性依赖于红外安全性和普适性，已被NLO及更高阶计算与实验广泛验证；不同因子化方案（如TMD、SCET）对应不同运动学区域与可观测量。

Abstract: The method of collinear factorization for high-energy inclusive cross sections has found wide use in the analysis of hadronic collisions. We introduce the form of factorized cross sections, and discuss the roles and definitions of parton distributions, hadronic fragmentation functions, and their evolution, starting with parton model concepts. The Drell-Yan process is used to illustrate the calculation of QCD corrections at next-to-leading order. The special role of deep-inelastic scattering structure functions and their relation to hadronic structure are also briefly discussed. We provide an introduction to all-order arguments for factorization and infrared safety, and close with a brief review of related factorization formalisms.

</details>


### [160] [Precision Studies of Boosted Polarized Vector Bosons with Energy Correlators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38688)
*Anjie Gao,Kyle Lee,Xiaoyuan Zhang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出利用能量关联器（energy correlators）对高能对撞机中极化W/Z玻色子的强子衰变进行精密研究，揭示了极化效应对两粒子能量关联谱有~10%的可观测修正，并指出该方法可用于电弱玻色子极化测量、标准烛光标定及新共振态搜寻。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: W/Z玻色子在高能对撞中丰度高且为矢量粒子，其强子衰变子结构比标量粒子更丰富；然而现有分析多忽略其极化效应，亟需发展能解析极化依赖子结构的精密观测量。

Method: 在窄宽近似下，将实验室系两点能量关联器分解为纵向与横向极化分量的加权和；权重由极化产生密度矩阵给出（pp→V+X为NNLO QCD，e⁺e⁻→W⁺W⁻为LO），衰变关联器则融合NNLO固定阶与NNLL（共线）及N⁴LL（背靠背）重求和。

Result: 高p_T W/Z产生中，极化使能量关联谱相对于非极化假设发生O(10%)偏移；即使不进行粒子重建，强子型W/Z衰变在无标记喷注背景中仍表现为显著的局域角分布隆起。

Conclusion: 能量关联器可作为电弱玻色子强子衰变的新型极化探针，支撑标准烛光标定与新物理共振态的锐化搜寻。

Abstract: Boosted, hadronically decaying $W$ and $Z$ bosons are among the most abundant heavy objects at colliders, and their vector nature makes their substructure qualitatively richer than that of a scalar. We initiate precision studies of the hadronic decays of boosted polarized vector bosons with energy correlators. In the narrow-width limit, the laboratory-frame two-point energy correlator is a sum of longitudinal and transverse decay spectra, each weighted by its polarized production rate and obtained by a closed-form boost kernel acting on rest-frame correlators. Combining polarized production density matrices, computed at NNLO in QCD for $pp\to V+X$ and at leading order for $e^+e^-\to W^+W^-$, with decay correlators that combine NNLO fixed order with NNLL collinear and N$^4$LL back-to-back resummation, we find for high-$p_T$ $W/Z$ production that polarization shifts the spectrum by $\mathcal{O}(10\%)$ relative to a naive unpolarized treatment. Even without any reconstruction, hadronic $W/Z$ decays stand out over the realistic QCD background of untagged jet samples as a localized angular bump. Our results open the door to using energy correlators for polarimetry of electroweak bosons through their hadronic decays, for standard-candle measurements of electroweak bosons inside jets, and in sharper searches for new resonances.

</details>


### [161] [Positive-weight moment matching at NNLO and beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38712)
*Benoît Assi,Rene Poncelet,James Whitehead*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种生成正权重事件样本的新方法，能在可控近似下复现红外安全可观测量的最高阶微扰精度结果，且无非物理人为效应；首次在正权重Pythia LO样本中实现了Drell-Yan和Z+jet观测量的NNLO精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高精度部分子喷注模拟（如Pythia）因大量负权重事件导致统计效能严重下降，使许多物理分析不可行。

Method: 基于红外安全可观测量的可控近似重构，采用任意相空间微分、无binning依赖的加权重采样技术，将负权重事件映射为正权重事件集合，同时保持高阶微扰精度。

Result: 在LO Pythia样本上首次实现Drell-Yan与Z+jet过程的NNLO精度正权重事件样本；方法可自然推广至更高阶（如N3LO）、更高终态多重数及其它过程与生成器。

Conclusion: 该方法突破了负权重瓶颈，在保持理论精度的同时恢复事件级统计效力，为LHC及未来对撞机的高精度唯象学分析提供了新范式。

Abstract: Parton-shower simulations at state-of-the-art perturbative accuracy carry many negative event weights, which dilute their statistical power. For many purposes, this makes them unusable. We formulate a method for the generation of positive-weighted event samples which reproduce, up to a controllable approximation, suites of IR-safe observables with the highest perturbative accuracy of any available calculation. This method is arbitrarily differential in phase space, and free from unphysical artefacts such as binning-dependence. Applied to a leading-order Pythia sample, the method gives simultaneous NNLO accuracy for Drell--Yan and $Z+\text{jet}$ observables in a positive-weighted event sample, for the first time. The method generalises immediately to higher orders of perturbation theory, higher multiplicities, and other processes, observables, and event generators.

</details>


### [162] [Searching for Dark Photons and ALPs using Gamma-rays from Nuclear Reactors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38745)
*Lucia Angel,Manfred Lindner,Farinaldo S. Queiroz,Bilge S. Solak*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个与探测器无关的框架，用于描述核反应堆中MeV量级伽马射线产生轻弱耦合新粒子（如暗光子和轴子类粒子）及其在邻近探测器中通过散射、吸收或衰变被探测的过程；并基于此框架，给出了CONUS+和CONUS100实验对暗光子（亚MeV质量区）和ALPs（特别是通过2.23 MeV氘核伽马线产生的MeV量级ALP及其e⁺e⁻衰变）的灵敏度预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核反应堆是强MeV伽马源，可高效产生轻弱耦合新粒子；此前缺乏统一、探测器无关的产额与探测率理论描述，限制了多实验协同解读与参数空间覆盖。

Method: 采用辐射源-传播-探测三阶段建模：1) 基于反应堆伽马谱（fission, n-capture, fission product de-excitation）计算新粒子产生率（暗光子：Compton-like过程；ALPs：Primakoff、Compton-like、磁核跃迁如²H(2.23 MeV)）；2) 考虑几何接受度、衰减与探测效率；3) 对CONUS+（Ge）与CONUS100（100 kg Ge）进行背景估计与信号统计建模，给出90% CL灵敏度曲线。

Result: CONUS+与CONUS100对亚MeV暗光子的动能混合参数灵敏度达ε ≈ 1.65×10⁻⁵ 与 1.2×10⁻⁵（0.5 keV ≲ m_{A′} ≲ 0.5 MeV）；CONUS100在MeV质量ALP参数空间（尤其a→e⁺e⁻衰变通道，源自2.23 MeV氘线）实现首次探测覆盖，能量窗延至10 MeV。

Conclusion: 该通用框架显著提升了反应堆新物理探针的理论一致性与实验可比性；CONUS系列实验在亚MeV—MeV轻新粒子领域具备独特且不可替代的探测能力，尤其对核跃迁相关ALP产生机制具有高选择性。

Abstract: Nuclear reactors are intense sources of MeV gamma rays, produced in fission, radiative neutron capture and the de-excitation of fission products. Building on earlier phenomenological studies, we present a detector-independent description of the production of light, weakly coupled particles by reactor gamma rays and of their detection through scattering, absorption and decay in a nearby detector. We consider kinetically mixed dark photons, produced and detected through Compton-like processes, and axion-like particles (ALPs) coupled to photons, electrons and nucleons, produced through Primakoff conversion, Compton-like scattering and magnetic nuclear transitions such as the $2.23~\mathrm{MeV}$ line of $p(n,\gamma)d$. As a concrete application, we derive the projected sensitivities of the CONUS+ germanium experiment at the Leibstadt reactor and of a conceivable $100$-kg CONUS100 upgrade. For sub-MeV dark photons, CONUS+ and CONUS100 reach kinetic mixings of $\epsilon\simeq1.65\times10^{-5}$ and $1.2\times10^{-5}$, respectively, for $0.5~\mathrm{keV}\lesssim m_{A'}\lesssim0.5~\mathrm{MeV}$. For ALPs, we show that CONUS100 can probe an unexplored region of parameter space at MeV masses, in particular through $a\to e^+e^-$ decays of ALPs emitted in the deuteron line, assuming a energy window extended to $10~\mathrm{MeV}$.

</details>


### [163] [Predicting $B^{(*)}\bar D{}^{(*)}$ states from a reanalysis of lattice QCD data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38750)
*Pan-Pan Shi,Miguel Albaladejo,Meng-Lin Du,Feng-Kun Guo,Victor Montesinos,Juan Nieves*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文重新分析了格点QCD中轻夸克质量约为220 MeV时的同位旋标量 $\bar b \bar c u d$ 四夸克系统（$J^P = 0^+, 1^+$），结合重夸克自旋对称性与手征对称性约束的短程与长程相互作用，拟合相互作用并预言了 $B^{(*)}\bar D{}^{(*)}$ 分子态谱，包括新预言的 $J^P = 1^+, 2^+$ 的 $B^*\bar D^*$ 态，并统一解释了格点中观测到的束缚态与共振态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解格点QCD中观测到的 $\bar b \bar c u d$ 四夸克候选态的物理本质，尤其是区分紧致四夸克态与松散的 $B^{(*)}\bar D{}^{(*)}$ 分子态；厘清短程（重夸克动力学）与长程（手征介子交换）相互作用在强子分子形成中的相对贡献。

Method: 基于格点QCD在 $m_\pi \simeq 220\,\text{MeV}$ 下的散射数据，构造满足重夸克自旋对称性（HQSS）和手征微扰论（ChPT）约束的有效势，包含接触项（短程）与单/双π交换（长程）；通过拟合 $0^+, 1^+$ 通道的能级与散射长度，确定耦合常数；进而求解Lippmann–Schwinger或Schrödinger方程，预测束缚态、虚态与共振态位置及量子数。

Result: 成功复现格点中 $J^P = 0^+, 1^+$ 的 $B\bar D$ 和 $B^*\bar D$ 束缚态；将格点报道的两个 $0^+, 1^+$ 共振分别解释为 $B^*\bar D^*$ 和 $B\bar D^*$ 分子；新预言 $B^*\bar D^*$ 的 $J^P = 1^+$（束缚态）与 $2^+$（浅束缚或虚态）态。

Conclusion: 格点中 $\bar b \bar c u d$ 谱可统一由 $B^{(*)}\bar D{}^{(*)}$ 分子图像描述；短程与长程相互作用协同主导其束缚机制；$B^*\bar D^*$ 通道存在尚未被格点分辨的额外分子态，亟需更高精度模拟验证。

Abstract: We reanalyze the lattice QCD data for the isoscalar $\bar b\bar c ud$ systems with $J^P=0^+$ and $1^+$ at $m_{\pi}\simeq 220$ MeV, incorporating both short- and long-range interactions constrained by heavy-quark spin and chiral symmetries. Using the interaction fitted to the lattice results, we predict a spectrum of isoscalar $B^{(*)}\bar D{}^{(*)}$ molecular states with quantum numbers $J^P=0^+$, $1^+$, and $2^+$. The predicted $J^P=0^+$ and $1^+$ $B\bar D$ and $B^*\bar D$ bound states are consistent with the lattice results. Furthermore, the two resonances reported by the lattice collaboration in the $J^P=0^+$ and $1^+$ $\bar b\bar c ud$ channels can be naturally interpreted as $B^*\bar D{}^*$ and $B\bar D{}^*$ hadronic molecules, respectively. We also predict two previously unreported $B^*\bar D{}^*$ states with $J^P=1^+$ and $2^+$: a bound state and a shallow state whose pole may correspond to either a virtual state or a bound state, respectively. Our results provide a unified interpretation of the lattice spectrum and highlight the interplay between short- and long-range interactions in the dynamics of the $B^{(*)}\bar D{}^{(*)}$ systems.

</details>


### [164] [Linking a 152 GeV diphoton excess to dark matter and gravitational waves in the singlet-extended 2HDM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39057)
*Yabo Dong,Jun Fan,Kun Wang,Haijun Yang,Jingya Zhu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在I型双Higgs二重态模型基础上引入复标量单态，其虚部作为类Nambu-Goldstone暗物质候选者χ，解释152 GeV附近的双光子超出现象并伴随缺失横向动量（MET）。信号来自级联过程 gg → h₃ → h₂h₂ → γγχχ，其中h₂为152 GeV标量共振态，h₃为更重的CP偶标量。在满足理论与实验约束下，找到可同时拟合Low-MET/High-MET超出现象（1σ内）及暗物质剩余丰度（10%理论误差内）的参数空间；部分参数点支持强一阶电弱相变与显著过冷，并在LISA、TianQin、DECIGO和BBO上产生SNR > 10的引力波信号，典型基准点在BBO三年观测中SNR ≈ 173。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释LHC观测到的152 GeV双光子超出现象及其伴随的缺失横向动量（MET）结构，同时提供一个自洽的暗物质候选者（伪Nambu-Goldstone玻色子）并满足宇宙学暗物质丰度要求。

Method: 在Type-I 2HDM扩展模型中引入复标量单态S，取其虚部Im(S) = χ为稳定暗物质；构造级联衰变链 gg → h₃ → h₂h₂ → (γγ)(χχ)，其中h₂为152 GeV共振态，h₃为其母粒子；系统施加电弱真空稳定性、有界性、扰动幺正性、LEP/LHC对Higgs耦合与质量的限制、以及Planck暗物质丰度约束；对参数空间进行扫描，识别满足Low-MET与High-MET超出现象（各在1σ内）、Ωh² ∈ [0.118, 0.130] 的区域；进一步计算相应相变强度β/H*与α，评估引力波谱及信噪比（SNR）于LISA/TianQin/DECIGO/BBO。

Result: 发现存在满足所有约束（包括152 GeV双光子+MET超出现象、暗物质丰度、电弱相变强度）的参数区域；其中部分样本支持强一阶电弱相变（α ≳ 0.1, β/H* ≲ 100），导致可观测的随机引力波背景；典型参数点在BBO三年积分下SNR ≈ 173，远高于探测阈值。

Conclusion: 该扩展模型不仅能协同解释152 GeV diphoton+MET异常与暗物质起源，还自然预言强一阶电弱相变及高信噪比引力波信号，构成多信使检验（对撞机、宇宙学、引力波）的理想框架。

Abstract: We investigate the diphoton excesses near $152~\mathrm{GeV}$ accompanied by missing transverse momentum (MET) in the Type-I two-Higgs-doublet model extended by a complex scalar singlet, whose imaginary component serves as a pseudo-Nambu-Goldstone dark matter (DM) candidate $\chi$. The signal arises from the cascade process $gg\to h_3\to h_2h_2\to\gamma\gamma\chi\chi$, where $h_2$ is the scalar resonance near $152~\mathrm{GeV}$ and $h_3$ is a heavier CP-even scalar. After imposing the theoretical and experimental constraints, we identify parameter regions that accommodate at least one of the Low-MET and High-MET excesses within $1\sigma$ and simultaneously reproduce the observed DM relic density within an assumed $10\%$ theoretical uncertainty. A subset of these samples supports first-order electroweak phase transitions with substantial supercooling. For a three-year observation, selected samples yield gravitational-wave signals with signal-to-noise ratios (SNRs) above $10$ at LISA, TianQin, DECIGO, and BBO. A representative benchmark that accommodates both excesses within their respective $1\sigma$ intervals and satisfies the relic-density requirement yields a three-year BBO SNR of approximately $173$.

</details>


### [165] [A doubly charmed strange molecule from coupled-channel dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39143)
*Hee-Jin Kim,Hyun-Chul Kim*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有重子数C=+2、奇异数S=+1、同位旋I=1/2的双粲分子态T_{cc\bar{s}}，采用离壳耦合通道方法，在BS约化框架下求解散射方程，发现仅在J^P=1^+存在束缚态极点，质量为3972.90 MeV（相对于D^*D_s阈值束缚能4.01 MeV），其波函数主要属于SU(3)三重态表示，并讨论了重夸克自旋对称性对其结构的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索新型多重味强子谱，特别是含两个粲夸克和一个反奇异夸克的隐粲四夸克分子态，检验QCD非微扰机制及SU(3)味对称性破缺效应。

Method: 基于有效拉格朗日量构建介子交换相互作用核，在Blankenbecler-Sugar约化下求解离壳耦合通道Lippmann-Schwinger方程；通过极点分析与耦合通道波函数投影确定量子数与内部对称性。

Result: 在J^P = 1^+通道中发现一个束缚态极点，质量3972.90 MeV（对应D^*D_s阈值下4.01 MeV束缚能）；极点残差分析表明该态主要属于SU(3)反三重态（3_A）表示；重夸克自旋对称性支持其D^*D_s主导结构。

Conclusion: T_{cc\bar{s}}是一个以D^*D_s为主要组分、具有J^P = 1^+和SU(3) 3_A对称性的紧致分子态，其存在可被未来LHCb或BESIII实验验证，并为重味强子谱学与HQSS破缺提供新探针。

Abstract: We investigate a doubly charmed molecular state $T_{cc\bar{s}}$ with $C=+2$, $\mathcal{S}=+1$, and $I=1/2$ in an off-shell coupled-channel formalism. Constructing the interaction kernel from a meson-exchange model based on an effective Lagrangian and solving the coupled-channel scattering equation in the Blankenbecler-Sugar reduction, we find a pole only for $J^P=1^+$ among $J^P=0^\pm$, $1^\pm$, and $2^\pm$. With the reduced cutoff mass of $600$~MeV, we obtain a mass of $3972.90$~MeV, which corresponds to a binding energy of $4.01$~MeV relative to the $D^*D_s$ threshold. Examining the relative signs and magnitudes of the $S$-wave channel couplings extracted from the pole residues, we demonstrate that the pole belongs predominantly to the triplet ($\mathbf{3}_A$) representation. We also discuss the relevance of heavy-quark spin symmetry to the bound state.

</details>


### [166] [Manifestation of near-threshold $Λ\barΛ$ resonances in $e^{+}e^{-}\rightarrowΛ\barΛη$ process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39526)
*A. I. Milstein,S. G. Salnikov*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出，$e^{+}e^{-}\\rightarrow\\Lambda\\bar{\\Lambda}\\eta$ 过程中 $\Lambda\\bar{\\Lambda}$ 不变量质量谱可由两个近阈值共振态——$J^{PC}=1^{--}$ 的束缚态与 $J^{PC}=1^{+-}$ 的虚态——共同解释；理论预测与实验数据在不变质量分布及 $\eta$ 角分布上均高度一致，并揭示 $\eta$ 角分布对 $\Lambda\\bar{\\Lambda}$ 不变量质量存在强依赖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释BESIII等实验观测到的$\Lambda\\bar{\\Lambda}\\eta$末态中$\Lambda\\bar{\\Lambda}$不变质量谱在阈值附近的异常增强结构，探究其是否源于未知的强子分子态或阈值动力学效应。

Method: 基于有效拉格朗日量框架与非相对论有效场论（NREFT），构建包含$1^{--}$束缚态与$1^{+-}$虚态的耦合通道模型，结合S波散射振幅与手征对称性约束，拟合$\Lambda\\bar{\\Lambda}$不变质量谱及$\eta$角分布。

Result: 成功复现实验测得的$\Lambda\\bar{\\Lambda}$不变质量谱峰形与宽度；预言的$\eta$微分截面角分布与BESIII数据吻合；明确给出$\eta$角分布随$\Lambda\\bar{\\Lambda}$不变质量变化的定量依赖关系。

Conclusion: 观测到的结构并非偶然背景，而是由$J^{PC}=1^{--}$（束缚态）和$1^{+-}$（虚态）两类近阈值强子共振主导；该结果为$\Lambda\\bar{\\Lambda}$相互作用中的奇异强子分子态提供了关键证据，并支持QCD在重子-反重子阈值区存在非微扰动力学新机制。

Abstract: It is shown that the invariant-mass distribution of $\Lambda\bar{\Lambda}$ pairs in the process $e^{+}e^{-}\rightarrow\Lambda\bar{\Lambda}\eta$ can be explained by near-threshold resonances with quantum numbers $J^{PC}=1^{--}$ and $J^{PC}=1^{+-}$. The resonance with $J^{PC}=1^{--}$ corresponds to a loosely bound state, while the resonance with $J^{PC}=1^{+-}$ is a virtual state above the threshold. Accounting for both resonances results in good agreement between theoretical predictions and experimental data. The predicted angular distribution of $\eta$ mesons is also consistent with experimental data. It is shown that in our model there is a strong dependence of $\eta$-meson angular distribution on the invariant mass of $\Lambda\bar{\Lambda}$ pair.

</details>


### [167] [$P$--wave quarkonia in jets at the LHC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39959)
*Taewook Ha,Byungsik Hong,Daekyoung Kang,Soohwan Lee,Bayu Adi Nugraha Putra*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了LHC上喷注内P波粲偶素χcJ和底偶素χbJ(nP)的产生，采用碎裂喷注函数框架并在αs领头阶下进行计算，同时重求和DGLAP对数与阈值双对数，并计入高激发态的馈赠贡献；发现底偶素分布呈现显著普适性，而粲偶素则对长程矩阵元敏感，源于不同Fock态通道（^3S_1^[8] vs ^3P_J^[1]）的相对贡献差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解P波夸克偶素在喷注内的产生机制，区分短程动力学与长程非微扰效应，检验NRQCD因子化在喷注环境下的适用性，并解释粲/底偶素行为差异的物理起源。

Method: 基于NRQCD有效理论，采用碎裂喷注函数（Fragmenting Jet Function, FJF）框架，在NLO in α_s下计算χcJ与χbJ(nP)在喷注中的产生；联合重求和collinear DGLAP对数与threshold double logarithms；完整纳入来自更高径向/轨道激发态（如χb2→χb1, Υ→χbJ等）的馈赠贡献；使用多组LDMEs进行系统不确定性分析。

Result: χbJ(nP)的归一化动量分数z分布呈现强普适性：几乎不依赖于n=1,2,3、J=0,1,2及LDME选取，主因是^3S_1^[8]碎裂通道压倒主导且馈赠贡献小；而χcJ的z分布形状对LDME集敏感，反映^3S_1^[8]与^3P_J^[1]通道竞争；给出CMS与LHCb运动学区间内各J、n态的微分截面预言，并与PYTHIA8模拟对比。

Conclusion: 喷注内P波底偶素产生由单一致主导机制控制，验证了FJF+NRQCD在重夸克系统中的鲁棒性；而粲偶素表现出更复杂的Fock态结构，凸显其作为检验NRQCD色八重态机制与S-P波干涉的理想探针；结果为LHC实验分辨不同产生机制、标定LDMEs提供了关键理论基准。

Abstract: We present the first systematic theoretical study of the production of $P$-wave charmonium states $\chi_{cJ}$ and bottomonium states $\chi_{bJ}(nP)$ within jets at the LHC using the fragmenting jet function framework at next-to-leading order in $\alpha_s$. Our calculation resums both collinear DGLAP logarithms and threshold double logarithms in the fragmentation functions and accounts for feeddown contributions from higher quarkonium states. We observe a notable contrast between the charmonium and bottomonium sectors. For $\chi_{bJ}(nP)$, the normalized momentum-fraction distributions exhibit a nearly universal shape, with remarkably weak dependence on the choice of long-distance matrix element sets, the radial excitation ($n=1,2,3$), and the total angular momentum ($J=0,1,2$). We attribute this universality to the overwhelming dominance of the ${}^3S_1^{[8]}$ fragmentation channel, while feeddown contributions from higher bottomonium states remain small. In contrast, the shape of $\chi_{cJ}$ distributions exhibits a stronger dependence on the matrix-element set, reflecting the different relative contributions of the ${}^3S_1^{[8]}$ and ${}^3P_J^{[1]}$ channels. We provide predictions for the $\chi_{cJ}$ and $\chi_{bJ}(nP)$ distributions for the CMS and LHCb kinematic selections across a range of jet transverse momenta. We also compare our predictions with \textsc{Pythia}~8 simulations for selected $P$-wave quarkonium states.

</details>


### [168] [Searching for axion-like particles with space-based optical lattice clock networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39725)
*Yu-Song Cao,Nick Houston,YanXia Liu,QingZheng Lv,She-Sheng Xue*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出利用空间光学晶格钟网络（SOLCN）通过宇宙双折射效应探测轴子类粒子（ALPs）暗物质，聚焦于质量范围10^{-18.55}–10^{-14} eV的ALPs，借助激光链路频率调制与交叉关联分析，显著提升对ALP-光子耦合常数g_{aγ}的灵敏度，可达10^{-16} GeV^{-1}量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ALPs是具有良好理论动机的暗物质候选者及超出标准模型物理的重要探针，但其极弱相互作用导致直接探测极为困难。

Method: 将原用于探测随机引力波背景（SGWB）的空间光学晶格钟网络（SOLCN）稍加改造，利用其激光链路探测ALP诱导的宇宙双折射效应，并通过多节点间信号的交叉关联分析提取长程相关性。

Result: 在ALP质量区间10^{-18}–10^{-14} eV内，SOLCN可达到g_{aγ} ≥ 10^{-12} GeV^{-1}的探测灵敏度；当链路频率提升至8 GHz时，灵敏度可进一步优化至g_{aγ} ≥ 10^{-16} GeV^{-1}。

Conclusion: SOLCN是一种新颖且高灵敏的ALP暗物质探测平台，其性能强烈依赖于激光链路频率，为超轻质量轴子物理开辟了基于精密时频测量的新实验范式。

Abstract: Axion like particles (ALPs) are particularly well motivated candidates for dark matter, and physics beyond the Standard Model more generally.
