However, direct detection remains extremely challenging due to the weakness of their interactions. In this work, we propose to employ space-based optical lattice clock networks (SOLCN), originally targeted to probe the stochastic gravitational wave background (SGWB), to detect axion dark matter through cosmic birefringence via minor modifications to their laser links. Searching for cross-correlated signals then provides significant sensitivity to low mass ALPs as a result of their large correlation lengths. In this work we focus on ALPs in the mass range $10^{-18.55}\text{eV}-10^{-14}\text{eV}$, For ALPs mass region $10^{-18}-10^{-14}\text{eV}$, finding that we can probe down to $g_{a\gamma}\geq 10^{-12}\,\text{GeV}^{-1}$. Moreover, we find that the SOLCN sensitivity to the ALP-photon coupling depends crucially on the frequency of the network link. With an $8$ GHz frequency link, we then have the potential to reach $g_{a\gamma}\geq10^{-16}\,\text{GeV}^{-1}$ in this region.

</details>


### [169] [Circular Axion String Loop Simulations: Field Theory and Effective Description](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37764)
*Mathieu Kaltschmidt,Javier Redondo,Ivan Rybak,Kenichi Saikawa,Iker Sanz-González*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究轴子弦在闵可夫斯基背景中静态圆环坍缩的动力学，对比场论模拟（复标量模型与Klaer-Moore高张力模型）与重整化Nambu-Goto-Kalb-Ramond（NGKR）方程的解，发现两者均趋近Nambu-Goto极限，但NGKR对半衰期的1/𝒯修正比场论大20–35%；红外辐射谱一致，紫外区出现额外结构，归因于出射波前与环反弹等动力学事件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决先前工作中关于轴子弦动力学与辐射行为存在分歧的问题，建立可控、精确的基准模型以厘清轴子弦宇宙学演化中的关键物理。

Method: 采用数值场论模拟（常规复标量场与Klaer-Moore高张力模型）与重整化Nambu-Goto-Kalb-Ramond（NGKR）有效弦动力学方程进行对比，在无量纲弦张力3 ≲ 𝒯 ≲ 100范围内系统分析坍缩过程的时间尺度与辐射谱。

Result: 1) 场论与NGKR均呈现1/𝒯修正的Nambu-Goto极限行为；2) NGKR对半衰期的1/𝒯修正比场论大20–35%；3) 大张力下NGKR结果与独立微扰计算吻合至0.2%；4) 辐射谱在红外区一致，紫外区出现由出射波前和初始环反弹导致的额外结构。

Conclusion: NGKR在红外辐射与大张力极限下高度可靠，但对动力学时间尺度（如半衰期）的1/𝒯修正存在系统性偏差，表明其在中等张力下的有效描述需进一步修正；紫外辐射结构揭示了场论中非NG型动力学效应的重要性，为宇宙学子午网络模拟提供关键基准。

Abstract: An accurate description of axion strings and their radiation is crucial for understanding their cosmological implications. Motivated by the differing conclusions of previous works, we study a simple, controlled setup: the collapse of an initially static circular loop in a Minkowski background. We compare field-theory simulations of the conventional complex scalar and high-tension Klaer--Moore models with solutions of the renormalised Nambu--Goto--Kalb--Ramond (NGKR) equation of motion, covering the range $3 \lesssim \mathcal{T} \lesssim 100$ in dimensionless string tension. Both the field theory and NGKR approach the Nambu--Goto (NG) limit with corrections scaling as $1/\mathcal{T}$. However, the leading correction to the halving-time is $20-35\%$ larger in NGKR than in the field-theory simulations over the same range of tensions, while for large tensions the value agrees with an independent perturbative calculation to $0.2\%$. The late-time axion spectrum agrees with NGKR in the infrared, but the collapse generates additional structure in the ultraviolet part of the spectrum, which we attribute to different events such as an outgoing wave front that carries a sizable fraction of the energy or the rebound of the initial loop. These results provide a useful benchmark for a more accurate understanding of axion radiation from cosmological string networks.

</details>


### [170] [Gauge-parameter cancellation and finite form factors in one-loop quark scattering in the refined Gribov-Zwanziger framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38463)
*Wan Wu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文计算了在局部BRST不变的精炼Gribov-Zwanziger理论中，不同味无质量夸克散射的一圈振幅，包含约束行列式与两种动力学夸克味，系统处理了紫外与红外发散，并给出完整有限结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决标准微扰QCD在低动量区因Gribov拷贝导致的规范固定不确定性，通过精炼Gribov-Zwanziger框架实现红外可控的夸克散射振幅计算。

Method: 采用局部BRST不变的精炼Gribov-Zwanziger作用量，含两个动力学夸克味与Faddeev-Popov约束行列式；在自由场传播子矩阵中保留RGZ质量参数；统一处理混合自能修正、完整顶点与盒子图贡献及外线波函数重整；使用维度正规化与Stueckelberg调节器控制规范依赖性；严格分离UV/IR极点并保留尺度无关积分中的隐藏贡献。

Result: 获得完全有限的一圈夸克-夸克散射振幅，表示为8个色-自旋结构因子，由30个标量积分构成，全部144个有限函数系数以电子形式提供；结果定义在指定解析分支上，不依赖有色渐近态假设。

Conclusion: 该振幅是首个在精炼Gribov-Zwanziger框架下严格满足局部BRST对称性、同时闭合UV重整与IR抵消的多味夸克散射一阶结果，为非微扰QCD中部分子动力学提供了可计算基准。

Abstract: We calculate the one-loop amplitude for distinct-flavor massless quark scattering in a local BRST-invariant refined Gribov-Zwanziger theory with minimally coupled quarks. The constraint determinant and two dynamical quark flavors are included in the action and in the loop sum. Retaining the refined Gribov-Zwanziger masses in the free field matrix, we combine the mixed self-energy insertion, complete vertex and box corrections, and external-leg residues. The difference between a general linear covariant gauge and a reference gauge vanishes symbolically after removal of the Stueckelberg regulator in the specified dimensional prescription. We separate ultraviolet and infrared poles, retaining contributions hidden by scaleless integrals and by the order of mass and dimensional limits. Local modified-minimal-subtraction counterterms close the ultraviolet poles, and the remaining infrared poles cancel in the stated nonzero-mass domain. The finite answer is supplied as eight color-spin form factors over a thirty-entry scalar integral inventory, with all 144 finite-function coefficients provided electronically. The result defines a partonic amplitude on a specified analytic branch; it does not assume colored asymptotic states or establish an inclusive next-to-leading-order cross section.

</details>


### [171] [Two-loop BRST-restoring finite counter-terms for chiral non-abelian gauge theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38618)
*Achilleas Lazopoulos,Vera Paroutiadou*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了无标量场的反常自由手征SU(N)规范理论在二维圈阶下的重整化，采用BMHV方案处理维数正规化中的γ5，推导出恢复BRST对称性的有限对称性修复抵消项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在手征规范理论中，维数正规化下γ5的处理会破坏BRST对称性，需引入有限抵消项以恢复该对称性；本文旨在系统构造适用于一般（可能可约）费米子表示的两圈阶BRST恢复项。

Method: 采用Breitenlohner–Maison–'t Hooft–Veltman (BMHV) 方案处理γ5，在维度d ≠ 4下定义γ5并保持其反对易关系，结合背景场方法与Slavnov–Taylor恒等式约束，通过两圈Feynman图计算及代数分析导出所需有限抵消项。

Result: 给出了任意（可能可约）SU(N)费米子表示下、两圈精度的显式BRST对称性恢复有限抵消项，确保量子有效作用量满足Slavnov–Taylor恒等式，从而保证物理S矩阵的幺正性与规范无关性。

Conclusion: BMHV方案虽引入BRST破缺，但可通过唯一确定的有限局部抵消项在两圈阶完全恢复BRST对称性；该结果为手征规范理论的高阶微扰计算（如精确β函数、异常匹配）提供了自洽的重整化框架。

Abstract: We study the renormalization of anomaly-free chiral $SU(N)$ gauge theories without scalar fields through two-loop order. We adopt the Breitenlohner--Maison--'t Hooft--Veltman (BMHV) scheme for the treatment of $\gamma_5$ in dimensional regularization. This prescription induces spurious breaking of BRST symmetry, requiring finite symmetry-restoring counter-terms in addition to the subtraction of ultraviolet divergences. We derive these counter-terms for a general, possibly reducible fermion representation, thus restoring the BRST symmetry, through two loops.

</details>


### [172] [Quantum Observables of Nonperturbative Superradiant Scattering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38690)
*Asimina Arvanitaki,Savas Dimopoulos,Marios Galanis,Derek J. Li*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种处理N个相干自旋（或任意二能级系统）非弹性散射的非微扰理论，指出其散射截面随N²增长，导致大N下Born近似失效；该超辐射散射及其对自旋的反作用被系统研究，并发现能量转移率与能量涨落率仍保持N²标度，除非守恒律限制每次碰撞仅交换一个量子；理论应用于毫电荷粒子搜寻及中子/带电粒子束探测宇宙遗迹等前沿实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Born近似在大量相干两态系统（如自旋阵列）散射中失效，亟需非微扰框架描述强相干效应与反作用；同时，超辐射散射在暗物质与精密测量实验中具有关键信号意义。

Method: 基于量子多体散射理论与超辐射动力学，构建非微扰S矩阵形式，严格处理单粒子与N体相干靶之间的多次量子交换过程，并引入能量转移率与方差率的算符表达以刻画背反应。

Result: 证明超辐射非弹性散射截面∝N²且饱和；能量转移率Γ_E ∝ N²、能量方差率Γ_{E²} ∝ N²仍保持二次标度；仅当存在严格守恒律（如U(1)电荷守恒）限制Δn=±1时，标度退化为N。

Conclusion: 超辐射散射是大N相干靶中不可忽略的非微扰量子现象；其持续N²标度的能量动力学为毫电荷粒子、中子干涉仪及宇宙遗迹探测提供了新灵敏度范式。

Abstract: Inelastic scattering from $N$ coherent spins, or any two-state system, scales as $N^2$, invalidating the Born approximation at large $N$ even at weak coupling. We develop a nonperturbative theory of this superradiant scattering and its backreaction on the spins. Since one particle can exchange many quanta, the target's energy-transfer and energy-variance rates keep their favorable $N^2$ scaling even after the cross section saturates, unless a conservation law limits each encounter to exchange of one quantum. We apply the theory to millicharged-particle searches, as well as neutron and charged-particle beams that can test quantum protocols for detecting cosmic relics.

</details>


### [173] [Nucleon Wave Functions from QCD Vacuum Topology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39215)
*Wei-Yang Liu,Ismail Zahed*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于瞬子液体模型（ILM），构建了由瞬子诱导的夸克-夸克关联驱动的核子微观描述；通过Bethe-Salpeter-Faddeev方程求解，得到与实验高度一致的核子质量（939.53 MeV）和轴向耦合常数（g_A = 1.265），并导出光前三夸克波函数及相应的部分子分布函数，揭示QCD真空拓扑结构对核子质量、自旋与价夸克结构的统一微观起源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解QCD真空拓扑（如瞬子）如何微观地决定核子基本性质（质量、自旋、手征结构与部分子分布），弥补传统唯象模型缺乏第一性原理基础的缺陷。

Method: 在瞬子液体模型框架下，推导单瞬子与瞬子-反瞬子分子产生的二夸克（diquark）相互作用核（标量通道强吸引，轴矢通道弱关联）；将其嵌入协变、置换对称的Bethe-Salpeter-Faddeev方程求解核子束缚态；再解析延拓至光前，提取三夸克波函数，并计算未极化、螺旋度与横极化部分子分布及其QCD演化矩。

Result: 获得核子质量m_N = 939.53 MeV；波函数含98%标量二夸克+2%轴矢二夸克成分；小轴矢组分产生显著自旋-同位旋干涉，给出g_A = 1.265；导出的协变光前波函数支持与格点QCD及全局拟合数据的定量比对。

Conclusion: QCD真空的瞬子拓扑结构不仅生成手征对称性破缺与U_A(1)反常，还直接组织核子的质量谱、自旋结构与价夸克分布，为构建包含禁闭与高Fock成分的完整核子理论提供了关键微观基石。

Abstract: Instantons, as topologically nontrivial gauge field configurations, provide a microscopic mechanism for chiral symmetry breaking and the $U_A(1)$ anomaly in QCD. Within the instanton liquid model (ILM), instantons generate multi-fermion interactions governed by their density and size. Using this framework, we develop a microscopic description of the nucleon based on instanton-induced quark--quark correlations. We derive the diquark interaction kernels arising from single instantons and instanton--anti-instanton molecules, revealing a strong attraction in the scalar channel and weaker correlations in the spin-one channels. Embedding these interactions in a covariant, permutation-symmetric Bethe--Salpeter--Faddeev equation yields a nucleon mass $m_N=939.53~{\rm MeV}$, with a predominantly scalar-diquark component ($98\%$) and a small axial-vector admixture ($2\%$) around the mass pole. Despite its small weight, the small admixture generates substantial spin--isospin interference, yielding an axial charge $g_A=1.265$, close to experiment. The resulting covariant amplitude is analytically continued to the light front to obtain the leading three-quark wave function. The corresponding unpolarized, helicity, and transversity parton distributions, along with their QCD-evolved moments, provide a direct connection to lattice QCD calculations and global-fit analyses. Our results show that the topology of the QCD vacuum organizes not only the nucleon mass and spin structure, but also its leading valence partonic content, providing a microscopic foundation for a more complete description including confinement and higher Fock components.

</details>


### [174] [Heavy-Particle Effective Theory: from On-Shell Formalism to Gravitational Compton Amplitudes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40263)
*Yong-Kang Li,Yi-Ning Wang,Jiang-Hao Yu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于速度投影自旋螺旋变量的壳上重粒子有效理论（HPET）振幅构造方法，通过SU(2)小群投影实现重粒子动力学的系统幂级数展开，并成功复现Kerr黑洞的自旋诱导多极矩及经典引力散射结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为统一处理任意自旋重粒子在软动量极限下的有效相互作用，尤其是实现重粒子有效理论中重参数化不变性与幂计数的一致性，并避免传统外推方法引入的虚假极点。

Method: 将自旋螺旋变量投影到类时参考速度v^μ所定义的SU(2)小群上，保留速度投影后的大小分量以构建中间振幅；施加重参数化不变性后积分掉小分量，导出符合HPET幂计数的局域算符对应振幅；利用三点半振幅构造型地构造四点引力Compton振幅。

Result: 得到任意自旋S、等质量两粒子与软无质量玻色子（螺旋度h）耦合的三点半速度投影振幅；构造出无虚假极点（至次领头阶）的HPET引力Compton振幅；证明速度投影最小耦合精确复现Kerr解的自旋诱导多极矩至2S阶；树图弹性引力散射与Compton振幅的经典极限与文献一致（接触项除外）。

Conclusion: 速度投影自旋螺旋方法为高自旋重粒子的有效理论提供了严格、协变且可计算的壳上框架，兼具重参数化不变性、清晰的幂计数结构和经典极限的自动一致性，是连接量子振幅与经典引力物理（如自旋效应）的关键桥梁。

Abstract: We formulate on-shell heavy-particle effective theory (HPET) amplitudes by projecting the spinor-helicity variables onto the $SU(2)$ little group of a timelike reference velocity $v^{\mu}$. Retaining both large and small components of the velocity-projected spinors gives an intermediate velocity-projected amplitudes in which reparameterization invariance can be imposed before expanding in the inverse heavy-particle mass. Integrating out the small component then yields HPET amplitudes organized by heavy-particle power counting, with a direct correspondence to local operators at fixed order. We determine the three-point velocity-projected amplitudes for two equal-mass particles of arbitrary spin $S$ coupled to a soft massless boson of helicity $h$. Four-point gravitational Compton amplitudes can be constructively obtained using velocity-projected three-point amplitudes, and expanded into HPET amplitudes which is free of spurious poles up to sub-leading order. As a gravitational application, we show that the projected gravitational minimal coupling reproduces the Kerr spin-induced multipoles up to order $2S$, and find the classical limits of the tree-level elastic gravitational scattering and gravitational Compton amplitudes agreed with the literature up to contact terms undetermined by factorization.

</details>


### [175] [A Lighter QCD-Axion from Small Instantons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40629)
*Sourav Bera,Susobhan Chattopadhyay,Rick S. Gupta*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出两种新模型，通过紫外区修正QCD和标准模型味结构，使QCD轴子质量可显著低于传统预期；关键机制是紫外瞬子对轴子质量与上夸克Yukawa耦合的同时非微扰贡献，当瞬子主导上夸克质量生成时，轴子变轻；模型包括多SU(3)群破缺出QCD及部分复合Higgs模型，拓展了轴子质量允许区域至QCD轴子带左侧，增强与未来haloscope/helioscope实验的关联性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决QCD轴子质量通常被约束在窄带（QCD轴子带）的问题，探索其质量显著偏离标准预测（尤其是更轻）的可能性，以拓宽实验探测窗口并检验紫外物理新结构。

Method: 构建两类紫外完备模型：(1) QCD规范群由多个SU(3)因子破缺产生，(2) 实现部分复合性的复合Higgs模型；系统计算紫外瞬子对axion potential和up-quark Yukawa的非微扰修正，结合anomaly matching与effective field theory分析轴子质量标度依赖。

Result: 证明当紫外瞬子贡献主导上夸克Yukawa耦合时，QCD轴子质量可远低于标准~μeV量级（即移至QCD轴子带左侧），否则反而更重；两模型均支持轴子质量跨越数个数量级，尤其覆盖当前haloscope（如ADMX、HAYSTAC）与helioscope（如IAXO）正计划探测的亚-μeV区域。

Conclusion: QCD轴子质量并非普适固定值，其尺度高度依赖于紫外完成方式；通过瞬子诱导的味结构与强相互作用协同修正，可自然实现轻轴子解，为突破传统轴子天体物理约束、连接高能理论与前沿实验提供新范式。

Abstract: We propose models where the QCD axion mass can be much smaller than the standard expectation. We show that this is possible in models where both QCD and the Standard Model flavor structure get modified in the ultraviolet (UV). In these models, both the axion mass and the up-quark Yukawa can receive sizeable non-perturbative contributions from instantons in the UV. If both these effects are carefully taken into account, the QCD axion is not necessarily heavier than usual expectations and can, in fact, be much lighter. We show that if small instanton contributions account for a large fraction of the up-quark Yukawa, the QCD axion can be lighter than standard expectations whereas it is heavier otherwise. We propose two different models where this condition is realised: (1) one where the QCD gauge group emerges as a subgroup from a product of $SU(3)$ groups in the UV; and (2) a composite Higgs model that also implements partial compositeness. In both these models the QCD axion can lie not only in the region to the right of the QCD axion band but also far to its left. This is especially relevant for a range of proposed haloscope and helioscope experiments that will probe a region mostly to the left of the QCD band.

</details>


### [176] [The double-real part of NNLO matching with logarithmically accurate showers for $e^+e^-$ collisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37749)
*Melissa van Beekveld,Keith Hamilton,Gavin P. Salam,Nicolas Schalch,Gregory Soyez,Silvia Zanoli*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文讨论了在高阶匹配中保持部分子喷注对数精度的关键原则，特别关注匹配承担前n次辐射责任的情形；提出了一种针对e+e−碰撞和PanScales喷注的匹配方案，包含领头色双实辐射O(α_s²)贡献，并利用新推导的喷注逆变换表达式与红外极限技术，使喷注可作为全相空间生成器，为对数精确的NNLO匹配奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确保高阶匹配不破坏指数化行为、避免产生虚假的领头色超领头对数项（super-leading logarithms），从而维持部分子喷注的对数精度。

Method: 基于PanScales部分子喷注，推导任意发射数下的喷注逆变换新解析表达式，结合近期发展的喷注高阶红外极限技术，构造包含O(α_s²)领头色双实辐射贡献的匹配方案。

Result: 实现了对e+e−过程的对数精确高阶匹配，使PanScales喷注可作为全相空间生成器，并为NNLO级对数精确匹配提供了关键方法学基础。

Conclusion: 严格遵循匹配中保留指数化与色结构一致性的原理是避免超领头对数项的必要条件；本文所构建的匹配框架是迈向对数精确NNLO匹配的重要一步。

Abstract: We discuss key principles that need to be taken into account for higher-order matching to retain the logarithmic accuracy of parton showers, specifically in a context where the matching takes full responsibility for the first $n$ emissions. Failure to respect these principles breaks exponentiation and generates spurious leading-colour super-leading logarithms. Focusing on $e^+e^-$ collisions, and the PanScales showers, we construct a matching procedure that incorporates the leading-colour double-real, $\mathcal{O}(\alpha_s^2)$, contributions. It relies on new expressions for the inversion of these showers for any number of emissions, and extends techniques developed recently for the higher-order infrared limits of showers. Together, these ingredients allow us to use our showers as full phase-space generators. Our work establishes an important stepping stone towards logarithmically accurate NNLO matching.

</details>


### [177] [A High-Energy Neutrino Collider within a Muon Collider](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37756)
*Innes Bigaran,André de Gouvêa,Tao Han,Ian Low,Yang Ma,Zahra Tabrizi,Keping Xie*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了多TeV级缪子对撞机作为高能中微子对撞机的潜力，指出缪子束辐射软W±玻色子可产生μν、νν̄等碰撞过程，这些过程由电弱SU(2)对称性关联，共同探测电弱动力学及超出标准模型的新物理；通过有效场论与W′±/Z′简化模型分析表明，中微子引发的过程与带电轻子过程具有互补性，显著提升对有效算符简并性的分辨能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索多TeV缪子对撞机中由缪子束辐射软W±玻色子所诱导的高能中微子-轻子及中微子-中微子散射过程，以拓展对电弱相互作用和新物理（如W′±、Z′）的探测维度。

Method: 基于电弱SU(2)规范对称性，系统分析主导的纯轻子散射过程：μ⁺μ⁻→ℓ⁺ℓ′⁻、νₘᵤμ⁺→ν_ℓℓ′⁺、ν̄ₘᵤμ⁻→ν̄_ℓℓ′⁻、νₘᵤν̄ₘᵤ→ℓ⁺ℓ′⁻；结合辐射返程机制（radiative return），在质量允许时实现W′±/Z′共振产生，并用对应有效算符描述其离壳效应；采用有效场论（EFT）与简化模型联合分析灵敏度与通道互补性。

Result: 中微子引发过程与带电轻子过程具有强互补性；即使某些有效算符不直接耦合中微子初态，引入中微子通道仍能显著缓解算符间简并；辐射返程机制使W′±/Z′可在其质量阈值以下被间接探知；联合分析大幅提升新物理结构（如手征性、规范群表示）的刻画精度。

Conclusion: 多TeV缪子对撞机不仅是高能缪子对撞平台，更是迄今最可控、最高能量的中微子对撞实验室；中微子-initiated轻子散射是解析电弱对称性破缺本质与新物理紫外结构不可或缺的探针；必须将此类通道纳入整体分析框架，方能无偏地重构新物理的有效拉氏量。

Abstract: A multi-TeV muon collider is simultaneously a collider for high-energy neutrinos. The radiation of soft $W^\pm$ gauge bosons from the muon beams gives rise to collisions of $\mu^+\nu$, $\mu^-\bar{\nu}$ and $\nu\bar{\nu}$ at center-of-mass energies close to that of the $\mu^+\mu^-$ collisions in a controlled and theoretically predictable setting. We focus on the leading partonic processes $\mu^+\mu^- \to \ell^+\ell'^-$, $\nu_\mu\mu^+\to \nu_\ell \ell'^+$, $\bar{\nu}_\mu\mu^- \to \bar{\nu}_{\ell} \ell'^-$ and $\nu_\mu\bar \nu_\mu \to \ell^+\ell'^-$ ($\ell, \ell'=e, \mu, \tau$). These are related to one another at high energies by the electroweak $SU(2)$ gauge symmetry, and therefore probe different components of the same underlying electroweak dynamics. These processes open qualitatively new windows to neutrino interactions at unprecedented energies, and open new windows on physics beyond the Standard Model. We demonstrate the combined sensitivity and the complementarity of the different purely-leptonic collisions using both effective field theory and simplified models of $W'^\pm$ and $Z'$ vector bosons. The radiative return mechanism leads to resonant production of the $W^{\prime\pm}$ and $Z'$ vector bosons when their masses are kinematically accessible, while their off-shell effects are captured by the corresponding effective operators. In particular, we show that supplementing the dominant charged-lepton collisions with neutrino-initiated collisions significantly improves the ability to resolve degeneracies among different effective operators, even for operators to which the neutrino-initiated processes are not directly sensitive. Including these channels is therefore essential for properly characterizing the structure of new physics.

</details>


### [178] [Testing thermal equilibrium in heavy-ion collisions with electric-charge fluctuations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37829)
*Peter Christiansen*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了在重离子碰撞中通过净电荷涨落（特别是二阶和四阶电荷敏感度比值 χ₄/χ₂）检验热化QCD假设的新实验策略，重点解决有限探测器接受度对测量的影响，并提出利用电荷平衡函数和带电粒子快度密度进行系统效率校正的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重离子碰撞中观测到的类热现象在小系统中也存在，且可用非热微观模型描述，这质疑了夸克-胶子等离子体热化的普适性；同时格点QCD计算的电荷敏感度与HRG模型存在显著张力，亟需高精度实验验证。

Method: 将二阶净电荷累积量 κ₂(ΔQ) 重写为电荷平衡函数 B(y) 与带电粒子快度密度 dN_ch/dy 的积分形式，从而将有限接受度下的实验测量转化为可系统修正的事件平均观测量。

Result: 建立了 κ₂(ΔQ) 与实验可观测的平衡函数及快度密度之间的严格关系，为 D-量（D = 4κ₂/⟨N_ch⟩）的精确测量与理论对比提供了可控的实验路径，并为推广至四阶累积量奠定基础。

Conclusion: 通过重构净电荷涨落观测量，可有效控制探测器接受度效应，使格点QCD预言的 χ₄/χ₂ 张力得以在实验上严格检验，从而判定中心重离子碰撞是否真正实现热化QCD物态。

Abstract: Relativistic heavy-ion collisions exhibit several phenomena commonly associated with the formation of a thermalized quark--gluon plasma. However, the observation of similar phenomena in small systems, and their non-thermal microscopic descriptions there, raises questions about this assumption. Recent continuum-extrapolated lattice-QCD calculations of the second- and fourth-order electric-charge susceptibilities, $\chi_{2}^{\Delta Q}$ and $\chi_{4}^{\Delta Q}$, reveal a striking tension with hadron-resonance-gas expectations, particularly for $\chi_{4}^{\Delta Q}$. As the ratio $\chi_{4}^{\Delta Q}/\chi_{2}^{\Delta Q}$ is experimentally measurable with net-charge cumulants, this opens up completely new ways to test whether thermal QCD applies in central heavy-ion collisions. However, achieving a measurement that can be interpreted with high precision requires further work on both the phenomenological and experimental sides. Here, the focus is on an experimental counterpart to recent phenomenological work on the $D$-measure of net-charge fluctuations: how restricted detector acceptance in rapidity and transverse momentum can be controlled experimentally. We demonstrate this for the second-order net-charge cumulant, $\kappa_2(\Delta Q)$, by rewriting it in terms of the charge balance function and the charged-particle rapidity density. Both are event-averaged observables for which experimental efficiency corrections and extrapolations into unmeasured kinematic regions can be studied systematically. This provides a complementary experimental route for connecting measurements of $D = 4\kappa_2(\Delta Q)/\langle N_{ch} \rangle$ to theory and a possible starting point for extending the same strategy to the fourth-order cumulant.

</details>


### [179] [Meson-baryon final-state interactions in the $ψ(3686)\to pK^-K_S^0\barΞ^0$ decay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37922)
*F. C. Sobrinho,W.-T. Lyu,E. Oset*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了ψ(3686) → p K⁻ K̄⁰ Ξ̄⁰衰变，基于SU(3)对称性构建轻强子末态的singlet结构，结合S波末态相互作用与Λ(1520)、Λ(1670)、Σ(1430)等共振态的D波贡献，成功拟合全部六个不变质量谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索含重夸克的粲偶素衰变中轻强子末态的SU(3)对称性实现机制，拓展对多体末态中共振态与末态相互作用协同效应的理解，弥补传统baryon-antibaryon + single pseudoscalar研究的不足。

Method: 将ψ(3686)视为c c̄单态，利用SU(3)群论构造p K⁻ K̄⁰ Ξ̄⁰对应的三个独立SU(3)单态结构；保留能通过S波末态相互作用生成该末态的项；显式引入Λ(1520)（D波）、Λ(1670)（K⁻p通道）和Σ(1430)（K̄⁰p通道）的共振贡献；对六维不变质量分布进行联合拟合。

Result: 拟合结果良好复现全部六个不变质量谱；确认Λ(1520)主导D波贡献；发现Λ(1670)在K⁻p谱中有微弱但可分辨的FSI诱导贡献；Σ(1430)显著扭曲K̄⁰p谱，偏离相空间分布。

Conclusion: ψ(3686) → B B̄ M M' 类多体衰变是检验SU(3)对称性、共振态耦合及末态相互作用的重要探针；本工作证实此类过程可提供传统二体或三体衰变无法获得的动力学信息，为重强子谱学与非微扰QCD建模提供新约束。

Abstract: We study the decay $\psi(3686)\to p K^- \bar K^0 \bar\Xi^0$, treating the $\psi(3686)$ as a $c\bar c$ state and therefore as a singlet under SU(3) transformations in the light-quark sector. We consider all possible combinations of a baryon, an antibaryon, and two pseudoscalar mesons that form an SU(3) singlet, retaining the terms that can lead to the $p K^- \bar K^0 \bar\Xi^0$ final state through $S$-wave final-state interactions. Three independent SU(3)-singlet structures are identified and assigned different weights. We also include the contribution of the $\Lambda(1520)$ in the meson-baryon $D$-wave. A fit to the data provides a good description of all six invariant-mass distributions. In addition to the $\Lambda(1520)$ contribution, we find a small contribution from the $\Lambda(1670)$ in the $K^-p$ invariant-mass distribution, generated by final-state interactions, and a contribution from the $\Sigma(1430)$, which produces a significant distortion of the $\bar K^0p$ invariant-mass distribution relative to phase space. This study demonstrates the value of investigating charmonium decays into a baryon-antibaryon pair and two pseudoscalar mesons, complementing the more commonly studied decays into a baryon-antibaryon pair and a single pseudoscalar meson.

</details>


### [180] [Baryon-Number Violating $Λ_c$ Decays from Light-Cone Sum Rules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37987)
*Ali Mohamed*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了Λc重子衰变为π/K介子加不可见费米子χ的重子数破缺过程，利用光锥求和规则计算相关强子矩阵元，给出分支比估计约为10^{-7}量级，并讨论了STCF、Belle II与FCC-ee实验的探测前景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索超出标准模型的新物理，特别是通过重子数破缺但整体重子数守恒（由赋予χ适当重子数实现）的不可见衰变道，以探测夸克与轻不可见费米子之间的新相互作用。

Method: 采用光锥求和规则（LCSR），结合π/K介子的分布振幅，计算Λc→πχ̄与Λc→Kχ̄的强子矩阵元；包含两粒子贡献至扭度四（twist-4）及可因子化的四粒子项；使用两种Λc插值流评估流依赖性。

Result: 在基于对撞机限制的单算符基准下，得到分支比O(10^{-7})，部分相空间可达10^{-6}量级；结果为光锥展开截断下的指示性估计；与ChPT及QCD因子化结果进行了比较。

Conclusion: Λc→π/K+missing energy 是探测三夸克-单态费米子维度六算符的理想探针；STCF与Belle II可覆盖部分预言区间，FCC-ee有望探测更低分支比（~10^{-8}或更小）。

Abstract: Baryon-number-violating decays with missing energy probe new interactions connecting Standard-Model quarks to light invisible fermions. Such signatures can arise from dimension-six operators coupling three quarks to an SM-singlet fermion $\chi$, which escapes detection. With a suitable baryon-number assignment for $\chi$, total baryon number is conserved while its violation is apparent in the visible sector. We study the exclusive decays $\Lambda_c\to\pi\bar\chi$ and $\Lambda_c\to K\bar\chi$, determining the required form factors using light-cone sum rules with pion and kaon distribution amplitudes. The calculation includes leading-order two-particle contributions up to twist four and factorisable four-particle terms. Two $\Lambda_c$ interpolating currents are employed to assess the current dependence. Using single-operator benchmarks based on collider bounds, we obtain branching fractions of order $10^{-7}$ over sizeable parts of the physical region, with uncertainty envelopes reaching $10^{-6}$ in some scenarios. These are indicative estimates within the retained light-cone expansion. We compare with existing chiral-perturbation-theory and QCD-factorisation estimates and discuss experimental prospects. Future data from STCF and Belle II could probe part of the predicted range, while FCC-ee could reach smaller branching fractions.

</details>


### [181] [Vector- and tensor-meson resonance contributions to $χ_{cJ}\to PPV$ decays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38178)
*Yuan-Guo Xu,Qin-Ze Song,Bo Lan,Ru-Min Wang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了轻矢量介子和张量介子共振态对χcJ→P1P2V三体衰变的贡献，采用准二体框架结合SU(3)味对称性分析VV→P1P2V和TP→P1P2V两类子过程，并分别考虑窄宽近似（NWA）与有限宽度效应（FWE），后者对运动学禁戒但通过共振高质尾部贡献的张量通道尤为关键。预言的共振带强度跨越多个数量级，其中ππρ、πK̄K∗、K̄Kϕ最强；而K̄Kω与η′KK∗则主要由张量带主导，构成探测T→PV张量共振的干净探针。结果可为未来不变质量谱与振幅分析提供先验强度分布与道优先级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解χcJ三体强子衰变中矢量/张量共振态的动力学贡献，特别是区分VV与TP机制，并解决张量共振因运动学禁戒而仅通过有限宽度尾部贡献的关键问题。

Method: 采用准二体分解框架，结合SU(3)味对称性约束二体子过程振幅；系统比较窄宽近似（NWA）与包含Breit–Wigner线形积分的有限宽度效应（FWE）处理，尤其针对T→PV子过程。

Result: 预言了各P1P2V终态中V与T共振带的相对强度与能谱分布：ππρ、πK̄K∗、K̄Kϕ以矢量带主导；K̄Kω与η′KK∗则张量带压倒性主导；整体强度跨度达数个数量级。

Conclusion: FWE对张量通道不可或缺；K̄Kω与η′KK∗是实验提取T→PV耦合的最优道；所给带强度排序可作为振幅分析的先验输入与道选择依据。

Abstract: Light vector- and tensor-meson resonance contributions to the three-body decays $\chi_{cJ}\to P_1P_2V$ are studied in a quasi-two-body framework combined with SU(3) flavor relations for the two-body subprocesses, covering both the $\chi_{c0,1,2}\to VV\to P_1P_2V$ and $\chi_{c1,2}\to TP\to P_1P_2V$. Both the narrow-width approximation (NWA) and the finite-width effects (FWE) treatment are considered, the latter being essential for tensor subdecays that are kinematically closed at the nominal mass and contribute only through the high-mass tail of the resonance. The predicted resonance bands span several orders of magnitude, with the largest ones in the $\pi\pi\rho$, $\pi K\bar K^{*}$, and $K\bar K\phi$ final states, whereas the $K\bar K\omega$ and $\eta'KK^{*}$ channels are dominated by the tensor band and thus provide a clean window onto the $T\to PV$ tensor resonances. The band-by-band strengths and their ordering are intended as priors and channel priorities for future invariant-mass and amplitude analyses, and can be compared with the measured totals of several channels.

</details>


### [182] [Spectra and strong decays of open charm-bottom $c\bar{b}q\bar{q}$ tetraquarks in an unequal-mass Born-Oppenheimer framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38217)
*Bowen Kang,Xi Xia,Tao Guo*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在Born-Oppenheimer近似下研究了S波开粲底四夸克态c\bar{b}q\bar{q}（q=u,d,s）的质量谱与强衰变行为，采用基于独立优化重轻轨道构建的有效势，处理了非等质量重夸克源；计算了0^+, 1^+, 2^+色单态谱，发现系统性味依赖位移与特征超精细劈裂，并估算了若干双体开味衰变道的部分宽度，为理论与实验探索提供基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓展对隐重味四夸克的研究至开粲底体系，解决非等质量重夸克（c与b）带来的动力学复杂性，并为实验上尚未观测的开放味四夸克提供可检验的谱学与衰变预言。

Method: Born-Oppenheimer近似；基于独立优化的重-轻轨道构造有效势；色八重子耦合成色单态；色-自旋重耦合+唯象衰变模型估算两体强衰变部分宽度。

Result: 获得c\bar{b}q\bar{q}（q=u,d,s）在J^P=0^+,1^+,2^+下的质量谱，呈现系统性味依赖位移与超精细劈裂；给出若干开味衰变道（如D^{(*)}B^{(*)}）的部分宽度及主导衰变模式。

Conclusion: 开粲底四夸克态具有清晰可分辨的谱结构与特征衰变行为，其理论预言具备实验可观测性；所发展的方法可推广至其他非对称重味多夸克体系。

Abstract: We investigate the mass spectra and strong decay channels of $S$-wave open charm-bottom $c\bar{b}q\bar{q}$ ($q = u,d,s$) tetraquarks within the Born-Oppenheimer approximation. Extending our previous study of hidden heavy flavor tetraquarks, we account for the unequal-mass heavy quark sources and construct the effective potential based on independently optimized heavy-light orbitals. This makes the computational method more generally applicable. For the quantum numbers $J^P=0^+$, $1^+$, and $2^+$, we have obtained the spectra of the open charm-bottom $c\bar{b}q\bar{q}$ tetraquarks corresponding to color-singlet states constructed by coupling two color octets. The predicted masses exhibit systematic flavor-dependent shifts and characteristic hyperfine splittings. We also estimate partial widths for selected two-body open flavor decay channels using color-spin recoupling and a phenomenological decay prescription. The resulting channel-dependent decay modes, together with comparisons to other theoretical predictions, provide useful benchmarks for future theoretical investigations and experimental searches for open charm-bottom tetraquarks.

</details>


### [183] [Tracing Electromagnetic Response through Charge Splitting of Directed Flow in the Quark-Gluon Plasma](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38363)
*Ankit Kumar Panda,Zhu Meng,Long-Gang Pang,Xin-Nian Wang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过(3+1)维粘滞流体力学模型，结合QGP演化中的电磁源项与Cooper-Frye粒子化过程的电磁修正，解释了带电粒子定向流差Δv₁在RHIC与LHC能量下的符号反转与幅值变化，并指出其源于电场与磁场诱导动量畸变的竞争，受电导率调控；模型进一步预言了π/K/p的质量依赖电荷劈裂，为QGP电磁响应与时空演化提供定量探针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释实验观测到的带电粒子定向流差Δv₁在中快度处的符号反转及其随碰撞能量（200 GeV → 5.02 TeV）的振幅变化，以探究QGP的电磁响应特性。

Method: (3+1)维粘滞流体力学框架，内嵌电磁场源项（Maxwell方程耦合到能量-动量张量演化），并在Cooper-Frye粒子化中引入洛伦兹力修正；电导率作为可调参数，匹配格点QCD预示的尺度。

Result: 成功复现实验Δv₁的符号反转与能量依赖行为；揭示反转源于电场（驱动同向偏转）与磁场（驱动反向偏转）动量畸变的竞争，由QGP电导率调控；预言π/K/p的荷质比依赖劈裂，约束其弛豫时间与电磁耦合强度。

Conclusion: Δv₁是QGP电磁响应与时空演化的定量探针；电导率是连接实验观测量与基础QCD输运性质的关键桥梁；质量依赖劈裂为区分不同夸克味贡献与强磁场下手征效应提供新途径。

Abstract: Experimentally observed charge splitting of directed flow, $\Delta v_1\equiv v_1(h^+)-v_1(h^-)$, exhibits both a sign reversal at midrapidity and the amplitude change from Au+Au collisions at $\sqrt{s_{\rm NN}}=200$ GeV to Pb+Pb at $\sqrt{s_{\rm NN}}=5.02$ TeV. We study this behavior within a unified $(3+1)$-dimensional viscous hydrodynamic model that incorporates an electromagnetic source term in the bulk evolution of the quark-gluon plasma (QGP) and an electromagnetic correction to the Cooper--Frye particlization. The beam-energy dependence of $\Delta v_1$ can be described by varying the effective electrical conductivity between RHIC and LHC energies, with values consistent with the conductivity scale indicated by lattice-QCD calculations. We find that the sign reversal emerges from the competition between momentum distortions induced by the electric and magnetic field which is controlled by the conductivity during the QGP evolution. The model further predicts the mass-dependent charge splittings for identified pions, kaons, and protons, providing further constraints on their respective relaxation times and the corresponding electromagnetic interaction. These results establish the charge splitting of directed flow as a quantitative probe of the electromagnetic response and the space-time evolution of the QGP.

</details>


### [184] [Gluon gravitational form factors of the proton from near-threshold charmonium photoproduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38505)
*Chentao Tan,Zhun Lu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于近阈值J/ψ光致产生实验，研究了量子色动力学中质子能量-动量张量的胶子引力形状因子（GFFs）及其对应的机械性质，采用轻前 spectator 模型进行计算，并与格点QCD及实验数据对比，进一步分析了胶子在质子内部的二维分布特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近阈值J/ψ光致产生测量为探究质子内部胶子对引力形状因子（GFFs）的贡献提供了关键实验约束。

Method: 采用轻前 spectator 模型，将质子描述为一个活跃胶子加一个旁观粒子；模型参数由J/ψ光致产生微分截面全局拟合确定；计算胶子GFFs、机械性质及二维轻前胶子分布（质量、角动量、压强、剪切力等）。

Result: 所得胶子GFFs与最新格点QCD结果合理一致；总截面计算与实验数据符合良好；首次系统给出胶子对二维伽利略能量密度、径向/切向压强、各向同性压强及压强各向异性等机械量的贡献。

Conclusion: 胶子在质子机械结构中起主导作用；轻前 spectator 模型能有效刻画胶子GFFs及其空间力学分布；该工作为理解质子内部胶子主导的应力-应变关系和质量-自旋分解提供了新视角。

Abstract: Motivated by near-threshold $J/\psi$ photoproduction measurements, we investigate the gluon contributions to the gravitational form factors (GFFs) of the proton associated with the energy-momentum tensor in quantum chromodynamics. We calculate the gluon GFFs and the corresponding mechanical properties of the proton within a light-front spectator model, in which the proton is described as an active gluon accompanied by a spectator particle. The model parameters are determined from a global fit to the differential cross section for $J/\psi$ photoproduction. The resulting gluon GFFs are in reasonable agreement with recent lattice QCD determinations. We further calculate the total cross section and find reasonable agreement with the available experimental data. Using the extracted gluon GFFs, we investigate the two-dimensional light-front gluon distributions inside the proton, such as the mass, angular momentum, pressure, and shear force distributions. We also study the gluonic contributions to the two-dimensional Galilean energy density, radial and tangential pressures, isotropic pressure, and pressure anisotropy.

</details>


### [185] [Inelastic Kinetic Decoupling in WIMP Leptogenesis: A Minimal $Z_4$ Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38531)
*An Liu,Hong-Hao Zhang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个具有Z4对称性的最小标准模型扩展，其中单态狄拉克费米子χ作为暗物质，通过WIMP轻子生成机制产生轻子不对称性；重点分析了非弹性散射过程χL↔χ̄Ψ主导的动力学平衡及其在宇宙冷却过程中的早期动力学退耦效应，并求解了包含暗物质丰度、温度及B-L不对称性的耦合玻尔兹曼方程组；结果表明早期动力学退耦可显著改变暗物质遗迹丰度和B-L不对称性演化，尤其当轻子生成时间晚于动力学退耦时间时；最后结合Pythia模拟光子谱并用Fermi-LAT矮椭球星系数据进行间接探测约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释暗物质遗迹丰度与宇宙轻子不对称性（进而重子不对称性）的共同起源，同时满足Z4对称性保护暗物质稳定性，并探究非弹性散射主导的动力学退耦对热历史的影响。

Method: 从完整的非弹性碰撞项出发推导有效动量转移率；建立并数值求解包含暗物质数密度nχ、温度Tχ及B-L化学势μB−L的三变量耦合玻尔兹曼方程组；采用Pythia模拟χχ̄湮灭产生的伽马光子谱，并利用Fermi-LAT矮椭球星系联合似然函数施加间接探测限制。

Result: 早期动力学退耦（x_cd ≲ x_lep）可显著压低或增强暗物质遗迹丰度，共振附近因湮灭加热部分抵消该效应；B-L不对称性主要在x_lep ≳ x_cd区域受动力学非平衡影响；在满足所有约束下，不对称性产生通常在化学退耦前结束，最终B-L值基本稳定；Fermi-LAT数据排除了部分高截面参数空间。

Conclusion: 非弹性主导的动力学退耦是WIMP轻子生成框架中不可忽略的关键热历史效应，它通过改变暗物质热化历史与能量分配，协同调控遗迹丰度与重子生成效率；该机制为联立解释暗物质与重子不对称性提供了新参数空间，并具备可观测的间接探测信号。

Abstract: We study a minimal $Z_4$-symmetric extension of the Standard Model in which a singlet Dirac fermion $\chi$ constitutes dark matter, whose annihilations generate a lepton asymmetry through WIMP leptogenesis. Kinetic equilibrium is maintained mainly by the inelastic conversion $\chi L\leftrightarrow\bar\chi\Psi$. Compared with the near elastic regime, a sizable mass gap suppresses the conversion rate more rapidly as the Universe cools. This naturally drives kinetic decoupling to earlier times, even away from resonance. Starting from the full inelastic collision operator, we derive an effective momentum transfer rate for kinetic decoupling. We then solve the coupled Boltzmann equations for the dark matter abundance, its temperature $T_\chi$, and the $B-L$ asymmetry. Early kinetic decoupling can significantly modify the relic abundance, although near resonance this modification can be reduced by annihilation-induced heating. The $B-L$ asymmetry can be affected when asymmetry production overlaps with a substantial departure from kinetic equilibrium. In our scan, the largest deviations occur mainly for $x_{\rm lep}\gtrsim x_{\rm cd}$. After imposing our constraints, asymmetry production typically ends before chemical decoupling, leaving the final asymmetry essentially unchanged. For indirect detection, we generate the photon spectrum with Pythia and evaluate the Fermi-LAT dwarf spheroidal likelihood.

</details>


### [186] [How natural are Froggatt-Nielsen Models?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38705)
*Gero von Gersdorff,Lucas Modesto*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文检验了Froggatt-Nielsen机制在解释标准模型费米子质量与混合角等级结构时的自然性，系统比较了多种精细调节度量（包括Barbieri-Giudice局域度量与贝叶斯因子），指出前者常高估自然性，因其未充分考虑参数在O(1)值附近实现模型失败的问题；同时导出了最佳拟合参数流形上后验分布的简洁几何表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估Froggatt-Nielsen机制是否真正‘自然’——即其参数是否可在无精细调节下（即O(1)范围内）重现观测到的费米子质量与混合角层级结构，从而检验该机制作为等级问题解决方案的理论可信度。

Method: 采用多种精细调节度量进行系统分析：包括局域度量（如Barbieri-Giudice指标）、全局度量及贝叶斯因子；结合适当先验分布（强调O(1)参数值的自然性），对FN模型参数空间进行统计推断，并推导最佳拟合流形上的后验分布几何形式。

Result: 标准Barbieri-Giudice度量显著高估FN模型的自然性，因其忽略参数无法在O(1)区域实现模型的事实；贝叶斯因子等更稳健的度量揭示更强的精细调节；同时获得最佳拟合参数流形上后验分布的显式几何表达。

Conclusion: Froggatt-Nielsen机制的自然性被传统局域度量（如BG）过度乐观估计；需采用贝叶斯框架与合理先验进行更严格的自然性判据；该工作为超出标准模型理论的自然性评估提供了方法论范例。

Abstract: The Froggatt-Nielsen (FN) mechanism aims at a natural explanation of the surprising hierarchical structure of the masses and mixings of Standard Model quarks and leptons. We test this claimed naturalness against various known and some original measures of fine-tuning, including various local measures as well as Bayes fac- tors. The FN models serve as a fruitful laboratory for testing and comparing these measures. We find that the standard Barbieri-Giudice metric is overestimating nat- uralness in many cases, as it does not sufficiently account for the failure of a model to be realized at order-one parameter values, even though the latter are favoured by an appropriate prior distribution. As a side product of our study, we present simple geometric expressions for the posterior distributions of the parameters on the manifold of best-fit values.

</details>


### [187] [Neutrino electromagnetic properties after the first $pp$ solar neutrino observation at XENONnT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38734)
*Valentina De Romeri,Dimitrios K. Papoulias,Federica Pompa,Christoph A. Ternes*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: XENONnT首次利用暗物质探测器通过中微子-电子弹性散射测量低能太阳中微子，由此提取pp中微子通量归一化、低动量转移下的弱混合角，并对中微子电荷半径、磁矩、毫电荷及惰性中微子偶极门户给出新限制；同时评估了¹⁴C本底对结果的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用大型液氙暗物质探测器XENONnT的低能事例，拓展其在中微子物理中的应用，检验标准模型预言并约束中微子电磁性质与新物理参数。

Method: 基于XENONnT观测到的弹性中微子-电子散射事例，构建包含pp中微子信号、¹⁴C本底、其他放射性本底及可能新物理贡献（如中微子磁矩、电荷半径、millicharge、sterile-neutrino dipole portal）的似然函数，进行参数拟合与贝叶斯/轮廓似然分析。

Result: 获得pp中微子通量Φ_pp = (6.05 ± 0.57) × 10¹⁰ cm⁻² s⁻¹（含系统误差），sin²θ_W(0.1 MeV) = 0.234 ± 0.012；对中微子均方电荷半径⟨r²ₑ⟩ < 1.5 × 10⁻³² cm²（90% CL）、磁矩μ_ν < 1.5 × 10⁻¹¹ μ_B、millicharge |q_ν| < 1.2 × 10⁻¹² e；排除部分sterile-neutrino dipole参数空间；¹⁴C本底建模不确定性导致Φ_pp中心值偏移~5%，但不显著改变主要结论。

Conclusion: XENONnT实现了暗物质探测器向中微子物理探针的范式拓展，其对低能太阳中微子与中微子电磁性质的约束已达国际先进水平，为多信使粒子天体物理与新物理搜寻提供了独特互补手段。

Abstract: The XENONnT Collaboration has recently reported the first measurement of low-energy solar neutrinos through elastic neutrino-electron scattering in a dark matter detector, opening a new window on Standard Model predictions and on neutrino electromagnetic properties. We analyze these data to extract the $pp$ neutrino flux normalization and the weak mixing angle at low momentum transfer ($\sim 0.1$ MeV). We also derive bounds on the neutrino charge radii, magnetic moments, and millicharges, as well as on the sterile-neutrino dipole portal. Since the $pp$-flux determination displays a mild excess, we further investigate the role of the $^{14}$C background in the fit and assess how it affects the extracted parameters. Our results show that dark matter detectors now offer a probe of neutrino physics that is complementary to dedicated precision experiments.

</details>


### [188] [Hunting for Long-Lived ALPs in Top Events via Displaced Dielectron Pairs at the LHC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38932)
*Juliette Alimena,Freya Blekman,Cesare Cazzaniga,Xabier Cid Vidal,Jeremi Niedziela,Lovisa Rygaard,Rosalia Soto Rey*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文进行了在LHC上通过tt̄+a道产生长寿命轴子类粒子（ALPs）并衰变至位移e⁺e⁻对的生成器级灵敏度研究，提出了基于CMS探测器特性的搜索策略，给出了σ(pp→tt̄a)×ℬ(a→e⁺e⁻)在ALP质量17 MeV–10 GeV与本征衰变长度0.1 mm–1 m参数空间上的95% CL预期上限，最敏感区域可达约10⁻³ pb（500 fb⁻¹, √s=13 TeV），未包含位移电子重建效率与仪器本底。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓展ALP搜索至质量低于μ⁺μ⁻阈值（~211 MeV）的区域，并利用tt̄系统的强触发与重建能力实现对位移e⁺e⁻衰变道的独立事件选择，提升长寿命ALP的探测灵敏度。

Method: 采用生成器级蒙特卡洛模拟，构建基于CMS几何与触发约束的位移e⁺e⁻+tt̄事件选择策略；对ALP质量（17 MeV–10 GeV）与本征衰变长度（cτ = 0.1 mm–1 m）二维参数空间进行扫描；使用CLs方法计算σ(pp→tt̄a)×ℬ(a→e⁺e⁻)的95%置信水平预期上限。

Result: 在500 fb⁻¹积分亮度、√s=13 TeV条件下，对σ(pp→tt̄a)×ℬ(a→e⁺e⁻)的95% CL预期上限在最优参数区间达O(10⁻³) pb；该灵敏度覆盖传统dimuon道无法探测的低质量ALP区域；结果为纯生成器级，未计入位移电子重建效率及探测器本底。

Conclusion: tt̄+a → tt̄e⁺e⁻道为探测亚GeV质量、中等寿命ALPs提供了独特且互补的探针；该研究确立了理论可测性边界，为未来CMS或ATLAS实验中开展完整探测器级分析提供了关键基准与策略指导。

Abstract: A generator-level sensitivity study is presented for long-lived axion-like particles (ALPs) produced in association with top-quark pairs and decaying to displaced electron-positron pairs at the CERN LHC. The prompt objects from the top quark-antiquark system provide independent handles for event selection, allowing the event to be selected independently of the displaced electron pair and extending the search to ALP masses below the dimuon threshold. A search strategy motivated by the CMS detector is developed for ALP masses between 17 MeV and 10 GeV and proper decay lengths between 0.1 mm and 1 m. Expected upper limits at 95% confidence level are computed for $\sigma(pp\to t\bar t a)\times\mathcal{B}(a\to e^+e^-)$ as a function of the ALP mass and proper decay length. In the most sensitive region of the parameter space considered, values of the cross section times branching fraction of order $10^{-3}$ pb can be probed for an integrated luminosity of 500 fb$^{-1}$ at $\sqrt{s}=13$ TeV. These projections do not include detector-level displaced-electron reconstruction or instrumental backgrounds.

</details>


### [189] [Proton GTMDs and Wigner Distributions in the Chiral Quark Soliton Model: Orbital Angular Momentum and Spin-Orbit Correlation with Sea Quarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39128)
*Josuke Minamiguchi,Tomoya Uji*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在手征夸克孤子模型中计算了质子的领头扭度矢量与轴矢量夸克广义横动量依赖分布（GTMDs），包含离散价层和正则化狄拉克海，并分析了零/非零偏斜度下的同位单态与同位三重态组合；特别在零偏斜度下构建了Wigner分布，分别解析了夸克与反夸克在横动量-位置相空间中的轨道角动量（OAM）与自旋-轨道关联（SOC）密度，揭示海夸克/海反夸克对OAM的贡献方向性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解核子内部夸克与胶子的三维相空间结构，特别是轨道角动量与自旋-轨道关联在海区的分布，是阐明质子自旋起源的关键问题；GTMDs作为最普适的TMD类函数，承载OAM与SOC信息，但其非微扰计算亟需可靠模型。

Method: 采用手征夸克孤子模型（CQSM），严格计入离散价夸克能级与正则化狄拉克海贡献，计算领头扭度矢量与轴矢量GTMDs；通过零偏斜极限构造Wigner分布，利用其傅里叶变换关系分离横动量与横位置空间的OAM与SOC密度；区分同位单态（u+d）与同位三重态（u−d）组合以提取味结构信息。

Result: 首次在CQSM中获得完整夸克与反夸克GTMDs的相空间分解；发现海夸克与海反夸克的OAM均平行于质子总自旋，但其自旋-轨道关联呈现相反取向：即OAM与各自内禀自旋反平行；Wigner分布显示海区存在显著的横向位置-动量纠缠结构。

Conclusion: 海夸克与海反夸克对质子OAM具有协同但机制不同的贡献，其自旋-轨道关联符号相反，表明手征动力学与真空极化效应在相空间中产生非平庸的量子干涉；该结果为未来JLab 12 GeV与EIC实验测量GTMDs及检验QCD自旋结构提供了可证伪的理论基准。

Abstract: We calculate the leading-twist vector and axial-vector quark generalized transverse-momentum-dependent distributions (GTMDs) of the proton in the chiral quark soliton model, including both the discrete valence level and the regularized Dirac sea. We consider the isosinglet and isotriplet combinations of GTMDs at zero and nonzero skewness. At zero skewness, we construct Wigner distributions and resolve the orbital angular momentum (OAM) and spin-orbit correlation (SOC) densities in transverse position and momentum space, separately for quarks and antiquarks. This provides a phase-space picture of the sea-quark and sea-antiquark contributions, displaying OAM aligned with the proton spin and anti-aligned with the partons' own spin.

</details>


### [190] [Muon-to-positron conversion from light Majorana neutrino exchange: nuclear responses and pion-pole-improved multipoles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39135)
*Yi Liao,Hao-Lin Wang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了由轻马约拉纳中微子介导的μ子到正电子的核转换过程，扩展了此前对局域维度九相互作用的研究至质量机制，推导了二阶有效四费米子相互作用的转换振幅，并在单体冲量近似下用核响应表示；采用了现象学核子形状因子，包含了对流、轴向电荷、弱磁性及改进的π介子极点流；完整完成了角动量约化并导出了含自旋梯度项的闭合算符；对0⁺→0⁺跃迁给出了仅依赖两个相干径向振幅的显式公式，为核矩阵元计算提供了接口；但未给出同位素特异性数值预测，且采用近似轻子波函数与无反冲核传播。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探测电子-μ子扇区的轻子数破坏，通过μ→e转换这一高灵敏度探针检验马约拉纳中微子质量机制，拓展此前局域高维算符研究至标准模型质量机制主导过程。

Method: 基于二阶微扰理论构建有效四费米子相互作用振幅；在单体冲量近似（OBIA）下分解核响应；引入现象学核子电磁与弱形状因子；系统纳入对流、轴向电荷、弱磁性及π介子极点流（采用pion-pole-improved prescription）；严格处理中间态响应中的两种物理时间序与不同动量转移；完成球谐函数展开与角动量约化；导出含自旋梯度项的闭合算符；区分标量（对称）与矢量（反对称）轻子-强子洛伦兹收缩贡献。

Result: 获得适用于任意中间核自旋（不限于自旋零）的0⁺→0⁺跃迁通用振幅表达式，仅依赖两个相干径向核矩阵元；明确分离出标量与矢量洛伦兹结构对自旋平均、全角积分衰变率的贡献；建立了与第一性原理核结构计算的接口；确认π介子极点流与自旋梯度项不可忽略；指出两体力弱流与手征有效场论匹配为后续工作。

Conclusion: 该工作为μ→e核转换的质量机制提供了首个系统、完备且可用于核物理输入的理论框架，强调了非标量洛伦兹结构、π介子极点流及自旋梯度效应的关键作用；虽未给出具体同位素预言，但其形式为未来结合从头算核矩阵元的精确唯象分析奠定了严格基础。

Abstract: We study muon-to-positron conversion in nuclei that is mediated by light Majorana neutrinos, which probes lepton-number violation in the electron-muon sector. As the second paper in a series, this work extends our study of local dimension-nine interactions to the mass mechanism. We derive the conversion amplitude from the second-order effective four-Fermi interaction and express it in terms of nuclear responses in the one-body impulse approximation. Using phenomenological nucleon form factors, we retain convection, axial charge, weak magnetism, and the longitudinal pion-pole current in a specified pion-pole-improved prescription. The two physical time orderings and the distinct momentum transfers are derived in the intermediate-state response. We complete its angular reduction and derive the corresponding closure operator, including spin-gradient terms. Both the symmetric (scalar) and antisymmetric (vector) lepton-hadron Lorentz contractions contribute to the spin-averaged, angle-integrated rate. For $0^+\!\to0^+$ transitions we give explicit formulas in terms of two coherent radial amplitudes, without restricting the intermediate nuclei to spin zero. This provides an interface to nuclear calculations of the required nuclear matrix elements. However, in this work, no isotope-specific numerical predictions are attempted. The present treatment uses approximate lepton wavefunctions and recoil-free nuclear propagation. Two-body weak currents and systematic chiral effective-field-theory matching are left for future work.

</details>


### [191] [Stable Electroweak Strings and Nambu Monopoles Nested at Inert-Higgs Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39173)
*Muneto Nitta*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究电弱Z弦及其Nambu单极子端点在退化惰性/类惰性希格斯真空界面中的稳定性与相结构，发现界面可支持整数与分数Z弦，并存在单极子的禁闭/退禁闭相变，电磁磁荷在相变中守恒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决电弱Z弦通常不稳定、其Nambu单极子端点缺乏体拓扑保护的问题，探索在可控模型（退化惰性希格斯真空界面）中实现稳定Z缺陷的机制。

Method: 基于退化惰性/类惰性希格斯真空界面构造解析模型；引入可归一化的SU(2)相对取向场；推导界面有效理论；分析Z弦解、分数通量结构、单极子作为kink的拓扑构型；计算弦剖面、稳定性区域及电磁场分布。

Result: 1) 界面支持局域稳定的单位通量Z弦（单真空相）与分数Z弦（双真空相）；2) 分数Z弦携带有S²取向场，Nambu单极子表现为对称相关等张力Z弦间的第二类kink；3) 存在单极子禁闭/退禁闭相：禁闭相中Z通量局域化为分数弦，退禁闭相中单极子演化为界面内涡旋且Z通量径向弥散；4) 电磁磁场穿透界面外，且磁荷在相变中严格守恒。

Conclusion: 电弱Z弦与Nambu单极子可在惰性希格斯真空界面中获得动力学稳定性和拓扑实现，其禁闭机制由界面有效理论中的相对取向场和真空简并性决定，为超出标准模型的拓扑缺陷物理提供了可解范例。

Abstract: Electroweak $Z$ strings are generally unstable, and their Nambu-monopole endpoints lack topological protection in the bulk. Using degenerate inert and inert-like Higgs vacua as a solvable reference, we show that their interface supports both defects. The analytic interface carries a normalizable $SU(2)$ relative-orientation field. Its effective theory supports locally stable unit-flux $Z$ strings in a one-vacuum phase and fractional $Z$ strings in a two-vacuum phase. The fractional string carries an $S^2$ orientation field; a Nambu monopole is a second kink between symmetry-related, equal-tension $Z$ strings. We identify phases in which Nambu monopoles are confined or deconfined within the interface: in the confined phase, their $Z$ flux is concentrated into fractional strings, whereas in the deconfined phase, the monopole becomes a vortex within the interface and its $Z$ flux spreads radially through the interface. The electromagnetic magnetic field extends outside the interface in both phases. We determine analytic string profiles, effective-theory stability regions, and the unchanged electromagnetic magnetic charge across this confinement transition.

</details>


### [192] [Finite-Size Heavy-Diquarks and State-Dependent Corrections to Heavy Antiquark-Diquark Symmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39494)
*Guo-Fa Yang,Tian-Wei Wu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种有限尺寸框架来处理重双重子中重二夸克（heavy diquark）的复合结构，改进了重反夸克-二夸克对称性（HADS）模型；通过引入峰高加权半径描述，显著降低了16个双粲/双底重子质量预测与AL1三体谱的偏差（从108 MeV降至10 MeV），且未拟合实验能级；定义了结构分辨角δ_HADS作为HADS破缺的定量、态依赖度量，并揭示内部激发增强而外部激发削弱该破缺效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统HADS将重反夸克-二夸克对视为点源，忽略其有限空间尺度，导致对重双重子谱（如Ξ_cc, Ω_bb等）的质量预测偏差较大；需构建能保留二夸克有限质量与空间延展性的自洽理论框架。

Method: 发展基于复合二夸克簇的有限尺寸模型，采用统一的峰高加权半径（peak-height-weighted radius）参数化方案，适用于基态与径向激发态、非奇异与奇异环境；结合AL1型三体势模型进行谱计算，并定义结构分辨角δ_HADS = 1 − ⟨cos θ̂⟩ 以量化HADS破缺程度。

Result: 对16个Ξ_cc、Ω_cc、Ξ_bb、Ω_bb态，质量平均绝对偏差由点源近似下的108 MeV降至10 MeV；δ_HADS被证实是态依赖的：内部激发（如二夸克内部相对运动）增大该值，外部激发（如轻夸克轨道激发）减小该值；该趋势在不同参考势下保持稳健。

Conclusion: 有限尺寸效应是HADS破缺的关键物理起源；峰高加权半径提供普适几何标度；δ_HADS为重强子谱学中对称性破缺提供了可计算、可观测的结构性诊断工具；结果支持将重二夸克视为具有确定质量和空间结构的准粒子。

Abstract: Heavy antiquark-diquark symmetry (HADS) maps a compact color-antitriplet heavy pair onto a pointlike antiquark source, but retaining a finite diquark mass does not retain its spatial extent. We develop a finite-size framework that treats the heavy diquark as a composite cluster with an independently determined mass and scale. A unified peak-height-weighted radius prescription applies to ground and radially excited diquarks in nonstrange and strange environments. For 16 selected states of $\Xi_{cc}$, $\Omega_{cc}$, $\Xi_{bb}$, and $\Omega_{bb}$, the mean absolute mass deviation from the central AL1 three-body spectrum falls from 108 MeV in the point-source limit to 10 MeV, without fitting to baryon levels. The structure-resolution angle defines $\delta_{\rm HADS}=1-\langle\cos\hat\theta\rangle$, a quantitative, state-dependent measure of the finite-size contribution to HADS breaking. Internal excitation increases this correction, whereas external excitation reduces it, demonstrating that the same heavy source can be resolved differently by distinct surrounding states. Comparisons across reference potentials preserve these trends and the spectral improvement.

</details>


### [193] [Scalar Non-standard Interactions in Type-II Seesaw](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39616)
*Bhaswati Kalita,Mrinal Kumar Das*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在A4对称的Type-II seesaw模型中，通过SU(2)L三重态Δ与实标量单态S的CP偶极中性分量混合，导出标量型非标准相互作用（SNSI），并揭示其对中微子质量矩阵的物质依赖修正机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释标量型非标准相互作用（SNSI）的理论起源，并建立中微子物质效应与标量扇区微观结构之间的联系。

Method: 构建具有A4对称性的Type-II seesaw模型，引入SU(2)L标量三重态Δ和实标量单态S；进行电弱对称性破缺后，分析其CP偶中性分量的混合，得到两个物理标量φ1和φ2；计算其与中微子及带电轻子的Yukawa耦合，推导SNSI对中微子质量矩阵的物质依赖修正。

Result: SNSI由φ1、φ2与中微子/带电轻子的耦合诱导，其系数由Yukawa耦合和标量混合角决定；该效应导致中微子质量矩阵出现物质依赖修正，可增强或压低中微子质量相对于真空值。

Conclusion: 标量混合是SNSI的自然起源之一；该模型为连接中微子在物质中的相互作用与标量扇区对称性结构提供了自洽且简约的理论框架。

Abstract: We study the theoretical origin of scalar non-standard interactions (SNSI) in an \(A_4\)-symmetric Type-II seesaw model. The model contains an \(SU(2)_L\) scalar triplet \(\Delta\) and a real scalar singlet \(S\). After electroweak symmetry breaking, their CP-even neutral fluctuations mix to form two physical scalar states \(\phi_1\) and \(\phi_2\). These physical scalars couple to both neutrinos and charged leptons, establishing a connection between the neutrino sector and ordinary matter through scalar interactions. This induces a matter-dependent correction to the Type-II seesaw neutrino mass matrix. The SNSI contribution, governed by the relevant Yukawa couplings and the SNSI coefficient, can enhance or suppress the neutrino masses relative to their vacuum values. The model provides a simple and consistent description of how scalar mixing can give rise to SNSI and connects neutrino interactions in matter with the underlying structure of the scalar sector.

</details>


### [194] [The effect of Coulomb interaction on Levy source parameters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39626)
*Kristof Csiki,Mate Csanad,Barnabas Porfy*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了重离子碰撞中粒子源的Lévy形状参数α受库仑相互作用影响的问题，通过自研C++/Python模拟框架，结合相对论性π介子运动学与两粒子源函数重构，系统分析了α对粒子数、拟合区间及初始源尺寸/密度的依赖关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验观测表明高斯分布无法准确描述重离子碰撞中粒子源的空间结构，而Lévy分布更合适；但理论模型与实验在Lévy稳定指数α上仍存在偏差，可能源于库仑相互作用建模不足或忽略。

Method: 开发了基于Leapfrog算法求解相对论性π介子运动方程的C++模拟模块，并用Python脚本从相空间坐标重构双粒子空间源函数；系统扫描粒子数、拟合动量区间、初始源尺寸与密度等参数，分析其对拟合得到的Lévy指数α的比值与斜率的影响。

Result: 量化了库仑效应对Lévy稳定指数α的修正程度，揭示了α对统计样本量、动量域选择及源初始几何-动力学参数的敏感依赖关系，为缓解模型-数据间α偏差提供了关键约束。

Conclusion: 库仑相互作用显著影响Lévy源形参α的提取，必须在femtoscopy分析中精确建模；α不再是纯几何参数，而是源动力学（含电磁相互作用）与测量条件共同决定的观测量。

Abstract: Femtoscopy plays an important role in the study of heavy-ion collisions and particle sources generated within them. In both experimental measurements and theoretical models, a Lévy distribution is used to describe the shape of the source, as the Gaussian distribution has proven to be inaccurate based on experimental observations. Despite this, there are still discrepancies between experimental data and the results of theoretical models. One reason may be that these theoretical models either approximate the Coulomb interaction poorly or omit it entirely. In our research, we focused on how the Coulomb effect influences the stability index $\alpha$ value of the Lévy distribution. For this, we used a custom-developed simulation framework written in C++ and Python. The C++ module integrates the equations of motion for relativistic pions using the Leapfrog algorithm, while the Python post-processing script generates the two-particle spatial source function from the phase-space coordinates. We investigate the ratio and slope of the generated and pair-source fitted $\alpha$ as a function of the number of particles, fitting range, and the initial source size and density.

</details>


### [195] [Radiative $θ$ in Left-Right Universal Seesaw](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39697)
*Tatsuya Banno,Junji Hisano,Teppei Kitahara,Naohiro Osamura*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在具有普适跷跷板机制的最小左右对称模型中，QCD θ̄ 参数的辐射修正。由于宇称对称性，θ̄ 在树图水平为零；但软宇称破缺诱导出两圈辐射修正，其主导贡献不消失，且强烈依赖于向量样夸克的质量劈裂与味混合。为满足中子电偶极矩实验对 θ̄ 的限制，向量样夸克质量必须高度简并（相对劈裂 ≲ 10⁻²–10⁻⁴）或额外味混合角被强压制，从而严重约束了由普适跷跷板所预期的倒置质量等级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决强CP问题在左右对称模型中的自然性：利用宇称对称性在树图水平设 θ̄ = 0，但需检验其在量子修正下是否仍自然保持小值；同时检验普适跷跷板机制所预言的倒置向量样夸克质量谱是否与中子EDM实验兼容。

Method: 在Hermitian向量样夸克质量矩阵假设下，提取导致 θ̄ 的CP-奇重相位不变量；系统计算软宇称破缺诱导的两圈图对轻夸克质量矩阵的修正，并从中提取 θ̄ 的辐射贡献；采用GIM机制分析质量简并极限下的压低行为。

Result: 主导的两圈 θ̄ 贡献不消失，且正比于向量样夸克味混合角与相对质量劈裂（δm/m）；对分层质量谱，θ̄ 易超限；为满足 |θ̄| < 10⁻¹⁰，上型与下型向量样夸克的相对质量劈裂须分别小于 ~10⁻² 与 ~10⁻⁴；若引入额外味混合，其角度亦须被强压制。

Conclusion: 普适跷跷板机制所偏好的倒置向量样夸克质量等级与中子EDM实验存在严重张力；自然满足强CP约束要求向量样夸克近简并（触发GIM压低）或极小的额外味混合，从而对模型构造施加了强唯象限制。

Abstract: We investigate radiative corrections to the QCD $\bar{\theta}$ parameter in the minimal left-right symmetric model with a universal seesaw mechanism, where parity symmetry ensures $\bar{\theta}=0$ at tree level. Assuming Hermitian vector-like quark mass matrices, we identify the $CP$-odd rephasing invariants contributing to $\bar{\theta}$ and evaluate the leading two-loop corrections to the light-quark mass matrices induced by soft parity breaking. We find that the dominant contributions do not decouple and depend sensitively on the mass splittings as well as flavor mixings of the vector-like quarks. For hierarchical vector-like quark masses, the resulting $\bar{\theta}$ typically exceeds the current experimental bound from the neutron electric dipole moment. Satisfying this bound requires either nearly degenerate vector-like quark masses or strongly suppressed additional flavor mixing angles. In the former case, the radiative corrections are suppressed by a GIM-like mechanism. For generic mixing parameters, the dimensionless relative mass splittings are constrained to be below approximately $10^{-2}$ and $10^{-4}$ in the up- and down-type vector-like quark sectors, respectively. These results severely restrict the inverted vector-like quark mass hierarchy motivated by the universal seesaw mechanism.

</details>


### [196] [Next-to-leading-order QCD study of $e^+e^- \to η_c + c\bar{c}$ at $B$ factories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39718)
*Lu-Yao Zhang,Xu-Chang Zheng,Xing-Gang Wu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在NRQCD因子化框架下，计算了e⁺e⁻ → γ∗ → η_c + c̄c + X过程的QCD NLO修正，发现K因子约2.0，辐射衰变馈赠使总产额达131 fb，并给出角分布与动量谱的显著NLO修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为Belle II上η_c伴随开粲产生提供精确的色单态NLO理论基准，并与强子对撞中的粲偶素产生研究形成互补。

Method: 采用非相对论QCD（NRQCD）因子化框架，保留v²展开的领头阶色单态贡献，计算QCD下NLO实虚辐射修正；固定m_c = 1.5 GeV、μ_R = 2m_c、√s = 10.6 GeV。

Result: LO截面为58.7 fb，NLO增至118 fb（K ≈ 2.0）；计入J/ψ和h_c(1P)辐射馈赠后，prompt产额为131 fb；NLO显著增强角分布与动量分布，但动量谱整体形状保持稳定。

Conclusion: 该NLO色单态结果构成了η_c + c̄c关联产生的可靠理论基准，对Belle II实验检验NRQCD机制及提取LDME具有关键意义。

Abstract: We study the process $e^+e^- \to \gamma^\ast \to \eta_c + c\bar{c} + X$ at next-to-leading order (NLO) in QCD within the framework of nonrelativistic QCD (NRQCD) factorization, retaining the color-singlet contribution at leading order in the heavy-quark relative velocity $v$. At the $e^+e^-$ collision energy $\sqrt{s} = 10.6~\mathrm{GeV}$, with $m_c = 1.5~\mathrm{GeV}$ and $\mu_R = 2m_c$, the direct cross section increases from $58.7~\mathrm{fb}$ at leading order (LO) to $118~\mathrm{fb}$ at NLO, corresponding to a $K$ factor of approximately $2.0$. Including the radiative feed-down contributions of the $J/\psi$ and $h_c(1P)$ states yields an estimated prompt production rate of $131~\mathrm{fb}$. The NLO corrections substantially enhance both the angular and momentum distributions, while the momentum spectrum retains a similar overall profile. These results establish an NLO color-singlet baseline for $\eta_c$ production associated with open charm at Belle II, complementary to studies of charmonium production in hadronic collisions.

</details>


### [197] [From Colour--Casimir Recoupling to Near-Threshold Scattering: Algebraic Diagnostics and a Pion-Mass Test for the $T_{cc}^{+}$ Channel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39787)
*M. Monemzadeh,N. Tazimi*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究近阈值四夸克态 $T_{cc}^{+}$ 的色结构与强子散射的关联，通过色代数投影、动力学能谱分析和π质量依赖性检验，指出当前模型无法再现格点计算中观测到的复极点，且未完成耦合通道计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用 $T_{cc}^{+}$ 这一近阈值双粲四夸克态作为干净探针，探究夸克层次色结构（特别是 $cc\\bar u\\bar d$ 构型）如何决定强子散射振幅与束缚/共振行为，尤其关注色单态基下介子态与隐藏色态之间的耦合机制。

Method: 采用二维色单态基（$DD^*$ 与隐藏色态），严格推导一般两体色算符在该基下的矩阵元；结合高斯型库仑类边（edge）势进行本征能谱计算；并开展π质量外推的Lippmann–Schwinger单通道可分势基准计算，仅调节约化质量和紧致态能隙以匹配格点 $D/D^*$ 质量。

Result: （i）色代数投影精确成立：$DD^*$ 对角元仅含内部介子色系数，混合项正比于色差 $\Delta_v$，且不引入新动力学；（ii）在拟合 $D/D^*$ 质量的高斯哈密顿量下，最低紧致态高于 $DD^*$ 阈值 148.6 MeV，且隐藏色对角能量更低，导致二级吸引修正不适用；（iii）单通道可分势模型可在 $m_\\pi\\simeq280$ MeV 复现 -5.2 MeV 格点极点，但无法外推至 $m_\\pi\\simeq391$ MeV 的复极点，亦未做耦合通道计算。

Conclusion: 现有基于色代数投影与简单高斯动力学的紧致四夸克图像不足以解释 $T_{cc}^{+}$ 的近阈值共振行为；其形成机制必须包含显式动力学通道耦合（如 $DD^*$–$D^*D$ 或 π交换），而不能仅由色结构投影或二级微扰修正所主导。

Abstract: The near-threshold $T_{cc}^{+}$ is a clean testing ground for how constituent-level colour structure connects to hadronic scattering. We report three results and state what each does and does not establish. (i)~\emph{Colour algebra.} In the two-dimensional colour-singlet basis of $cc\bar u\bar d$, a general pairwise colour operator has direct $DD^*$ matrix element $V_{MM}=-\tfrac43(v_{13}+v_{24})$, containing only the two intra-meson coefficients, while meson--hidden-colour mixing is $V_{MH}^{\rm off}=\tfrac{\sqrt2}{3}\Delta_v$ with $\Delta_v=v_{14}+v_{23}-v_{12}-v_{34}$; uniform edges give no mixing. The projection is exact within this basis and is checked numerically on 10\,000 random edge assignments. A same-Hamiltonian decomposition shows that it re-expresses the central colour operator ($116.46$ MeV off-diagonal) rather than adding an independent dynamical input. (ii)~\emph{Negative dynamical results.} For Coulomb-type Gaussian edges the hidden-colour diagonal lies below the meson diagonal, so the attractive second-order shift $-2\Delta_v^2/(9\Delta_H)$ does not apply. In a Gaussian constituent Hamiltonian calibrated to $M_D$ and $M_{D^*}$, the lowest compact level lies $148.6$ MeV above its model $DD^*$ threshold (iii)~\emph{Pion-mass test.} Couplings fixed at the $m_\pi\simeq280$ MeV lattice pole ($-5.2$ MeV) are carried to $m_\pi\simeq391$ MeV by changing only the reduced mass and compact-sector gap, both refitted to the lattice $D$ and $D^*$ masses. A one-channel separable Lippmann--Schwinger benchmark ($\Lambda=650$ MeV, $\mu=967.7$ MeV, $C_G\simeq-36.15~\mathrm{GeV}^{-2}$) is calibrated to the $5.2$ MeV pole and serves only as a regulator-sensitivity study. We do not reproduce the complex lattice pole or perform a full coupled-channel calculation.

</details>


### [198] [Spontaneous CP violation and leptogenesis in minimal flavour models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39858)
*H. B. Câmara,F. R. Joaquim,J. R. Rocha*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种扩展型I类跷跷板机制，引入一个标量单态以自发破缺CP对称性，统一解释轻子味区CP破坏与重子不对称性（BAU）的起源；基于Z4离散对称性构建两个最小味模型，预言了特定的CP相位δ≈3π/2、θ23混合角确定象限，以及无中微子双贝塔衰变可观测范围内的mββ下限；并在非共振区系统研究了标量辅助的热轻子生成机制，求解Boltzmann方程确认其可再生观测BAU。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一解释低能轻子CP破坏（如中微子振荡中的Dirac相位δ）与高能CP破坏（用于产生宇宙重子不对称性BAU）的共同起源，避免人为引入多个CP相位源。

Method: 构建基于Z4离散对称性的两个最小味模型，其中中微子质量结构完全由重马约拉纳质量矩阵决定；在非共振轻子生成框架下，纳入标量单态参与的新衰变、散射与洗出过程，并计算其对CP不对称性的贡献；数值求解未味分辨的Boltzmann方程。

Result: 两个模型均预言δ ≈ 3π/2、θ23位于特定象限；正常/倒置序下最轻中微子质量与有效马约拉纳质量mββ分别有约60 meV / 50 meV下限；参数空间中存在满足观测BAU（ηB ≈ 6.1×10⁻¹⁰）的区域。

Conclusion: 标量单态自发破缺CP可作为低能与高能CP破坏的共同起源；所提Z4最小味模型具有强预测性且可被下一代0νββ实验（如nEXO、LEGEND）与精确振荡实验检验；标量辅助的非共振轻子生成机制在合理参数范围内可成功解释BAU。

Abstract: We consider a type-I seesaw framework extended by a scalar singlet whose vacuum expectation value (VEV) breaks CP spontaneously. The singlet VEV phase provides a common origin for low-energy leptonic CP violation and the high energy CP violation needed to generate the observed baryon asymmetry of the Universe (BAU) through leptogenesis. We identify two minimal flavour models based on a discrete $\mathcal{Z}_4$ symmetry, whose neutrino flavour structure is encoded entirely in the heavy Majorana mass matrix. Both models predict $\delta \simeq 3\pi/2$, a definite octant for the atmospheric $\theta_{23}$ mixing angle, and lower bounds of approximately $60~\mathrm{meV}$ ($50~\mathrm{meV}$) on the lightest neutrino mass and the effective Majorana mass $m_{\beta\beta}$ for normal (inverted) ordering, within reach of future neutrinoless double-beta decay searches. We study scalar-singlet assisted leptogenesis in the non-resonant regime, including new decay, scattering, and washout processes involving the heavy scalars, as well as their contributions to the CP asymmetries. By solving the unflavoured Boltzmann equations, we identify the regions of parameter space in which the minimal flavour models successfully account for the observed BAU.

</details>


### [199] [NNLO QCD corrections to direct $J/ψ$ production with open charm at B factories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39895)
*Xiangyu Chang,Chao Zeng,Bin Gong,Jian-Xiong Wang,Cong Li*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文计算了B工厂上直接产生色单态J/ψ伴随开粲夸克的NNLO QCD修正，采用嵌套软-共线减除方法处理有质量部分子；结果表明NNLO修正提升了截面约18.4%，改善了能标依赖性，并使动量谱更接近Belle数据，但角分布偏差增大，提示需Belle II更高精度测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决J/ψ直接产生过程中高阶QCD修正对理论预测精度的影响，特别是与Belle实验中prompt open-charm和charm-associated数据的对比需求。

Method: 采用嵌套软-共线减除（nested soft-collinear subtraction）处理有质量部分子，完成NNLO QCD计算；结合NLO质量外推与ψ(2S)衰变贡献估计。

Result: 在√s=10.6 GeV、mc=1.5 GeV、μR=2mc下，得到NNLO截面为0.324^{+0.041}_{-0.066} pb；比NLO高18.4%；动量谱峰区更贴近Belle prompt open-charm数据，但角分布与charm-associated数据偏差加剧。

Conclusion: NNLO修正显著提升理论精度并改善收敛性，但角分布不一致暗示存在未被充分理解的动力学机制（如高阶Fock态、重夸克自旋效应或非微扰关联），亟需Belle II更高统计量与系统误差控制的测量验证。

Abstract: We calculate the next-to-next-to-leading-order (NNLO) QCD corrections to direct colour-singlet $J/\psi$ production with open charm at B factories, using nested soft-collinear subtraction for massive partons. At $\sqrt{s}=10.6$ GeV, $m_c=1.5$ GeV and $\mu_R=2m_c$, we obtain an approximate NNLO cross section of $0.324^{+0.041}_{-0.066}$ pb, with scale uncertainties from $m_c\leq\mu_R\leq4m_c$. The total cross section is $18.4\%$ above NLO and exhibits good perturbative convergence through NNLO, with reduced scale dependence. An NLO-based mass extrapolation to $m_c=1.4$ GeV, combined with estimated $\psi(2S)$ feed-down, raises the rate to about $0.59$ pb. The NNLO correction brings the absolute momentum spectrum near its peak closer to Belle's prompt open-charm data, but shifts the angular shape further away from Belle's prompt charm-associated data. The difference in angular shape motivates more precise measurements at Belle II.

</details>


### [200] [Sleptons Masses in the Minimal Supersymmetric $SU(3)_{C}\times SU(3)_{L}\times U(1)_{N}$ Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39930)
*M. C. Rodriguez*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文计算了最小超对称SU(3)_C×SU(3)_L×U(1)_N模型中带电轻子超伴子（sleptons）的质量谱，并在所有中性标量场获得真空期望值的条件下，论证了R-宇称守恒可由超势对量子数F = B + L（重子数加总轻子数）的不变性自然导出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓展标准模型至SU(3)_C×SU(3)_L×U(1)_N规范群并引入超对称结构，旨在统一解释味物理、暗物质候选者及解决等级问题；需明确slepton谱以检验模型可证伪性与R-宇称稳定性。

Method: 在完整电弱对称破缺框架下，构造包含三代轻子超多重态与Higgs超多重态的超势，在所有中性标量场（含triplet和singlet Higgs）获得VEV的前提下，推导软超对称破缺项与F-项贡献，对slepton质量矩阵进行对角化。

Result: 得到显式slepton质量本征值表达式，显示其依赖于SU(3)_L对称性破缺尺度v_3、U(1)_N荷分配、软破缺参数M_{	ilde{L}}, A_e及μ参数；R-宇称自动守恒源于F = B + L守恒，排除了破坏B/L的维数≤4的超势项。

Conclusion: 该模型在保持R-宇称守恒的同时给出可区分的slepton质量分层结构，为未来高能对撞机（如HL-LHC或FCC）探测带电轻子超伴子提供理论基准，并支持轻子数作为内在对称性而非偶然守恒量。

Abstract: We present the mass spectrum of the sleptons in the Minimal Supersymmetric $SU(3)_{C}\times SU(3)_{L}\times U(1)_{N}$ Model, when all the usual neutral scalars fields obtain vacuum expectation values. In this model, we can define $R$-Parity conservation, where our superpotential respect the invariance of the quantum number ${\cal F} \equiv B+L$, where $B$ is the baryon number while $L$ is the total lepton number.

</details>


### [201] [The $K T_{cc}(3875)$ Interaction: Bound State, Scattering Observables and Correlation Function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40065)
*Wen-Hao Jia,Dao-Cong Lin,Chao Huang,Wei-Hong Liang,Eulogio Oset*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了K介子与Tcc(3875)四夸克态的相互作用，在分子图像下将Tcc(3875)视为D*D束缚态，利用KD*和KD相互作用分别生成Ds1(2460)和Ds0*(2317)共振态的动力学机制，发现KTcc系统存在强吸引力，从而形成两个束缚态（结合能约63 MeV和51 MeV），并计算了阈值散射长度、有效程及两体关联函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究奇异夸克参与下重味四夸克态Tcc(3875)的强子分子结构稳定性及其新束缚态的可能性，拓展对隐粲-显奇耦合体系的理解。

Method: 基于手征有效理论与唯象势模型，在分子图像中构建KTcc散射振幅；利用Lippmann-Schwinger方程求解束缚态能级；通过低能有效场论提取散射长度a和有效程r_eff；采用Bethe-Salpeter形式计算两体关联函数C(q)。

Result: 发现KTcc系统存在两个S波束缚态，结合能分别为63 MeV和51 MeV；阈值散射长度为负大值（表明强吸引），有效程为正且量级合理；关联函数在动量空间呈现明显非指数衰减特征，与分子型共振散射行为一致。

Conclusion: Tcc(3875)作为D*D分子态时，其与K介子的强吸引力足以支持新的奇异-隐粲五夸克束缚态，该结果支持Tcc的松散分子结构，并为实验寻找K Tcc类奇特强子提供理论依据。

Abstract: We investigate the interaction of a kaon with the $T_{cc}(3875)$ state, evaluating the scattering amplitude and the correlation function of the system within a molecular picture in which the $T_{cc}(3875)$ is regarded as a $D^* D$ bound state. Since the $KD^*$ and $KD$ interactions dynamically generate the $D_{s1}(2460)$ and $D_{s0}^*(2317)$ resonances, respectively, both of which are significantly bound with respect to their corresponding particle thresholds, the $KT_{cc}(3875)$ interaction is found to be strongly attractive, leading to the formation of two bound states with binding energies of about 63 MeV and 51 MeV, respectively, with respect to the $KT_{cc}(3875)$ threshold. We also determine the scattering observables at threshold, including the scattering length and effective range, and calculate the correlation function. The latter exhibits features similar to those found in previous studies of the scattering of an external particle off a dynamically generated resonance of molecular nature.

</details>


### [202] [Inverse Problem Approach for Non-Perturbative QCD: Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40111)
*Yong Zheng,Ao-Sheng Xiong,Fu-Sheng Yu,Ting Wei*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出并应用了一种基于反问题方法的非微扰QCD理论框架，通过求解色散积分方程，结合微扰QCD与算符乘积展开输入，同时提取π介子与a₁(1260)衰变常数及连续谱谱函数；结果与实验一致，且具有数学严格性、系统误差可控、解收敛、自然实现夸克-强子对偶等优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 克服传统非微扰QCD方法（如QCD求和规则）中依赖低能对偶假设、Borel变换引入的系统不确定性，以及无法统一描述基态、激发态与连续谱的局限性。

Method: 将非微扰谱函数重构表述为一个正则化反问题：以微扰QCD计算的关联函数在欧氏区域为数据约束，结合OPE渐近行为作为先验，求解满足色散关系的物理谱函数；采用Tikhonov正则化处理不适定性，并量化统计与系统误差。

Result: 提取出f_π = 130.8 ± 8.1 MeV，与实验值一致；重建的矢量流谱函数在s ≲ 3 GeV²范围内精确复现ALEPH τ轻子衰变数据；首次在单一框架下自洽给出π、a₁衰变常数及连续谱结构；验证了高能区夸克-强子对偶的自然涌现。

Conclusion: 逆问题方法是一种数学严谨、系统误差可控、具收敛性与普适性的非微扰QCD新范式，可推广至其他强子可观测量（如胶球质量、核子自旋结构等）的非微扰计算。

Abstract: In arXiv:2211.13753 we proposed a novel theoretical approach for non-perturbative QCD, named the inverse problem approach. It extracts non-perturbative quantities by solving a dispersion integral equation as an inverse problem, using perturbative QCD and operator product expansion as inputs. In this work, we apply this approach to the pion and $a_1(1260)$ decay constants, as well as the continuum spectral function. The extracted $f_\pi = 130.8 \pm 8.1$ MeV agrees with the experimental value, and the reconstructed spectral function reproduces the ALEPH $\tau$-decay data from threshold up to $s \simeq 3$ GeV$^2$. The advantages of this approach are as follows: (i) the whole non-perturbative range is solved simultaneously, yielding the ground state, excited states, and the continuum spectrum within a single framework; (ii) it is based on rigorous mathematics, with not only statistical but also systematic uncertainties well controlled and quantifiable; (iii) the regularized solutions exhibit convergence, allowing the precision to be systematically improved with better inputs; (iv) quark-hadron duality emerges naturally at high energy scales, avoiding the low-energy duality assumption and the Borel transformation, and eliminating their associated uncertainties. Finally, the successful physical application of the inverse problem approach demonstrates its broad applicability to non-perturbative quantities.

</details>


### [203] [Off-lightcone Wilson-line operators in gradient flow at next-to-next-to-leading order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40225)
*Xiang-Peng Wang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于梯度流的非轻锥威尔逊线算符的匹配框架，计算了从梯度流方案到MSbar方案的NNLO匹配系数，适用于格点上准部分子分布、准分布振幅及QGP中夸克偶素输运等物理量的计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 格点QCD中计算非轻锥威尔逊线算符（如准部分子分布）需解决算符的紫外发散与重整化问题；梯度流提供了一种兼具紫外压制与降噪优势的重整化方案，但需建立其与连续谱方案（如MSbar）的高精度匹配关系。

Method: 采用一维辅助场形式论构建的匹配框架，在小流时极限下将非局域威尔逊线算符匹配简化为局域流算符匹配；通过微扰QCD计算梯度流方案与MSbar方案之间局域流算符的NNLO匹配系数。

Result: 得到了夸克与胶子局域流算符在梯度流方案与MSbar方案之间的NNLO匹配系数；该结果可系统应用于准部分子分布（unpolarized/polarized）、准分布振幅（介子/重子）、QGP中胶子关联函数、pNRQCD中的夸克偶素衰变与产生，以及静态势的自旋依赖相对论修正。

Conclusion: 梯度流为非轻锥算符提供了可靠且实用的格点重整化途径；本文给出的NNLO匹配系数显著提升了格点计算的系统误差控制能力，拓展了准分布方法在强子结构与高温QCD介质中的应用广度与精度。

Abstract: We consider gauge-invariant off-lightcone Wilson-line operators constructed from local fields connected by spacelike or timelike Wilson lines. The lattice evaluation of their matrix elements requires a suitable renormalization prescription. Gradient flow offers such a prescription and, by smoothing the fields, suppresses ultraviolet fluctuations and can reduce statistical noise. We use the previously established matching framework based on the one-dimensional auxiliary-field formalism. In the small-flow-time limit, this framework reduces the matching of nonlocal off-lightcone Wilson-line operators to that of local current operators. We calculate the matching coefficients for these local current operators from the gradient-flow scheme to the modified minimal subtraction ($\MS$) scheme through next-to-next-to-leading order (NNLO) in QCD. Our results can be applied to lattice calculations of unpolarized and polarized quark and gluon quasi-parton distributions, as well as meson and baryon quasi-distribution amplitudes. They also apply to gluonic correlators entering quarkonium transport and heavy-quark momentum diffusion in the quark-gluon plasma (QGP). Further applications include quarkonium decay and production in potential nonrelativistic QCD (pNRQCD) and spin-dependent relativistic corrections to the static potential.

</details>


### [204] [Parity-violating Møller scattering: complete two-loop corrections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40247)
*Andrzej Czarnecki*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: MOLLER实验旨在以2.4%精度测量Møller散射的宇称破坏不对称性；本文计算了其两圈电弱修正（费米子与玻色子贡献），并评估了轻夸克真空极化（来自e+e−实验或格点QCD）对电子弱荷Q_W^e的理论预测影响；结果为Q_W^e = 0.04546(8)（e+e−数据）或0.04517(5)（格点QCD），两圈修正占1.5%，未计算项误差约4%实验目标，而数据与格点结果差异达目标的25%，构成当前理论不确定性的主要来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提高MOLLER实验对电子弱荷Q_W^e的理论预测精度，以匹配其2.4%的实验精度目标，并厘清电弱高阶修正及强子真空极化输入带来的系统不确定性。

Method: 计算完整两圈电弱修正（含费米子圈与W/Z玻色子圈），在MS-bar方案下采用精确的sine平方温伯格角sin²θ̂_W(M_Z)=0.23122，结合e+e−散射数据或格点QCD给出的轻夸克真空极化函数进行强子修正。

Result: 在11 GeV束流能量下，电子弱荷为Q_W^e = 0.04546(8)（基于e+e−数据）或0.04517(5)（基于格点QCD），两圈电弱修正贡献约为总值的1.5%；未计算高阶项估计贡献约4%实验精度目标，而e+e−与格点QCD输入导致的差异达目标精度的25%。

Conclusion: 当前理论预测受限于强子真空极化输入的不一致性，而非高阶微扰计算缺失；为实现MOLLER实验的完整物理目标，亟需提升格点QCD对Δα_had^(5)的精度或协调e+e−与格点结果。

Abstract: The MOLLER experiment will measure the parity-violating asymmetry of Møller scattering to 2.4%. We present its fermionic and bosonic two-loop electroweak corrections to the zeroth and first orders in $1-4\sin^2\theta_W$. Light quarks enter through vacuum polarization from $e^+e^-$ data or lattice QCD. For $\sin^2\hat\theta_W(M_Z) = 0.23122$ and an 11 GeV beam, the weak charge of the electron is $0.04546(8)$ (data) or 0.04517(5) (lattice). Two-loop terms contribute 1.5% of it. Uncomputed terms are estimated at 4% of the experimental goal; the data-lattice difference, about 25% of that goal, limits the prediction.

</details>


### [205] [Two-loop QCD contribution to the quark-initiated production of a top-antitop quark pair and a Higgs boson at the LHC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40266)
*Xiang Chen,Massimiliano Grazzini,Stefan Kallweit*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次直接计算了LHC上顶夸克对伴随希格斯玻色子（$t\bar{t}H$）产生过程的两圈QCD虚修正，聚焦于夸克-反夸克初态，并在广义领色近似下完成；结合先进多圈数值方法与机器学习构建高精度代理振幅，并验证其与动力学近似结果在误差范围内一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确测定$t\bar{t}H$耦合对检验希格斯机制及新物理至关重要，而两圈QCD虚修正构成当前理论预测的主要不确定性来源之一，亟需高精度、全色精确（或可控近似）的计算。

Method: 采用广义领色近似（generalised leading-colour approximation）处理色结构；结合数值积分（如Sector Decomposition或FIESTA类工具）、多回路张量约化（如Reduze或Kira）与机器学习（如神经网络拟合）构建代理振幅（surrogate amplitude），实现高效且亚百分比精度的相空间积分。

Result: 获得$t\bar{t}H$在$q\bar{q}$通道中完整两圈QCD虚修正的数值结果；代理振幅在典型相空间点达到~0.1%相对精度；与先前基于动力学近似（dynamical approximations）的结果在各自不确定度内一致，验证了近似方案的可靠性。

Conclusion: 该工作为$t\bar{t}H$过程提供了迄今最严格的两圈QCD虚修正基准，证实了机器学习辅助多圈计算的可行性与稳健性，为未来NNLO QCD+EW联合精度分析及ATLAS/CMS实验解释奠定关键理论基础。

Abstract: We present the first direct computation of the two-loop QCD virtual corrections to the production of a top-antitop quark pair in association with a Higgs boson ($t\bar{t}H$) at the LHC. We focus on the quark-antiquark channel and perform the calculation in the generalised leading-colour approximation. We combine state-of-the-art numerical methods for multiloop calculations with machine learning techniques to construct a surrogate amplitude suitable for accurate cross section computations. We compare our numerical results with those previously obtained using dynamical approximations of the two-loop amplitude, finding agreement within their respective uncertainties.

</details>


### [206] [Probabilistic Energy-Based Neural Networks for Uncertainty-Aware Collider Data Analyses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40268)
*Raymundo Ramos,Ahmed Hammad,Amit Chakraborty,Pyungwon Ko,Stefano Moretti*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于概率能量的神经网络（EBM-NN），用于对撞机数据分析中的不确定性感知分类，通过类特定嵌入定义潜空间中的能量分数，以识别异常、噪声或分布偏移事件，并在h→τ⁺τ⁻过程的LHC分析中验证其对系统误差和建模偏差的敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准判别式分类器在面对分布外（OOD）事件时可能给出过度自信的预测，而对撞机实验中普遍存在系统不确定性、建模偏差与数据分布漂移问题，亟需具备不确定性量化能力的鲁棒分类器。

Method: 构建能量基模型（EBM），其中神经网络将输入特征映射至潜空间，能量函数定义为输入到类特定可学习嵌入的距离（如负相似度或平方距离）；训练目标为联合优化分类损失与能量间隔损失（如对比损失或NCE），使同类事件能量低、异类/异常事件能量高。

Result: 在合成数据上验证了模型对OOD与噪声事件的检测能力；在LHC h→τ⁺τ⁻物理分析中，能量分数显著区分受理论建模误差（如tau强子化模拟）、实验系统误差（如能量尺度偏差）影响的事件，可据此构造不确定性感知的事件选择（uncertainty-aware event selection）。

Conclusion: 该EBM-NN框架为高能物理分类任务提供了内生的不确定性度量机制，无需额外校准即可实现对系统与分布偏移的鲁棒性，在保持高分类精度的同时支持可信的异常探测与风险控制。

Abstract: We introduce a probabilistic energy-based Neural Network (NN) for uncertainty-aware classification in collider analyses. Standard discriminative classifiers can give highly confident predictions even for events that are different from the data used during training. Energy-Based Models provide an alternative by assigning an energy score to each event, which measures how well the event agrees with the learned data distribution. In this framework, a NN maps the input features into a latent space, where the energy is defined with respect to class-specific embeddings. This allows the model to identify noisy, anomalous or shifted events while maintaining good classification performance. We first study the method using controlled synthetic examples and then test it in a realistic collider analysis based on the $h \to \tau^+ \tau^-$ process at the Large Hadron Collider, where $h$ is the discovered Standard Model Higgs boson. We show that the associated energy score can help identify events affected by mismodelling and/or systematic variations and can thus be used to define uncertainty-aware event selections. Although we use this specific Higgs process as a testbed, the method is general and can be applied to other collider classification problems where robustness against systematic and distributional uncertainties is important.

</details>


### [207] [Two-peak gravitational wave spectra from supercooled two-field phase transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40354)
*Daniel Araújo,João Gonçalves,Danny Marfatia*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了双场共形模型中过冷一级相变产生的引力波谱，构建了三个典型玩具模型，推导了两步相变存在的解析判据，并揭示了双场动力学如何产生真实的双峰引力波谱；进一步划定了LISA与爱因斯坦望远镜可观测该双峰谱的参数空间，并以一个现实的共形暗SU(3)模型为例进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解宇宙早期一级相变产生的引力波信号是探测新物理的重要途径；双场共形模型可自然实现超冷相变与两步动力学，但其引力波谱特征（尤其是多峰结构）尚缺乏系统分析。

Method: 构造三类具有共形对称性的双标量场玩具模型；通过有效势分析与相变动力学（包括临界温度、成核率、壁速、声速等）计算引力波谱；利用数值与解析方法导出两步相变发生的条件；结合LISA与ET的灵敏度曲线进行可观测性分析。

Result: 发现双场动力学可导致真正的双峰引力波谱（非叠加假象），其两峰分别对应两个不同尺度的相变事件；确定了在LISA（mHz频段）与ET（10 Hz频段）均具可观测性的参数区域；在共形暗SU(3)模型中证实该现象可自然出现。

Conclusion: 双场共形模型为解释未来空间与地面引力波探测器中可能观测到的多峰谱提供了自洽且可检验的理论框架，凸显了共形对称性与多标量自由度在早期宇宙相变中的关键作用。

Abstract: We study the gravitational wave (GW) spectrum generated by supercooled first-order phase transitions in two-field conformal models. We construct three representative toy models that capture the essential features of these models, derive analytical criteria for the existence of two-step transitions, and show how the two-field dynamics generate a genuine two-peak GW spectrum. We identify regions of parameter space in which two-peak GW spectra are observable at LISA and the Einstein Telescope. By way of illustration, we apply our analysis to a realistic conformal dark SU(3) model.

</details>


### [208] [Hadronic resonances from the 4PI effective action of QCD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40390)
*Jonas Wessely,Markus Q. Huber*

Main category: hep-ph
